# Progress Tracker — Form-Builder SaaS

**This file is the single source of truth for "where are we right now."** It must be kept up to date and must be updated with a clear handoff note any time work is interrupted, paused, or a session ends before a milestone is finished.

## Standing Rules for This Project

1. **Always maintain this progress tracker.** Update "Current Phase" / "Status Summary" as work completes, and always write a dated entry in "Notes / Handoff" before stopping work for the session — even (especially) if the task was interrupted mid-way. Never leave the repo in a state where the next person/session has to reverse-engineer what was in progress.
2. **One standard design system, no exceptions.** Every page (builder, dashboard, submissions inbox, settings, public form runtime) uses the same shared app shell, component library, and design tokens (styles, buttons, forms, lists, inputs, etc.) — see Main Feature #6 in the plan and the future UI/UX Design System Reference (doc #19). No page invents its own one-off layout or component styling. Any exception requires an explicit, documented rationale, not a default under time pressure.
3. **Styling default — don't re-ask (confirmed with the user 2026-07-05).** Do not ask "backend-only / minimal-unstyled vs. fully styled?" per increment. The default is **every page is built fully styled, assembled from the shared design-system components**. The only increments with no styling work are those with **no pages at all** (a pure data-model / API / backend step). The bare pages parked in B1/B2b/B2c (login, register, password-reset, invite-accept, super-admin console, 2FA setup) are brought up to standard in Increment C, which builds out the shared component library itself (only `Button` exists in code today; the color/border/radius/type tokens are complete). Earlier increments were unstyled only because the component library didn't exist yet and styling then would have been rework — not because styling was ever optional.
4. **End every session with a save + a next-prompt guide (confirmed with the user 2026-07-10).** When a session is completed (or told to stop/pause), before signing off: (a) **save state to all relevant MD files** — this tracker (Status Summary + "Next Session — Resume Here" + a new dated "Notes / Handoff" entry), the auto-memory (`MEMORY.md` snapshot + any affected memory file), and any `docs/**` / ADR / plan file actually changed this session; touch only what changed. (b) **Always hand back an explicit, copy-paste "what to prompt next" guide.** For phased/increment work that means the exact next step (e.g. ``Read PROGRESS.md and build Increment F5``) with a one-line note of what it does plus any alternative tracks, and the same prompts mirrored into the "Prompts to resume next session" list below so the file and the chat never disagree.

  - **D4a — builder CRUD + config panels + undo/redo state** ✅ **DONE — MERGED to `main` (PR #13, `250911a`; all 6 CI jobs green on the first run, incl. the live E2E Playwright+axe on the builder page).** The three-pane interactive builder (`resources/js/Pages/forms/Builder.vue` + `resources/js/components/builder/*`): field-type **palette** (all 31 `FieldType`s grouped by category from the enum; advanced types flagged), **canvas** (ordered sections + fields, selection, explicit Move-up/down, duplicate, delete — pointer drag is D4b), and a **tabbed config panel** (Basics/Options/Validation/Advanced with real `role=tablist/tab/tabpanel` + arrow-key roving — NOT the radiogroup `MdsSegmentedControl`; `RequiredMode` tri-state IS the segmented control). **Undo/redo is the state core** (`useBuilderStore.ts`): every add/delete/duplicate/move/config-edit is a reversible command on an undo+redo stack, fields/sections keyed by a stable client `uid` decoupled from the server id (so delete→undo survives a new-id recreate); edits are optimistically persisted through a serialized queue. **CSRF fetch seam** (`builderClient.ts`): reads the `XSRF-TOKEN` cookie → `X-XSRF-TOKEN`, same-origin, `Accept: application/json` (the only write-`fetch` in the app — Inertia can't be used or it would blow away builder state on each edit). **409 optimistic-concurrency**: content PATCH carries the row's microsecond `updated_at` token → drift returns 409 + the fresh row → `ConflictDialog` preserves the in-flight value (merge/overwrite). Backend: thin `FormBuilderController` (9 JSON methods, exceptions→status in one helper) → `FormBuilderService` (draft-only writes via `assertDraftChild` + the `forms`-row lock on structural ops; reorder writes via query-builder so it does NOT bump `updated_at` and can't spurious-409 the next PATCH) → `BuilderPresenter` (draft read model + palette + enum catalogs); `StoreFieldRequest`/`UpdateFieldRequest`/`UpdateSectionRequest` (key format + per-version uniqueness + the validation expression-XOR-rule_type mirror). **Built `MdsSelect` + `MdsNumberInput`** (deferred from D3; axe stories). `AppLayout` gained a full-bleed **fluid** variant (detected off the page component; single persistent instance kept). **Foundational fix:** `bootstrap/app.php` `shouldRenderJsonWhen` now also fires on `$request->expectsJson()` — the builder's JSON endpoints need 422 validation bodies, not the HTML redirect Inertia visits get (Inertia requests don't `expectsJson()`, so their flow is unaffected). **Verified locally: 125/125 Pest (real Postgres; +9 — `BuilderRoutesTest` permission matrix + CRUD + reorder + 409 + key-uniqueness, `BuilderDraftGuardTest` published-version rejection through HTTP + RLS backstop), `composer run quality` (Pint + Larastan L8 + controller-gate + migration-lint), `vue-tsc`, `npm run build`, `npm run ds:test` (108 axe stories, 0 violations). Playwright builder page added to `responsive-axe.spec.ts` (navigates via the list link; auto-selects a field so the config panel mounts) — the live e2e runs on CI.** Pointer drag + the interaction-driven `builder-axe.spec.ts` are **D4b**.
  - **D4b — drag-and-drop reordering + a11y** ✅ **DONE — MERGED to `main` (PR #14, `3c86e93`; all 6 CI jobs green first run, incl. the merge-blocking interaction-driven `builder-axe` at all three viewports light+dark).** Replaced D4a's Move-up/down buttons with a **left drag grip** per field/section that carries BOTH reorder paths (no third-party DnD library — those inject aria-hidden mirror DOM + steal focus and fail the axe gate): **hand-rolled pointer drag** (`useCanvasReorder.ts` — pointerdown captures, each move `document.elementFromPoint` hit-tests the `data-drop-group` container + row midpoints and live-reorders across sections incl. into empty groups; pointerup commits, Escape cancels; `touch-action:none` on the grip so touch-drag doesn't scroll) and a **keyboard grab-mode** (Enter/Space grabs → Arrow keys walk the field up/down **across section boundaries** through the flattened visual order → Enter drops, Escape cancels; focus never leaves the grip, so no vanishing-control problem). Every state change is announced through an **assertive `aria-live` region** on the canvas. A whole drag/grab is ONE store **reorder session** (`beginReorder`→`commitReorder` = one persist + one undo entry; `cancelReorder` restores) built on new local primitives (`flattenedFields`/`placeField`/`stepFieldAcross`/`placeSection`/`stepSection`). Section reparent-to-empty-section is still available via the config Section select (D4a). **`builder-axe.spec.ts`** (interaction-driven, the merge-blocking gate this increment exists for): drives the config panel + every tab (mounting MdsSelect/MdsTextarea/MdsNumberInput/MdsCheckbox/MdsSegmentedControl), selects the seeded repeatable section, and executes a **full keyboard reorder (grab→arrow→drop) asserting the aria-live announcements**, plus scans the **empty-canvas** state — all at 375/834/1440px in light+dark. *Verification loop (now fully set up):* **Docker Desktop + WSL2 is installed and the Compose stack runs locally**. The proven loop for each increment: `docker compose up -d` → `docker compose exec app php artisan migrate:fresh --force` → `... php artisan test` (real Postgres as `meridian_app`; auth tests use the `pgsql_auth`/`pgsql_privileged` roles too) → `composer run quality` + `npm run type-check` on the host → push branch → `gh pr create` → CI green on Linux Postgres → `gh pr merge --squash`. The **`gh` CLI (2.96.0) is installed and authenticated** (via the stored git credential / `GH_TOKEN`). Note: local Docker test runs are ~3× slower than CI (Windows volume I/O under WSL2), not a code issue. If the `pgdata` volume is ever reset, the init SQL recreates `meridian_app` + `meridian_auth`; otherwise the guarded role migrations create them.

Persisted shapes: matrix `{row:{col:cell}}`, likert_matrix `{row:score}` (both object-valued). **User decisions (AskUserQuestion): a matrix cell is a single-select from a shared `config.cells` pool; a required grid demands EVERY row be answered** (per-row/per-cell `field_required`). **The load-bearing correctness point:** `Coercion.isEmpty` diverges on an empty object (PHP `isEmpty([])===true` vs TS `isEmpty({})===false`), so composites are NEVER routed through the scalar `collectFieldErrors`/`isEmpty` path — a new **doubly-mirrored `processComposites` pass** (PHP `SemanticValidator` + `semantic-validator.ts`) reads the field's declared rows/columns/cells, prunes the object to known cells IN CONFIG ORDER (dropping empties, coercing each scalar cell), and applies required-completeness + cell-membership; object-emptiness is decided by counting surviving cells, never `isEmpty(object)`. `EngineValue` stays frozen (only the answers-map unions widen, exactly like G1's `InstanceAnswers[]`); `SemanticError.cellPath` reused for `field.<row>` (likert) / `field.<row>.<col>` (matrix) — no signature change. Stage-1 object coercion (`expected_object`/`unknown_row`/`unknown_column`, PHP-only). Publish gates: `StructuralValidationGate` (rows/columns/cells non-empty+distinct; a grid may NOT sit in a repeatable section) + a NEW type-aware `ExpressionValidationGate` rule forbidding any expression from referencing a grid field (via a new `ExpressionParser::referencedKeys()`), both via new `PublishValidationException` factories. Renderers: a new `MatrixGrid.vue` (fieldset-per-row + flex-wrap → reflows for free at 375px, no `<table>` overflow; native radios/selects) shared by encode + SPA + repeat instances via `FieldInput.vue` (`AnswerValue` widened to object shapes); builder `MatrixEditor.vue`/`LikertMatrixEditor.vue` (+ shared `GridOptionList.vue`) wired via `FieldType::configEditor()`. Golden `matrix.json`(8) + `likert_matrix.json`(8) via BOTH engines (manifest 72→88; golden-validation 73→89 byte-identical). - **Known-flaky test to clean up (surfaced during G2 CI, unrelated to G2):** `tests/Feature/Guest/GuestRuntimeTest › it 401s a tampered token` flips only the share token's LAST base64 char, which for some random tokens aliases to the same signature bytes (base64 unused low bits) → the tamper isn't detected → 200 not 401. Fix by mutating a guaranteed-significant byte (or asserting over several tampers).
- (phpstan environment note, now fully explained: `composer run quality`'s phpstan step OOMs its parallel workers at 128M in the local Docker container, corrupting the result cache into phantom errors — clear the cache + re-run single-threaded with `--debug --memory-limit=2G` for the true count. **Larastan has `disableMigrationScan: false`, so it builds the schema by scanning `database/migrations` — on CI (clean env, adequate memory) that scan resolves every model column + FK-nullability → 0 errors → green; the ~78 local "undefined property"/relation-nullability errors are that scan failing under the local OOM, NOT real.** For code that traverses a possibly-null relation chain, prefer `data_get($model, 'rel.attr')` (returns `mixed`, immune to the local flip-flop) over `?->`/direct access.)

- [x] **Phase 0 Increment A — Tenancy + RLS (2026-07-05, branch `increment-a-tenancy-rls`; verified green on real PostgreSQL via Docker Compose — 21/21 suite, fail-closed + FORCE proven red-then-green).** The crown-jewel increment: the tenant-isolation machinery every later increment sits on. Built:
  - **`meridian_app` role (the linchpin)**: a **non-superuser, NOBYPASSRLS** role that **owns** the tables and is the app's runtime connection. RLS is ignored for superusers, and a table owner bypasses RLS unless `FORCE` is set — so making `meridian_app` the owner means the fuzz pack proving "A can't see B" *is* the proof FORCE is active. Created in `docker/postgres/init/01-create-testing-db.sql` (local) and a CI provisioning step; `.env.example`/`config/database.php` default `pgsql` → `meridian_app`, plus a `pgsql_privileged` (superuser) connection for seeding platform-global rows.
  - **`app/Support/Tenancy/TenantIsolation.php`** — RLS policy generator, SQL-generation split from execution. Emits all **four policy shapes**: strict-equality + nullable-global (both fuzz-tested now), and `users` join-shape + belongs-to-me + draft-guard (emitted + unit-tested; behaviorally fuzzed when their tables land in B/D). Encodes the non-negotiables once: `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` and the fail-closed two-arg `current_setting(...,true)` form.
  - **`withTenantIsolation()`** global helper (autoloaded via composer `files`) — the one call every tenant-scoped migration ends with; **`app/Support/Tenancy/TenantContext.php`** (set_config, transaction-scoped `applyLocal` for tests/jobs + session-scoped `apply`/`forget` for HTTP; clears to SQL NULL, never `''`, to stay fail-closed); **`BelongsToTenant`**/**`TenantScoped`**/**`HasUuidv7`** concerns; **`EstablishTenantDatabaseContext`** middleware (registered as the `tenant.context` alias — attached to routes in B; sets both `app.current_tenant_id` and `app.current_user_id`).
  - **`scripts/migration-lint.php`** (new CI gate, `composer run migration-lint`) — fails any migration that declares a `tenant_id` column without calling `withTenantIsolation()`. **Proven red-then-green locally.**
  - **Harness tables** (throwaway, like `SkeletonProbe`): `tenants` (RLS-exempt central table), `probes` (strict), `global_probes` (nullable-global) + models. **`tests/Feature/Tenancy/CrossTenantIsolationTest.php`** (the fuzz pack: SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE isolation, fail-closed, FORCE metadata, ORM-scope-agrees-with-RLS, nullable-global read + write-refusal, non-superuser guard) and **`tests/Unit/TenantIsolationSqlTest.php`** (8 pure SQL-shape tests, green locally).
  - **CI**: `static-analysis` gains migration-lint; `tests` provisions `meridian_app` + a `meridian_testing` DB it owns and connects as it.
  - **Verified green on real PostgreSQL (Docker Compose, 2026-07-05)**: `docker compose up` → `meridian_app` confirmed non-superuser/non-BYPASSRLS and owner of both DBs → `artisan migrate` as `meridian_app` (probes/global_probes show `relrowsecurity`+`relforcerowsecurity`, 8 correct policies) → **`php artisan test` = 21/21** (the 10-test fuzz pack included). **FORCE proven red-then-green** (commenting out the FORCE line → 7 fuzz tests fail as the owner bypasses RLS → restore → green); the migration linter was likewise proven red-then-green. Also green on the host: `composer run quality` + the SQL unit suite.
  - **Bug caught by the live run + fixed**: a cleared custom GUC reads back as `''` (empty string), not `NULL`, and `''::uuid` errors — turning fail-closed into a hard failure. Fixed by wrapping every policy read in `NULLIF(current_setting(...), '')::uuid` so both "never set" and "set-then-cleared" map to NULL ⇒ zero rows. (This is exactly the kind of runtime-only defect the Postgres run exists to catch; the static/unit gates could not have.)
  - **Deviation from the approved plan — stancl/tenancy deferred to B**: the plan assumed "v4," which **does not exist** (latest stable is v3.10.0). Since stancl only provides tenant *identification* (subdomains/auth), which isn't exercised until B and can't be verified without a running app, and A's DoD is fully met by the machinery above with our own `Tenant` model, the dependency is deferred to B (where it's wired end-to-end). Nothing built in A depends on it.

- [x] **Phase 0 Increment B1 — Auth foundation + identity + user-level RLS (2026-07-05, merged to `main` via PR #2 `e4253fa`; verified 31/31 on real Postgres locally + CI).** The auth layer + real identity tables + the user-level RLS shapes A left emitted. Built:
  - **Packages**: Fortify 1.36 (session auth) + Sanctum 4.3 (tokens) + **stancl/tenancy 3.10** (single-DB). stancl configured `bootstrappers => []` (RLS is the isolation, not per-tenant DB swapping); the `TenantCreated → CreateDatabase` job pipeline pruned from `App\Providers\TenancyServiceProvider`. `App\Models\Tenant` extends stancl's base with a **UUIDv7 id generator** (`app/Support/Tenancy/Uuid7Generator.php`) + explicit `getCustomColumns()`.
  - **The crux — RLS-on-`users` vs. login bootstrapping**: the join-shape `users` SELECT policy fails closed with no user/tenant context (exactly the state during login/registration/password-reset). Solved with a dedicated **non-superuser `meridian_auth` role** + **`pgsql_auth` connection** + **`app/Auth/RlsAwareUserProvider.php`** — resolves users on the pre-auth connection (permissive `TO meridian_auth` SELECT, `users`-only), resets the authenticated model back to `meridian_app` so no elevated connection leaks into request code, and keeps the credentials lookup on `pgsql_auth` so the unauthenticated password-reset save works. `meridian_auth` created via a privileged, guarded migration + `docker/postgres/init`.
  - **Identity/schema**: `users` → UUIDv7 PK + Fortify 2FA cols + `is_super_admin` + ToS/privacy consent + soft-deletes (`sessions.user_id` → uuid); new `tenant_users`, `user_ui_preferences`, `personal_access_tokens(+tenant_id)`; `tenants` gains `owner_user_id`/`status`/stancl `data`; the stancl `domains` migration re-dated (uuid `tenant_id`) and added to the migration linter's `EXEMPT_TABLES` (it's the pre-context tenant lookup).
  - **RLS**: `TenantIsolation::usersVisibility()` (SELECT via a `tenant_users` active-membership join) + `usersWritePolicies()`. **Design decision — user WRITE policies are permissive** (`USING/WITH CHECK (true)`), per **RBAC §6** ("user writes are a user's own account, governed by application-layer authorization, not tenant RLS"): the SELECT visibility policy enforces read isolation, and permissive writes let central account-management (2FA/profile/password, no tenant context) update the user's own row. `user_ui_preferences` uses belongs-to-me (keyed on `app.current_user_id`); `personal_access_tokens` strict.
  - **Hardening/UX**: breached-password check (`Password::uncompromised()`, ASVS L1) via `Password::defaults()`; email-uniqueness resolves on `pgsql_auth` (`CreateNewUser`/`UpdateUserProfileInformation`); minimal **unstyled** Inertia auth pages under `resources/js/Pages/auth/` + a `Dashboard` placeholder (design-system-styled versions land in Increment C). `routes/tenant.php` = the authenticated subdomain group (`InitializeTenancyBySubdomain` → `PreventAccessFromCentralDomains` → `EstablishTenantDatabaseContext` → `auth`).
  - **Tests (31/31 on real Postgres)**: `tests/Feature/Tenancy/UserRlsTest.php` (fail-closed / active-membership-join / belongs-to-me / PAT-strict), `tests/Feature/Auth/AuthenticationTest.php` (login end-to-end, wrong-password, registration + breached-password), `TenantIsolationSqlTest` additions. **Test-harness pattern documented in `docs/testing-strategy.md`**: auth tests that log a user in must seed a COMMITTED user on `pgsql_privileged` (RefreshDatabase's transaction is invisible to the separate `pgsql_auth` session). Static gates + `vue-tsc` green.
  - **CI**: `tests` job env gains `DB_AUTH_*`; the provision step creates `meridian_auth`.
  - **Deferred within B / to later**: Spatie RBAC (roles/permissions/5-role catalog/matrix/pivots-RLS/`tenant_users` lifecycle/`setPermissionsTeamId`/super-admin) → **B2**; `form_collaborators` + `.own` FormPolicy → **D** (needs `forms`); polished auth UI + the full 2FA enrolment-flow test → **C** (the 2FA backend + columns + Fortify feature are wired here).

- [x] **Phase 0 Increment B2a — Core Spatie RBAC foundation (2026-07-05, merged to `main` via PR #3 `4c094ad`; verified 39/39 on real Postgres locally + CI).** Spatie Laravel-Permission in tenant-scoped teams mode — a user's role resolves fail-closed against the active tenant, the fixed catalog is seeded, the pivots are RLS-isolated and fuzz-proven. Built:
  - **Package/config**: `spatie/laravel-permission ^8.3` (Laravel 13 compatible; the docs' assumed "v4" of stancl is unrelated). `config/permission.php`: `teams=true`, **`team_foreign_key='tenant_id'`** (keeps the schema-wide discriminator name + leading-index column, ADR-0002 §D1); models pointed at **`app/Models/Role.php`** + **`Permission.php`** — Spatie subclasses using the existing **`HasUuidv7`** concern (roles are externally addressable via GET /api/v1/roles; no sequential-ID enumeration).
  - **Schema + RLS** (`2026_07_05_032540_create_permission_tables.php`, a UUID-PK rewrite of Spatie's stub with **literal `tenant_id`**): `roles`/`permissions` → **nullable-global** (`TenantIsolation::nullableGlobal`; the global catalog is readable by every tenant, writable by none but the privileged seeder); `model_has_roles`/`model_has_permissions` → **strict**; `role_has_permissions` → un-isolated (no tenant dimension, ADR-0002 §D1 exempt). Note: Spatie's teams-mode stub adds the team column to `roles`/`model_has_roles`/`model_has_permissions` only — I also gave `permissions` a nullable `tenant_id` to match RBAC §4/§12 and the `TenantIsolation` class's own adopter list. **Verified**: RLS enabled+forced on the four tables (4 policies each); catalog = 5 roles / 27 permissions / 63 grants (owner 23, admin 21, form_editor 9, reviewer 6, viewer 4).
  - **`invited_role_id → roles` FK** (`2026_07_05_032600_...`): the constraint B1 deferred (roles didn't exist yet).
  - **Migration linter** (`scripts/migration-lint.php`): **new Spatie-pivot rule** — any migration touching the permission tables must attach RLS to `roles`/`model_has_roles`/`model_has_permissions` **by literal name**. Closes RBAC §4's flagged gap: Spatie's stub names both the table AND the `tenant_id` column via config variables, so the generic literal-`tenant_id` check is blind to it (the pivots would silently ship with no isolation). **Proven red-then-green** (comment out one `TenantIsolation::strict` call → the Spatie rule fails while the generic rule still passes → restore → green).
  - **Runtime**: `HasRoles` on `App\Models\User`; **`setPermissionsTeamId($tenant)` added to `EstablishTenantDatabaseContext::handle()`** (reset in `terminate()`) — RBAC authorizes against the same tenant RLS isolates by; two concerns, one middleware trigger. The `// added in B2` anchors in the middleware + `routes/tenant.php` are resolved.
  - **Seeder** (`database/seeders/RolePermissionSeeder.php`): the fixed 5-role / 27-permission / 63-grant matrix (RBAC §3/§5) as **global rows via `pgsql_privileged`** (the strict write policy forbids `meridian_app` from authoring NULL-tenant rows — same platform-global seeding pattern as A). Idempotent (matched on name); catalog exposed as public consts (`ROLES`/`PERMISSIONS`/`MATRIX`) so tests assert against one source of truth. Wired into `DatabaseSeeder`.
  - **Tests (39/39 on real Postgres)**: `tests/Feature/Tenancy/RbacRlsTest.php` (DB-level fuzz — model_has_roles strict isolation, global-catalog readable-by-all/writable-by-none, RLS enabled+forced), `tests/Feature/Rbac/RolePermissionTest.php` (Spatie resolution against the active team, the **fail-closed hazard** — RLS context set but permissions team forgotten ⇒ every check false — and per-tenant resolution for a multi-tenant user), `TenantIsolationSqlTest` additions. **Test-harness note**: the global catalog is seeded on the privileged connection (committed, outside RefreshDatabase's transaction) and **deliberately not cleaned up** — deleting a parent `roles` row from the privileged connection deadlocks against the uncommitted `model_has_roles` child rows a test leaves in its own not-yet-rolled-back `meridian_app` transaction; idempotent re-seeding + migrate:fresh handle it instead.
  - **CI**: no workflow change needed — the `tests` job already provisions `meridian_app`/`meridian_auth` and a `meridian` (superuser) `pgsql_privileged` connection (from B1). All 6 jobs green on Linux Postgres.
  - **Deferred to B2b / later**: `tenant_users` invite/accept/decline/remove/ownership-transfer service layer (RBAC §7) + super-admin service layer + mandatory-MFA + the 3 open super-admin questions (RBAC §9) → **B2b**; `form_collaborators` + `.own` FormPolicy → **D** (needs `forms`); styled role-management / invite / 2FA-enrolment UI → **C**.

- [x] **Phase 0 Increment B2b — Tenant membership lifecycle (2026-07-05, merged to `main` via PR #4 `d7a8d01`; verified 51/51 on real Postgres locally + CI).** The invite→accept/decline + remove + ownership-transfer lifecycle on top of B2a (RBAC §7). Scoped (AskUserQuestion) to **membership lifecycle only** (super-admin + mandatory-MFA → B2c) and **backend + minimal unstyled**. Built:
  - **`App\Services\Tenancy\TenantMembershipService`** (the whole lifecycle, RLS + Spatie-team-aware):
    - **invite** — reserve a role on a `tenant_users` row + a hashed, opaque token (7-day expiry); create a placeholder user or reuse the existing global identity (resolved on **`pgsql_auth`** — the join-shape `users` RLS hides a non-member from the inviting Admin, same cross-RLS lookup `CreateNewUser` uses); reactivate a prior declined/removed row rather than duplicate it (writes wrapped in a txn, notify after commit). **Owner is never invitable.**
    - **accept** — activate the membership and **only then** materialize the reserved role into a real `model_has_roles` row (§7: an unaccepted invite grants nothing).
    - **decline** — record declined, grant nothing.
    - **remove** — one txn: mark removed + detach the tenant-scoped role (`syncRoles([])`) + revoke the user's tenant-scoped Sanctum tokens. The **Owner can't be removed** (transfer first).
    - **transferOwnership** — one txn: repoint `tenants.owner_user_id` (central, RLS-exempt), promote the incoming member to Owner, demote the outgoing Owner to Admin (never left roleless; the outgoing owner is an active member ⇒ resolvable on the default connection, not `pgsql_auth`). Audit emission is a `// TODO(audits, Phase 1)` — no `audits` table yet.
  - **HTTP** (thin controllers → service; controller-gate green at 4 files): **`MemberController`** (invite/remove/ownership) on the authenticated subdomain group, authorization via the Spatie **`can:`** middleware per route; **`InvitationController`** (show/accept/decline) on a **NO-AUTH** subdomain group that still establishes tenant context (the invitee isn't a member yet — which is exactly what makes the strict-RLS invite row visible and lets accept materialize the role). Minimal unstyled `resources/js/Pages/invitations/Show.vue`. **`MembershipException`** → redirect-back-with-errors handler in `bootstrap/app.php`. Accept uses `Password::default()` (B1's breached-password check) WITHOUT `confirmed` (single-field minimal page).
  - **Tests (51/51)**: `MembershipLifecycleTest` (9 — the full state machine, business rules, atomic side effects, cross-tenant invite isolation, through real RLS + Spatie teams) + `MembershipRoutesTest` (3 — the `can:` gate 403, a gated Admin invite, and accept end-to-end through the no-auth subdomain pipeline). Shared helpers (`enterTenant`/`catalogRole`/`makeActiveMember`) hoisted to **`tests/Pest.php`** (Pest loads all test files into one process — a duplicate top-level function fatals).
  - **Two CI/test gotchas fixed**: (1) the accept HTTP test **deadlocked** when `afterEach` deleted the committed invitee on the privileged connection while the test's own uncommitted transaction held a lock on it (the B2a lesson again) → don't clean it; (2) the GET show test 500'd on **CI only** with `ViteManifestNotFoundException` (the tests job doesn't `npm build`) → `$this->withoutVite()`, mirroring the B1 SmokeTest. Larastan needed a `@property TenantUserStatus $status` on `TenantUser` (the `casts()` `@return array<string,string>` had erased the enum type, making the status comparisons read as always-true dead code).
  - **Deferred**: super-admin service + mandatory MFA + the 3 open questions → **B2c**; `form_collaborators` + `.own` FormPolicy → **D**; styled member/invite/2FA UI → **C**.

- [x] **Phase 0 Increment B2c — Super-admin service layer + mandatory MFA (2026-07-05, merged to `main` via PR #5 `a5351cd`; verified 67/67 on real Postgres locally + CI).** The elevated-role platform-operations mechanism (RBAC §9 / ADR-0002 §D3 / security §8), the last auth/RBAC piece before the app shell. Scoped (AskUserQuestion) to **backend + minimal unstyled**, and the **3 RBAC §9 open questions resolved with the user**. Built:
  - **Elevated role**: a dedicated **non-superuser / NOBYPASSRLS `meridian_superadmin`** login role (`2026_07_05_040000_create_meridian_superadmin_role`, guarded, on `pgsql_privileged`) + a `pgsql_superadmin` connection — mirroring B1's `meridian_auth`. Deliberately NOT the superuser seeding role: RLS still applies to it, so the carve-out is defense-in-depth, not a wholesale bypass. Provisioned in all four places `meridian_auth` is (migration + `config/database.php` + `docker/postgres/init` + `ci.yml` env/provision + `.env`/`.env.example`).
  - **`TenantIsolation::superAdminBypass`** — a new **additive, role-scoped, GUC-gated** permissive policy shape: `TO meridian_superadmin USING (current_setting('app.is_superadmin_context', true) = 'true')`, layered onto a table that already has its base RLS+FORCE (it deliberately emits **no** ENABLE/FORCE). Applied to `users` (`2026_07_05_040100_apply_superadmin_bypass_to_users`: `GRANT SELECT ON users` + the policy). The gate is a plain text equality (no `NULLIF(...)::uuid`) so it is inherently fail-closed with no cast hazard. **Real-Postgres bug caught & fixed**: the `users` visibility policy's `EXISTS(… FROM tenant_users …)` subquery is evaluated for every role reading `users`, so the elevated role also needs `GRANT SELECT ON tenant_users` (table privileges are checked before RLS) — added, with its own rows still RLS-filtered to zero (no bypass there; super-admin user visibility comes purely from the gated carve-out).
  - **`SuperAdminContext`** (mirrors `TenantContext`) — sets `app.is_superadmin_context` **transaction-locally** (`is_local=true`) on the elevated connection; the leak-proof primary API. **`App\Services\Admin\SuperAdminService`** — the one narrow named service: `listTenants`/`suspendTenant`/`reactivateTenant` (on `tenants`, the RLS-exempt central table, ordinary connection — deliberately NOT elevated) + `listAllUsers` (the flagship cross-tenant read, the only place `elevated()` opens the context). `TenantStatus` enum + `SuperAdminException` (rendered in `bootstrap/app.php`, mirroring `MembershipException`).
  - **Mandatory MFA + gate**: `EnsureSuperAdmin` (404 non-disclosure for non-staff) + `EnsureSuperAdminMfa` (redirect to enrollment unless `two_factor_confirmed_at` is set) middleware, on a **central-domain `/admin`** console (`routes/admin.php`, loaded via `bootstrap/app.php` `withRouting(then:)`, domain-constrained via a new `tenancy.central_domain` config key so it survives route caching). Thin `TenantAdminController` + three unstyled Inertia pages (`admin/{Tenants,Users,TwoFactorSetup}.vue`); the enrollment page sits OUTSIDE `superadmin.mfa` to avoid a redirect loop and drives Fortify's own global 2FA endpoints. **Console-gated, not login-gated** (no Fortify-pipeline surgery; login-time enforcement is a Phase-1+ note).
  - **RBAC §9 questions resolved (with the user)**: **impersonation → deferred** (needs the Phase-1 `audits` table + `acting_as_user_id`); **audit visibility → transparency** (a tenant's Audit Log will surface super-admin actions; GDPR-processor aligned — recorded as the Phase-1 principle); **staff tiers → binary** (`is_super_admin` stays one boolean). Recorded in `docs/multi-tenancy-rbac-design.md` §9; `docs/security-threat-model.md` §9.6 updated (MFA now implemented, network hardening still deferred).
  - **Tests (+16 → 67/67)**: `TenantIsolationSqlTest` additions (the bypass shape: role-scoped, GUC-gated, no FORCE, rejects unsafe role/table/command); **`SuperAdminBypassTest`** (DB fuzz — context open ⇒ sees all users cross-tenant; unset ⇒ fail-closed; the GUC set on the ordinary app role widens nothing — committed users on `pgsql_privileged`, asserted by id, no `afterEach` DELETE); **`SuperAdminConsoleTest`** (the `superadmin`/`superadmin.mfa` gates all-cases, suspend/reactivate + authorization, business-rule redirect-back, `withoutVite()` on GETs). **Red-then-green proven** for both crux mechanisms: removing the `superAdminBypass` policy → the positive fuzz test goes red; removing `superadmin.mfa` → the no-2FA redirect test goes red.
  - **Verified**: `docker compose exec app php artisan test` = **67/67** on real Postgres; `composer run quality` (Pint + Larastan L8 + controller-gate + migration-lint) + `npm run type-check` green. **Deferred**: platform `settings`/billing/`feedback_reports` consoles + cross-tenant `audits` search + `Auditable` logging (Phase-1 tables); impersonation; login-time MFA + network hardening; styled console UI → **C**.

- [x] **Phase 0 Increment C1 — Design-system foundation + package wiring + styled auth flow (2026-07-05, merged to `main` via PR #6 `48fc7c4`; verified 72/72 on real Postgres locally + CI, 27 axe stories light+dark).** The first of the three C sub-increments — makes the design system real in the app and ships the first *visible* surface. Built:
  - **Tokens** (`packages/design-system/tokens/`): added `space/typography/radius/elevation/motion/breakpoint.json`; **replaced `primitive.json`/`semantic.json` with the exact ratified DSR §2.2 hexes** (the seed values had drifted — primary/neutral/success/warning/danger full scales, Paper/Ink neutrals, the 3-role Bahnschrift/system-sans/mono font stacks replacing Nunito). Semantic layer expanded to the full §2.2 alias table + button/input-state aliases so components stay **semantic-token-only**; added the `--mds-font-family-body` dyslexia re-point hook. Style Dictionary build unchanged (values authored as complete CSS strings so no numeric transform rewrites them; `outputReferences` keeps semantics as `var(...)`).
  - **Dark theme rebuilt** (`src/theme/theme-overrides.css`): an authentic **"blueprint print"** — `:root[data-theme-mode='dark']` **flips the neutral *primitive* custom properties** to their deep drafting-blue dark-column hexes, and the semantic aliases (emitted as `var(--mds-neutral-*)`) follow automatically; a handful that use a *different* step in dark (bg-surface, action-primary, focus-ring, danger-text, input-border, on-warning, scrim) are re-pointed, contrast-checked for the dark ground. Same flip mirrored in the `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` "system" fallback. Added the central `prefers-reduced-motion` guard collapsing all `--mds-duration-*` to `1ms`. Kept the `data-accent='teal'` Phase-2 hook.
  - **Package → app wiring**: `package.json` `exports` gains `./theme.css`; root `package.json` consumes it via **`"@meridian/design-system": "file:packages/design-system"`**; `vite.config.ts` gets **`resolve.dedupe:['vue']`** (the load-bearing fix — the package carries its own `vue@3.5` for Storybook; without dedupe the source `.vue` could resolve a second Vue → broken reactivity) + `optimizeDeps.exclude`; `tsconfig.json` `paths` maps the package to its `src/index.ts` (types resolve, `exclude:["packages"]` kept); `resources/css/app.css` `@import`s tokens.css + theme.css and sets token-based `body` defaults; Storybook `preview.ts` renders the canvas in the body font on the themed ground so axe measures true contrast. **De-risk smoke** (MdsButton in Dashboard → build + type-check, no dual-Vue warning) passed before building UI.
  - **Server-side theme emission**: `User::uiPreference()` (`HasOne`) + `uiTheme()` (fail-safe via `rescue()` + `->value('theme_mode')` — the belongs-to-user RLS read degrades to `system` when `app.current_user_id` isn't set, never throws); `UserUiPreference::user()` (`BelongsTo`); `HandleInertiaRequests::share()` exposes `auth.user` + `ui.theme`; `app.blade.php` emits `data-theme-mode`/`data-accent` on `<html>` from the shared prop, **"system" = absence of the attribute** (guests emit nothing). The `accent`/`font_size`/`dyslexia_font` columns are deferred to C2 (not exercisable on C1's guest-only surfaces; blade + theme CSS are already accent-ready).
  - **Components** (`packages/design-system/src/components/`, replicating the `Button.vue` semantic-token/`:focus-visible`/BEM pattern; each state incl. dark = its own axe-scanned story): expanded **`Button`** to `variant: primary|secondary|tertiary|destructive` × `size: sm|md|lg` + `loading` (inline spinner, `aria-disabled`, stays in tab order, 44×44 hit-area), fully tokenized; new **`FormField`** (shared wrapper: real `<label>`, "(required)" text, help, error = **icon + text** in `aria-live="polite"`, exposes `{id, describedby, invalid}`), **`TextInput`**, **`PasswordInput`** (Show/Hide text toggle with `aria-pressed`, WCAG 2.5.3-correct name). Exported from `src/index.ts`.
  - **Auth layout + page restyle**: `resources/js/Layouts/AuthLayout.vue` (slim centered card, owns the shared `.auth-*` helper classes as a global style block so pages don't each re-invent them; logged as **`docs/ux/exceptions-log.md` #1** — a Guest-shell family member, not a third top-level shell). **All 8 pages restyled** (`auth/{Login,Register,ForgotPassword,ResetPassword,ConfirmPassword,TwoFactorChallenge,VerifyEmail}.vue` + `invitations/Show.vue`) using the shared components — **`<script setup>`, `useForm`, and every Fortify endpoint left intact** (template-only changes).
  - **Tests/gates**: new `tests/Feature/Theme/ThemeEmissionTest.php` (5 cases — guest share structure + system-omit, `uiTheme()` fail-safe + dark-read-with-context, and a real `view('app', …)` render proving `data-theme-mode="dark"` emission). **72/72** on real Postgres; **27** axe stories green (light+dark); `composer run quality` + `npm run type-check` + `npm run build` green. CI `frontend` job already ordered `ds:tokens` before `type-check`/`build` (no change needed); the ds-governance same-PR CI gate is **deferred to C2**. `dist/tokens.css` + `storybook-static` stay gitignored (CI regenerates via `ds:tokens`).

Built:
  - **Persistent app shell**: `resources/js/app.ts` resolver now default-assigns `resources/js/Layouts/AppLayout.vue` to every tenant page (excluding `auth/`, `invitations/`, `admin/`, `Welcome`, which keep their own chrome) — a stable module-level import so Inertia keeps ONE persistent shell instance across visits. `AppLayout` = `TopNav` + `Sidebar` + a scroll-container content region (max-width, `--mds-space-8`/`-4` padding) + the **page-header slot contract** (each page renders `<PageHeader>` as its first child; the layout never renders the header, since Inertia injects the page as the layout's default slot).

  - **Design-system primitives** (`packages/design-system/`, dark-inclusive axe stories, exported): **`MdsIcon`** — a single component over a hand-authored inline-SVG registry (`icons.ts`, 15 glyphs), **no external icon dependency** (keeps the guest runtime request-free); decorative-`aria-hidden`-vs-control-`aria-label` convention documented as **DSR Appendix B** + exceptions-log #2. Plus `MdsCard`, `MdsEmptyState`, `MdsSegmentedControl` (native-radio radiogroup; **selected = a solid filled chip**, not the subtle tint, because the tint fails AA in dark — Appendix A #13), `MdsSpinner`.
  - **Theme toggle — for real**: `App\Http\Controllers\Tenant\PreferencesController@updateTheme` (thin, validates `theme_mode ∈ {system,light,dark}`, `UserUiPreference::updateOrCreate` keyed on `user_id`) on `PATCH /settings/appearance` **inside the authed tenant group** (so `app.current_user_id` is set — the belongs-to-user RLS key). `resources/js/Pages/Settings/Index.vue` (Appearance panel) + the top-nav quick toggle (exceptions-log #3, a beyond-spec addition the user requested) both use the shared `useThemePreference()` — **apply instantly client-side** (SPA visits swap `<body>`, not `<html>`, so the client sets/removes `data-theme-mode`; `system` = remove) then persist; the blade root emission stays the durable source. **No migration** (`theme_mode` column already existed).

Two type-fixes worth knowing: the Inertia resolve must return `page.default` (the component with `.layout` attached), not the module, to satisfy the resolve type; and the global `PageProps` augmentation made two existing pages' `usePage<{errors}>()` generics fail the constraint → switched them to the augmented `usePage()`.

Built the full app surface (confirmed via AskUserQuestion: full scope + Playwright gate this increment):
  - **Six design-system primitives** (`packages/design-system/src/components/`, all with light+dark axe stories, semantic-token-only, `:focus-visible`, ≥44px targets): **`MdsBadge`** + a single central **`statusVariant`** enum→variant map (`status-variant.ts`) — needed new `color.status.{success,warning,danger,info,neutral}.{bg,fg}` semantic tokens (only `danger` was aliased before) re-pointed for dark in `theme-overrides.css`; **`MdsCheckbox`** (mirrors `TextInput`'s id/describedby/invalid contract; check glyph is the non-color signifier); **`MdsSkeleton`** (reduced-motion aware); **`MdsModal`** (self-contained focus-trap + Escape + scroll-lock, top-level `<Teleport>` portal via a `teleport` prop the stories set false so axe scans it, full-screen sheet ≤480px); **`MdsToast`** + **`MdsToastHost`** (presentational; the app owns the store `useToast` + a server-flash → toast bridge in both layouts) with per-toast polite/`role="alert"` semantics; and the flagship **`MdsDataTable`** — a **generic-over-row** component (so `#cell-<key>`/`#row-actions` slots are fully typed, no consumer casts) owning column layout, sortable `aria-sort` headers, a structure-preserving Skeleton loading state, a mobile card-per-row collapse, and an `#empty` slot.
  - **Shared-props plumbing** (`HandleInertiaRequests::share()`): `auth.can` (fail-closed ability map — `manageMembers`/`transferOwnership`; team set during render, false off-tenant) drives a **permission-aware Members nav item**; `flash.toast` bridges controller `->with('toast', …)` to the `MdsToastHost` in `AppLayout`/`AdminLayout`. `types/inertia.d.ts` extended.
  - **Members management page** (`Pages/members/Index.vue`): new `GET /members` route + `MemberController@index` (gated `can:tenant.members.invite` — the closed 27-permission catalog has no `members.view`) + **`TenantMembershipService::listMembers`** (active + pending; resolves pending-invite identities on **`pgsql_auth`** since the join-shape `users` RLS hides them, `withTrashed` so the map is always complete). DataTable + status Badges + invite/remove/transfer-ownership Modals + success Toasts.
  - **Admin console restyle**: a minimal central-domain **`AdminLayout`** (exceptions-log **#4** — Console-shell family) + restyled `admin/{Tenants,Users}.vue` (DataTable/Badge + suspend/reactivate confirm Modals + Toasts) + a **styled shared `TwoFactorSetup` component** (drives Fortify's 2FA endpoints — QR + recovery codes + confirm) used by both the super-admin `admin/TwoFactorSetup.vue` and tenant Settings → Security.
  - **Feedback backend + panel** (PRD #11): `feedback_reports` migration (strict tenant RLS via `withTenantIsolation`; screenshot + cross-tenant support console deferred to Phase 1) + `FeedbackReport` model + `FeedbackStatus` enum + thin `FeedbackController@store` (`can:feedback.submit`, all roles) + `POST /feedback`. `FeedbackButton` upgraded from the C2 "Coming soon" stub to a real **`MdsModal`** + working submit (route + browser_info + remarks) + Toast.
  - **Settings expansion** (`Settings/Index.vue` → `PreferencesController@show` passing 2FA state): **Profile** (Fortify `PUT /user/profile-information`) + **Appearance** (existing) + **Security** (password `PUT /user/password` + the styled 2FA enrolment) — delivers the tenant-user 2FA surface deferred since B1.
  - **Playwright responsive + composed-page axe gate** (PRD Feature #5 / metric G6): root `@playwright/test` + `@axe-core/playwright`, `playwright.config.ts` (3 projects at **375/834/1440px**, webServer boots `artisan serve` in CI, reuses a running app locally), a guarded **`E2eSeeder`** (tenant `acme` + Owner login + a pending invite), `tests/e2e/{global-setup,responsive-axe.spec}.ts` (**18 tests**: 3 pages × light/dark × 3 widths — zero axe violations + no horizontal overflow), and the previously-stub CI `e2e` job wired (provision roles → migrate → seed → build → `playwright test`). MFA-gated admin routes are covered by a composed **"list page" Storybook story** instead of a full Playwright drive (avoids TOTP-in-CI) — documented, not silently cut.
  - **Tests (+6 → 81/81 on real Postgres)**: `MembersIndexTest` (403 for a non-manager; roster lists active + pending with **pgsql_auth-resolved** identities — committed users on `pgsql_privileged`, no `afterEach` delete), `FeedbackTest` (submit under RLS, validation 422, cross-tenant isolation), `SettingsPageTest` (renders with 2FA state). **83 axe stories** green light+dark (incl. the composed list-page). `composer run quality` (Pint + Larastan L8 + controller-gate + **migration-lint** on the new table) + `vue-tsc` + `vite build` all green.
  - **Two real bugs caught & fixed during verification**: (1) the `MdsCheckbox` **disabled label** dimmed to `text-disabled` (~3.5:1) but the `disabled` attr sits on the hidden input not the visible span, so axe couldn't apply the disabled-contrast exemption → dimmed to `text-secondary` (≥4.5:1); (2) `E2eSeeder` set the RLS GUC via transaction-local `applyLocal` **outside a transaction**, so the membership INSERT hit `new row violates row-level security policy` — wrapped the writes in `DB::transaction` (would have failed the CI e2e seed step too). **Local gotcha (not a code issue)**: the root `npm install` for Playwright pruned the **isolated** design-system package's `node_modules` (the documented Vite-8-vs-Vite-6 no-hoist reason) → `npm ci` in `packages/design-system` restores it; CI is unaffected (it installs DS deps *after* the root install).

## Phase 2 — Increment G decomposition (G1→G11, dependency-ordered)

Plan: `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\read-progress-md-and-start-shimmying-yeti.md` (full current-state map + G1 detail). Each Gn is a mergeable PR with the standard CI gates. Entry point (repeat-groups-first, backend-core-first) chosen via AskUserQuestion 2026-07-11.

- **G1 — Repeat groups: pipeline + engine core (backend)** ✅ **MERGED (PR #27 `4656283`).** Nested JSONB-array instance storage under the section key; `StructuralAnswerNormalizer` nesting + per-instance coercion; `SemanticValidator` (PHP **+** TS mirror) per-instance relevance/required/constraint scoping with `section[i].field` addressing (via `SemanticError::path()`); min/max instance-count enforcement in Stage 3 (relevance-aware); index stays scalars-only; 9-vector `repeats.json` golden drift suite (manifest 38→47). **Grammar FROZEN at v1.0** (no `count()`/arithmetic — that is G3).
- **G2 — Repeat groups: runtime + manual-encode UI** ✅ **MERGED (PR #28 `21e3326`).** Wired `is_repeatable`/`min_instances`/`max_instances` through the SPA schema-mapping (they were dropped, so the G1 engine repeat pass was inert client-side); `useFormRuntime` manages nested instance answers with **stable per-instance `uid`s** (middle-row removal never mis-addresses touched/error state), consuming `repeatFieldRelevance` + instance-addressed errors, min/max count errors, address-based `erroredItems`, repeat-aware multi-step gating; new `RepeatGroup.vue` + `InstanceField.vue` (fieldset/instance, add/remove + aria-live + focus mgmt, disabled-add-at-max, retain-and-restore) via `SectionView`; `EncodeFormPresenter` unlocks repeatable sections + `Encode.vue` add/remove loop (nested answers keyed `answers[sec][i].fld`, 422 by path). Vitest 219 (+15), Pest 504 (+3 encode HTTP). No engine/grammar/migration change.
- **G3 — Full expression engine + calculated fields** ✅ **MERGED (PR #29 `846071f`).** Grammar v2.0 (doubly-mirrored PHP+TS, held byte-identical by the golden suite run through BOTH engines): arithmetic `+ - * /` (precedence, left-assoc, unary minus; NaN-propagating; div-by-zero → NaN → blank), `>=`/`<=` (numeric-only — date ordering DEFERRED to preserve the pinned non-numeric-→false contract), function library `if/3` + `count/1` (repeat instances + multi-select) + `int/1` + `today/0` + `now/0` (from an injected ISO clock on `EvaluationContext`, never the system clock — deterministic + cross-engine identical). `calculate` → `SemanticResult.computed` (relevant top-level calc fields' `config.calculated_formula`, evaluated over the effective answers incl. repeat instance arrays so `count(${section})` works) → **write-back** into `submission_answers.answers` + the typed index; publish gate parses/resolves calc formulas + section keys are referenceable; builder Calculation-formula editor in `ConfigPanel`. `GRAMMAR_VERSION` 1.0→2.0 across both engines + all vectors; golden expr 109→180, validation 47→55. Pest 598 + Vitest 299 green; no migration, no `ci.yml` change.
- **G4 — New field-type renderers: matrix / Likert (scale+matrix) / cascading select.** ✅ **COMPLETE** (G4a PR #30 `409898a` + G4b PR #31 `59ca345`).
- **G5 — Geo: geopoint / geotrace / geoshape.** The one UNDESIGNED surface → decomposed into **G5a (spike → ADR-0006, docs-only) + G5b1 (backend + PostGIS + engine) + G5b2 (Leaflet renderer + builder + E2E)** (AskUserQuestion 2026-07-12; plan `read-progress-md-and-build-glittery-sutton.md`).
  - **G5a — geo-storage spike → ADR-0006** ✅ **MERGED (PR #32 `d2a3ff7`, all 6 CI jobs green).** ADR-0006 **Accepted**: **hybrid storage** — the GeoJSON envelope `{type, coordinates:[lon,lat(,alt)], accuracy?}` is the source of truth in `submission_answers.answers`, **and** a persist-time **GiST-indexed PostGIS `geometry(Geometry,4326)`** projection (`submission_geo_index`) is written atomically alongside it (Risk R1 pattern extended to geometry); **capture** = a bundled, lazy-loaded **Leaflet** map over **online OSM** tiles atop a mandatory offline **manual lat/lon + `navigator.geolocation` GPS** baseline (never-unfillable). Value shape pinned (GeoJSON `[lon,lat]` order everywhere internal; ODK `"lat lon alt acc"` swap confined to the G7 converter). The **Linux-PostGIS path validated** in a throwaway `postgis/postgis:17-3.5` container (`CREATE EXTENSION` needs a **superuser/privileged role** — the `NOSUPERUSER` app role is denied; `geometry(4326)` + GiST + `ST_GeomFromGeoJSON` round-trip + **FORCE RLS under `meridian_app`** all verified); **Windows-prod install carried as a documented deploy-time risk**. Risk **R11 resolved**; spike `docs/spikes/geo-storage-spike-plan.md`; ADR `docs/adr/0006-geospatial-field-storage-and-map-picker.md`. **Docs-only** (no product code / no migration / no `ci.yml` change) — all 6 CI jobs pass trivially.
  - **G5b1 — backend + PostGIS + engine** ✅ **MERGED (PR #33 `3c4ae07`, all 6 CI jobs green first run).** Docker + all 3 CI Postgres services → `postgis/postgis:17-3.5`; `CREATE EXTENSION postgis` via the dual init-SQL (`docker/postgres/init/02-enable-postgis.sql` + CI heredocs, superuser) + guarded `pgsql_privileged` migration (self-contained `migrate:fresh`); `submission_geo_index` migration (`geometry(Geometry,4326)` + GiST + strict RLS via `withTenantIsolation`) + `SubmissionGeoIndex` model/factory + `GeoIndexProjector` (bound `ST_SetSRID(ST_GeomFromGeoJSON(?),4326)`) written inside `SubmissionPipeline::persist`'s transaction (top-level geo only, Risk R1); `coerceGeo` Stage-1 arm (`expected_object`/`geo_malformed` + ordinate coercion); a **doubly-mirrored** `processGeo` Stage-3 pass (`SemanticValidator.php` + `semantic-validator.ts`, bypasses scalar `isEmpty`, empty envelope unset for `{}`/`[]` parity; slugs `geo_out_of_range`/`geo_too_few_points`/`geo_not_closed`/`geo_type_mismatch` + `field_required`); `FieldType::isGeo()`; `GeoAnswer` union (`EngineValue` FROZEN); `geo.json` golden (12 vectors, manifest 88→100, run through BOTH engines); publish gates (forbid expr-refs-a-geo via the shared object-valued-keys map + ban geo-in-repeatable-section, 2 new `PublishValidationException` factories); `SchemaValueFormatter` geo summary. Geo stays `supported=false` → frontend untouched. **No `GRAMMAR_VERSION` bump.** Pest 673 + Vitest 348; `G5FieldTypesTest` proves the SRID-4326 `ST_AsText` round-trip + cross-tenant RLS on the new table.
  - **G5b2 — Leaflet renderer + builder + E2E** → **SPLIT into G5b2a + G5b2b** (AskUserQuestion 2026-07-12; map editing = full click-to-draw + drag).
    - **G5b2a — geo capture renderer + Leaflet + scoped CSP + wiring** ✅ **MERGED (PR #34 `53cdffe`, all 6 CI jobs green).** `leaflet` + `@types/leaflet` (bundled, no CDN); lazy code-split `GeoInput.vue` (a11y baseline = labelled Lat/Lon(/Alt) inputs + GPS + keyboard vertex list; progressive-enhancement Leaflet+OSM map, own chunk so geo-free forms load neither) emitting the frozen lon-first envelope (empty→null, ring auto-closed); img-src-only `PublicRuntimeSecurityHeaders` CSP (`config/geo.php` OSM origins) on the guest + encode routes; lockstep control-kind wiring across `FieldInput`/`schema-mapping`/`types`/`useFormRuntime`/`FieldControl`/`EncodeFormPresenter` flips geo to **`supported=true`**; E2eSeeder geo in "Field Types Showcase" + `public-runtime-axe` scan (OSM tiles blocked in-spec); Vitest 352 + Pest 675. (CI fixes: replaced Leaflet's low-contrast attribution control with accessible AA text; de-flaked the tampered-token guest test.)
    - **G5b2b — builder geo config editor + Map tab** **← NEXT.** `FieldType::configEditor()` geo arm → `'geo'`; `GeoEditor.vue` (capture_altitude / accuracy_threshold / default center+zoom, numeric — no builder map); a "Map" tab in `ConfigPanel.vue`; a lenient nullable geo arm in `UpdateFieldRequest::configRules()`; a geo builder-demo draft + builder-axe Map-tab walk. Outline: plan `composed-gathering-teacup.md` §G5b2b.
- **G6 — Media capture: image/audio/video** (build out `attachments` write path).
- **G7 — Bidirectional XLSForm import/export** (depends on repeat/cascading/expression).
- **G8 — Installable PWA + offline sync** (service worker + IndexedDB/Dexie + Background Sync + 409-conflict UX; Risk R4; biggest lift).
- **G9 — Question library + form templates** (schema-complete; UI + instantiate/clone + RLS carve-out).
- **G10 — Generalized `morphTo` resource-scoping** (largely independent).
- **G11 — Richer personalization (#9)** — accent/text-size/dyslexia (columns exist).

*(Newest entry on top. Every entry: date, what was in progress, what's left, any blockers.)*

- **2026-07-26 (Phase 3 — Increment H12b: scheduled-forms UI; MERGED (PR #64 → `2278f2d`, squash + delete branch, all 6 CI green); H12a was also merged first this session)** — **First action: merged H12a (PR #63 → `a07beae`, squash + delete branch, all 6 CI green), branched H12b off `main`.** Plan `…starry-matsumoto.md`; three decisions locked with the user (TZ = wall-clock-in-zone stored UTC · builder = modal-from-toolbar-button · encode = banner+disabled-submit). **Builder:** new `ScheduleModal.vue` from a "Schedule" toolbar button (XLSForm-import `MdsModal` precedent) → `PATCH /forms/{form}/schedule` via Inertia `useForm().patch` (inline 422s via `MdsFormField :error`); native `<input type="datetime-local">` (no DS date primitive) + `MdsSelect` tz + `MdsNumberInput` cap + "Clear schedule"; `BuilderPresenter` surfaces the raw window/cap + the **server-canonical `DateTimeZone::listIdentifiers()`** (client list can't drift → no 422). **TZ linchpin (caught by a test):** `UpdateFormScheduleRequest` parses the naive wall-clock in the chosen zone **then `->utc()`** before storage — Eloquent's datetime cast serializes a Carbon in its OWN zone as a naive string, so a non-UTC offset is silently dropped by the `timestamptz` column otherwise; the first draft (`->date($k,null,$tz)` without `->utc()`) stored 09:00-as-UTC and the new test failed → fixed. **Guest:** `SchemaResponse.form.schedule` typed; `App.vue` full-screen closed/opens_soon/capacity state (`MdsEmptyState`) **fresh-load only — resume path never gated** (H12a grace); `RuntimeSession.submit()` **fail-closed offline for scheduled/capped forms** (unscheduled keep the G8b outbox); mid-fill 403 → new `schedule` `ErrorKind` → the state. All copy/predicates in one testable `lib/schedule.ts`. **Encode:** `EncodeFormPresenter` emits the twin block via a shared pure `FormScheduleView` (both presenters delegate → can't drift); `Encode.vue` banner + disabled submit when `acceptance != open`. **Gates (local):** vue-tsc 0 (both tsconfigs, run as `node node_modules/vue-tsc/bin/vue-tsc.js` — the `.bin` shim isn't on PATH under the Windows `--no-bin-links` install); Pint + PHPStan L8 **0** on changed files; Pest — `FormScheduleSettingsTest` (+TZ +BuilderPresenter props, 6), new `EncodeFormSchedulePresenterTest` (5), guest + `ScheduledFormAcceptanceTest`/`MaxResponsesCapacityTest` (53) + Forms/OpenApiContract (124) regression-green; **no `openapi.json` drift** (guest block delegates but keeps its `@return` shape). Vitest (`schedule.test.ts` + error-normalizer 403 cases) + the seeded **Closed Survey** `/f/closed-survey` public-runtime-axe scan are **CI-only** (Windows can't run Vitest/Vite/Playwright). **CI caught one regression the local gates couldn't:** the new **Schedule** toolbar button pushed the non-wrapping `.builder__actions` past narrow viewports / large personalization fonts → the responsive-overflow gate failed, and that overflow cascaded into axe **color-contrast** flags as buttons spilled off the light toolbar surface (the closed-form scans I added all passed). Fixed with `flex-wrap` on `.builder__actions` (commit on the same PR); re-ran → all 6 green → squash-merged. **Lesson for H13a+:** any new builder-toolbar control must clear the responsive-overflow gate — the Playwright/axe job is the only place this surfaces (Windows can't run it locally). **Done:** H12a + H12b both merged; **next = H13a** (webhook engine core, H-map row 235). **No forward seams broken;** narrowings: native tz select (no typeahead) + native datetime-local (no styled DS date primitive) both deferred; `capacity_reached` still doesn't emit `form.closed` (H12a synchronous guard only).

all three open decisions locked with the user to the recommended options: **include the per-form toggle · newest-wins non-destructive reconciliation · persist the current step server-side**). **Guest SPA** (`resources/public-runtime/`): `main.ts` reads `data-resume-token`; Plan mode. **Method:** 3 parallel `Explore` surveys (tenancy/RLS plumbing · CI/compose/lint conventions · tests/services conventions) → a 14-agent design workflow (6 parallel area designs → **7 adversarial verifiers**, each tasked to REFUTE rather than confirm → 1 synthesis). **The verifiers earned their keep twice over: they REFUTED two risks I had flagged as likely blockers, and CONFIRMED five places where ADR-0007 is contradicted by the installed framework.** **User decisions up front:** split **H2a (substrate) / H2b (gate + ops)**; **DELETE** the forbidden `deferred`/`background`/`failover` connections rather than annotate them; **bump `composer.json` `"php"` `^8.3 → ^8.4`** (every real environment was already 8.4 — `ci.yml:14`, `docker/Dockerfile:3`, local 8.4.14 — only the manifest lagged) to unlock the abstract-property-hook enforcement. **The five ADR corrections, each mutation-proven and each written back into ADR-0007 as a `> Corrected in H2 (as-built)` block:** §D4's after-edge is **`JobAttempted`, not `JobProcessed`** (`Worker::process` raises `JobProcessed` on the success path only at `:561` and dispatches `JobAttempted` from the `finally` at `:566-570`, so the ADR-as-written leaks the tenant static out of every job that throws — mutation fails exactly the "clean after a job that THREW" case); §D4 must **save/restore, not flush** (under `sync` — every CI job — `SyncQueue` raises both events *inline in the caller's stack*, so a blind flush corrupts the dispatching request; the stack must be `static` because `Dispatcher::createClassCallable` resolves a fresh listener per dispatch; deliberately **not** gated on `connectionName`, which would make the listener dead code in every CI job); §D7's **`$tries = 3` inverts §D9** (`DatabaseQueue::release():328` preserves the incremented `attempts`, so three fairness *deferrals* → `failed_jobs`; replaced by `retryUntil()` + `$maxExceptions`, and the mutation is subtle — `$tries` *with* `retryUntil` changes nothing because `maxTries` is then dead code, so the test kills the ADR's actual combination and the comment says so); §D6's **`$queue` property is a fatal error** (`Illuminate\Bus\Queueable:29` declares it untyped) → the framework's `#[Queue(QueueName::X)]` attribute, which is inherited from a parent and *more* statically checkable; and §D13's **`status !== TenantStatus::Active` is always true** (uncast `?string` vs enum object) and would have **released-then-deleted every job for every tenant on the platform** — fixed with `Tenant::isActive()`/`scopeActive()` sharing one definition with §D3's fan-out. **Two feared regressions REFUTED and confirmed:** `after_commit => true` and the flush listener break **zero** existing tests, because the only dispatch site is reached by no un-faked test — which is reassuring for the diff and damning for the status quo. **Built (H2a):** `QueueName` enum · `InvalidJobPayloadException` + `dontRetry` · `TenantContext::restoreMirror()` · `Tenant::isActive()/scopeActive()` · `config/queue.php` (`retry_after` 90→120, `after_commit` true, three connections deleted, `retention_days`, sqlite→pgsql fallbacks) · `config/queue-fairness.php` (ceilings labelled unvalidated **in the file**, plan-tier hook with no Cashier dep) · **`TenantAwareJob`** (abstract property hook + runtime uuid assertion incl. the sentinel case + `final` handle/middleware/failed/retryUntil + `final false $deleteWhenMissingModels`) · **`MaintenanceJob`** (`dontRelease()` mandatory — the constructor defaults `$releaseAfter` to **0**, not null) · `ScanAttachmentJob` refit (ctor order preserved; `G6FieldTypesTest:254` constructs it positionally) · **`ScopeTenantContextToJob`** · `QueueServiceProvider`. **Built (H2b):** `scripts/job-payload-lint.php` (two-pass FQCN→ancestry map; **never `->getLast()`**, so a shadow-named `TenantAwareJob` in another namespace goes red — verified; §D5 inverted into a **closed scalar whitelist** because "is this an Eloquent model" is undecidable from a declaration) wired into **both** `composer.json quality` **and a new `ci.yml` step** (mandatory — **no CI job runs `composer run quality`**) · `PruneFailedJobsJob` · `routes/console.php` schedule · compose `worker` + `scheduler`. **Gates: Pest 1001 → 1045 (+44)** across 7 new `tests/Feature/Queue/**` files; **PHPStan level 8 ZERO on every H2 file** (77 app-wide = documented local baseline; PHPStan also caught a real dead-code branch of mine); Pint green; `schedule:list` = `10 3 * * *`. **Verification went past the suite, as the plan required:** a genuine **two-process** run and then the **compose `worker` service** each drove a real attachment `pending → skipped` unattended — and that exercise found a defect no test could: `php:8.4-fpm-alpine` ships **no php.ini**, so CLI `memory_limit` is exactly 128M and `queue:work`'s default `--memory=128` can never fire its graceful recycle before PHP's hard limit, turning a clean restart into a fatal OOM mid-job → `--memory=112`. **Gotcha worth keeping: `sed -i` with a `$` anchor silently no-ops on this repo's CRLF files** — a mutation that appears to apply but doesn't will quietly tell you a test is stronger than it is; verify the mutant landed before trusting the red. **Narrowings stated rather than papered over:** `$timeout` is unverifiable in CI (SIGALRM armed only in `daemon()`); the genuine §D8 two-process race is not reproducible in-process (the *ordering contract* is covered, the race is not); `MaintenanceJob`'s fan-out arm has **no production caller** until the first rollup vertical; everything contends on one Postgres instance; `RateLimited` md5s its bucket key so fairness state is not greppable by tenant. Plan file: `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\read-progress-md-h1a-is-proud-galaxy.md`. **Next: branch, commit, PR, get CI green — then H3 (queued mail), which is likely the first `EXEMPT_JOBS` entry.**

- **2026-07-20 (Phase 2 — Increment G10a: generalized `morphTo` resource-scoping, backend; BUILT + MERGED (PR #45 `9d7319e`, all 6 CI jobs green on the FIRST push — no follow-up fixes needed, unlike G9a/G9b which each took 3))** — User: "Read PROGRESS.md and build Increment G10." Planned in plan mode. **Key discovery: G10 had NO design spec** — a one-line roadmap bullet repeated in 5 places, with the entire written contract being PRD §6/§7, architecture §5 row 11, and RBAC §8:215. Two readings were possible (generalize `form_collaborators` vs. build the literal PSGC/catchment successor); the user chose a **unified** design where the `morphTo` IS the generalization and forms + hierarchy nodes are two morph targets, split **G10a (backend) / G10b (UI + API)**. Design was produced by a 12-agent workflow (3 independent designs → 3 judges → 5 adversarial attacks → synthesis; 52 findings), which reversed three things a naive implementation would have shipped wrong: (1) the backfill must run on `pgsql_privileged` — migrations run as `meridian_app` with no tenant GUC, so under FORCE RLS a naive `INSERT…SELECT` reads/writes/throws nothing and the drop migration then destroys every access grant, silently, on a green log; (2) the obvious upward `LIKE '%/id/%'` ancestor test is unindexable and sits inside the inbox query — reversed to downward constant-prefix matching, which requires `COLLATE "C"`; (3) the polymorphic hole is real and must close at the DB (verified `strictSql()` emits only `WITH CHECK (tenant_id = ctx)`, so a grant pointing at another tenant's form is *accepted* by Postgres). **Also surfaced a real shipped gap, now documented:** `forms.collaborators.manage` + `FormPolicy::manageCollaborators` exist but are referenced nowhere else, and the only production grant writer is `FormService::create` (always Editor, always the creator) — so **the Reviewer role has never been assignable in production**. G10a makes it reachable; G10b makes it clickable. **Built:** 5 migrations (`scope_nodes`, `forms.scope_node_id`, `resource_grants`, backfill, drop), `TenantIsolation::resourceGrantGuard` (6th RLS variant), `ResourceGrantResolver` + `GrantSet`, `ScopeNodeService`, `CollaboratorBackfill`, `ScopeNodePolicy`, 2 enums, 2 models, 2 factories, rewired `FormPolicy`/`SubmissionPolicy`/`Submission::scopeVisibleTo`/`FormService`/`FormPresenter`, `scopes.manage` as a 28th permission. **Gates:** Pest 818 → **900** (+82), Larastan 78 = exact `main` baseline with **zero errors in G10a files** (the rest are the documented local phantom class), Pint / migration-lint / controller-gate / vue-tsc green, no `openapi.json` drift. **Behaviour-identical by construction** (post-backfill every grant targets a form, every `scope_node_id` is NULL, no writer for either ships), and the pre-existing suite passed unchanged — that is the regression proof. **One deliberate exception, flagged in the PR:** `SubmissionPolicy::create` now requires Editor capacity rather than either. **Local test-authoring gotchas worth remembering:** RLS fixtures must go on the DEFAULT connection under the owning tenant's context (`pgsql_privileged` commits outside RefreshDatabase's transaction and cannot see tenants the test just created), and a test asserting a throw must issue no further statements (Postgres aborts the surrounding transaction). A plan-shape `EXPLAIN` assertion was tried and abandoned: on a few-hundred-row table the planner is indifferent between `(tenant_id, path)` and `(tenant_id, is_active)` and tie-breaks unpredictably, so it measured the cost model rather than the property — replaced by asserting the two structural facts (C collation + the composite index). **Left for G10b:** all routes/UI/API, the escalation rules, `ScopeNodeService::move()`, the `scope_node_id` picker, blast-radius preview, the `tenant_users` composite FK, audit entries (blocked until Phase 1). **Two product calls flagged for the user:** single-node-per-form (confirm before the picker ships), and the pre-existing `FormPresenter::list()` disclosure (every Form Editor already sees every form's title/version history — one-line fix, deliberately out of G10a as a visible change to a shipped screen).

- **2026-07-11 (Phase 2 — Increment G3: full expression engine + calculated fields — grammar v2.0; MERGED to `main` PR #29 `846071f`, all 6 CI jobs green first run)** — User: "Read PROGRESS.md and build Increment G3." G3 is the biggest doubly-mirrored engine lift (Risk R3): a grammar-**v2.0** bump held byte-identical between the PHP authority (`app/Services/Expressions/*`) and the TS client mirror (`resources/public-runtime/engine/*`) by the shared golden suite run through BOTH engines (Pest + Vitest). **Two permanent, contract-shaping forks confirmed via AskUserQuestion** (the F2/F3 precedent): (1) **date ordering DEFERRED** — `today()`/`now()` ship as injected ISO strings (equality + `calculate` + `if()` only); chronological `<`/`>` of dates is NOT added because the existing golden contract firmly pins `>`/`<` as **numeric-only** (12+ vectors assert a non-numeric operand → false), and lexicographic date ordering would flip all of them; deferred to a dedicated date increment. (2) **computed write-back = persist + index.** **Engine (both mirrors):** new tokens `+ * / >= <=` (the lexer already had `-`); new `ArithmeticNode` + `ArithmeticOperator` (add/sub/mul/div); parser precedence chain inserts additive < multiplicative < unary-minus below comparison (`-<number>` still folds to a negative literal for v1.0 byte-compat, `-<operand>` lowers to `0 - operand`); `ComparisonOperator` gains `Gte`/`Lte` (evaluator `numericCompare` refactored to take the op; **structured conditional rules can now use gte/lte too** — a bonus, and it keeps the `conditionForOperator` match exhaustive); `FunctionRegistry` opens `if/3`, `count/1`, `int/1`, `today/0`, `now/0` (parser gains a general `parseCall` + per-function `validateCallArgs`; `selected` keeps its bespoke string-literal-arg path); `FunctionCallNode.kind()` is now name-dependent (Value for the value-functions, Boolean for `selected`/`contains`). **Evaluator semantics (total over data):** arithmetic coerces both operands via `toNumber`; a NaN operand (empty/non-numeric) OR division by zero → NaN, **normalised to `null` at the value boundary** (a blank computed value) + `toBool(NaN)=false` (new guard); `count(x)` = array length (repeat instances / multi-select) / 0 empty / 1 non-empty scalar; `int(x)` = truncate-toward-zero (`(int)`/`Math.trunc`), non-numeric → 0; `today()`/`now()` read `EvaluationContext.now` (an ISO string the CALLER stamps — server time in the pipeline via `Carbon::now()->toIso8601String()`, a fixed value in golden vectors — so the two engines never diverge and never read the system clock; `today()` = `substr(now,0,10)`). **`calculate` → `computed`:** `SemanticValidator` reads each **relevant top-level** calculated field's `config.calculated_formula`, evaluates it over the FULL effective answers (flat + the repeat instance arrays merged in after `processRepeats`, so `count(${section})` works), and writes `computed[key]`; a hidden calc / blank / NaN result is omitted; calc fields are **skipped in the required/constraint pass** (they are outputs, not inputs); calc-in-repeat is deferred (documented). `now` is threaded through every `EvaluationContext` construction (settle/error/instance/compute). **Pipeline write-back:** `SubmissionPipeline::persist()` merges `$result->computed` into the persisted `submission_answers.answers` + runs `projectIndex` over the merged set (a queryable calc field lands in the typed index). **Publish gate:** `ExpressionValidationGate` parses + resolves each `calculate` formula (kind `Calculate`, so a `.` self-ref is rejected), and **section keys are now added to `knownKeys`** so `count(${roster})` publishes (field+section keys are globally unique per version). **Builder:** `ConfigPanel` gains a **Calculation formula** textarea for calculated fields (bound to `config.calculated_formula`, hidden placeholder/requiredness for calc); relevant-expression help text refreshed for the live v2.0 grammar. **SPA:** `buildEngineSchema` maps `calculate` from `config.calculated_formula` (the client engine is complete; the SPA does not yet render calc fields — engine-focus). **Golden / drift contract:** `GRAMMAR_VERSION` **1.0 → 2.0** across both engines + ALL vectors (scripted `grammar_version` bump); new `arithmetic.json` (33) + `functions.json` (31) + `validation/calculated.json` (8); **`parse-errors.json` refreshed** — the once-rejected `today()`/`if()`/`count()`/`now()`/`int()`/`>=`/`<=`/arithmetic are now VALID and moved to positive vectors, replaced by new arity/kind/`unknown_function` error cases; expression manifest **109→180**, validation **47→55**; both golden runners extended to map a field's `calculate` + a vector-level `now`. Also updated the pre-existing `ExpressionLexerTest`/`ExpressionParserTest`/`engine.test.ts` unit assertions that pinned the v1.0 rejections. **Verified: Pest 598** (real Postgres; +~94: the new golden datasets + evaluator/pipeline/gate tests), **Vitest 299** (the PHP↔TS drift gate — both engines byte-identical on all 235 vectors + the new grammar), **vue-tsc** clean, **Vite build** green, **Pint** (297 files) + controller-gate + migration-lint green, **phpstan 74** (fixed the 3 real G3 findings — the `is_string`-always-true in `fromVector`, the void-closure in `validateCallArgs`, and the non-exhaustive `conditionForOperator` match; the remaining 74 are the known environmental model-column phantoms CI's Larastan migration-scan resolves to 0). **PHP gotcha (again): double-quoted test strings interpolate `${var}` (removed in PHP 8.3) → use single-quoted PHP strings for grammar snippets, or double-quoted grammar literals (`"1"`).** **Outcome:** PR #29 → **all 6 CI jobs green on the first run** (incl. Static-analysis — confirming the 74 local phpstan errors were purely the env model-column gap) → squash-MERGED (`846071f`), branch deleted, local `main` synced. **No migration, no `ci.yml` change.** No blockers. **Known-flaky follow-up still open (not G3): `GuestRuntimeTest › tampered token` (F5) flips only the token's last base64 char.** **Next: G4 (matrix / Likert / cascading-select renderers).**
