G2 makes the two answer-collection channels **consume** the G1 engine (nothing was rendering repeat groups yet). **Load-bearing discovery:** the wire `schema_snapshot` already carries `is_repeatable`/`min_instances`/`max_instances` per section, but the SPA's `buildEngineSchema`/`buildRenderModel`/`RawSection` type were **dropping** them — so the G1 repeat pass was inert client-side; G2 wires them through (the pre-req for everything else). **Public-runtime SPA:** `useFormRuntime` widened to `AnswerMap` (`EngineValue | InstanceAnswers[]`), owns nested instance answers keyed by section key, with a **stable per-instance `uid`** kept in lockstep with the array so a middle-row removal never mis-addresses another row's touched/error state (touched is uid-keyed, not index-keyed). Per-instance relevance/required/constraint/error come STRAIGHT from the engine (`repeatFieldRelevance[sec][i][field]` + `errors` filtered by `sectionKey`+`instanceIndex`) — the store evaluates nothing; adds min/max count errors (gated on section-attempt/submit), address-based `erroredItems` (`sec[i].field`), and repeat-aware multi-step `attemptNext` (blocks on the section's aggregated errors + touch-all-instances). `snapshotAnswers()` deep-reads instance maps so the `result` computed tracks nested edits (a top-level spread would miss them) — and it must NOT spread a flat multi-select `string[]` as instances (guarded on `repeatSectionKeys`). New `RepeatGroup.vue` (fieldset-per-instance loop, add/remove with `aria-live` + focus mgmt, disabled-add-at-max, min/max bounds hint + count error) + `InstanceField.vue` (per-instance FieldRow analog: relevance show/hide + retain-restore + focus rescue + `field-<address>` anchor), wired into `SectionView`; `SummaryBanner`/`PageView`/`StepView` switched to addressed banner items. **Manual encoding:** `EncodeFormPresenter` unlocks repeatable sections (emits the section `key` + `min/max_instances`; members now `supported`); `Encode.vue` renders an add/remove loop with nested Inertia form data keyed `answers[sectionKey][i][fieldKey]` (monotonic-counter uids — NOT `crypto.randomUUID`, which throws on http localhost) — 422s bind by path (`answers.sec[i].fld`; `answers.sec` for count failures). **NO engine/grammar/migration/`ci.yml` change** (grammar frozen v1.0; `count()`/arithmetic = G3). **Runtime is a strict superset of the server:** the client feeds RAW instances (1:1 with UI rows) to the engine — client per-field required on an empty added row is a reasonable stricter guard vs the server dropping it, and it prevents a divergent submit. **Tests:** Vitest 219 (+13 runtime-unit incl. the uid-stable-touched-across-removal proof, per-instance relevance + cross-row independence, min/max, nested submit body, multi-step gating; +2 component: add→fill→submit nested + server-422-onto-instance), Pest 504 (+3 manual-encode HTTP: block metadata, nested persistence, instance+count 422s). Seeder: a guest-enabled repeatable **Household Roster** (single-page) + Playwright axe scans for the runtime + encode repeat UIs. vue-tsc + Vite build + Pint + controller-gate + migration-lint green; phpstan only the known environmental model-column phantoms (CI Static-analysis job → 0). **CI iteration (3 pushes):** (1) initial → E2E red: the runtime repeat scan timed out clicking "Add" because the seeded form defaulted to **multi-step** (`single_page_mode` false) so the repeat section sat behind a Next step → seeded it single-page; (2) E2E still red at dark/mobile encode — a `color-contrast` fail on the shared `.mds-button--primary` (#4e86a8/white = 3.96): **that colour is `--mds-primary-400`, the button's `:hover` bg** — Playwright's cursor was parked over the Submit button (the prior "New submission" click coordinate lands on it in that layout), so axe measured the hover state → fixed by `page.mouse.move(0,0)` in the shared `assertClean` (resting-state measurement; also removed a same-cause builder-mobile flake) + `emulateMedia({reducedMotion:'reduce'})` in `forceTheme` (settled palette); (3) all 6 green except a **flaky pre-existing `GuestRuntimeTest › tampered token` (F5)** — it flips only the token's last base64 char, which for some random tokens aliases to the same signature bytes (base64 unused low bits) → 200 not 401; passed on re-run (unrelated to G2 — Pest never runs `E2eSeeder`). **Outcome:** PR #28 → all 6 CI jobs green → squash-MERGED (`21e3326`), branch deleted, local `main` synced. **Known-flaky follow-up (not G2): fix `GuestRuntimeTest`'s tamper to mutate a guaranteed-significant byte.** **Next: G3 (full expression engine + calculated fields — grammar v2.0 bump; Risk R3).**

- **2026-07-10 (Phase 1 — Increment F6a: TypeScript expression + semantic-validation engine mirror + Vitest golden drift guard; MERGED to `main` PR #24 `a938e5c`, all 6 CI jobs green first run)** — User: "Read PROGRESS.md and build Increment F6." confirmed **F6a-first** via AskUserQuestion (foundation-first, lowest-risk, matches the user-confirmed sequencing). **F6a = the client half of the form engine: a byte-for-byte TypeScript mirror of the PHP authority** (`app/Services/Expressions` F2 + `app/Services/Validation` F3) that the F6b SPA will consume for relevance/skip-logic + hybrid on-blur/on-submit validation, while PHP stays the sole authority at submit time. **This closes Risk R3 (PHP↔TS drift) behind pure unit tests, ahead of any SPA UI** — exactly the F1/F2/F3 pattern (backend/lib, user-invisible). **Built** `resources/public-runtime/engine/` (new standalone-SPA tree, NOT Inertia/`resources/js`): `coercion.ts` (the normative §6 primitives — the ordered 6-rule Eq table, numeric-before-string `toBool`, anchored `NUMERIC_RE` with NO `u` flag, UTF-8 **byte**-length 2000 cap via `TextEncoder`, code-point `mb_strlen` via `[...s]`, `ABSENT` sentinel), `enums.ts` (string-union mirrors of the backed PHP enums), `tokens.ts`, `errors.ts` (`ExpressionSyntaxError`/`ExpressionEvaluationError` with the stable `slug` the golden `expected_error` asserts), `ast.ts` (discriminated-union nodes + `kind()`), `lexer.ts` (ASCII-only `ctype_*` classifiers — deliberately NOT `\s`/`\d`/`\w`), `parser.ts` (recursive descent, result-kind operand typing, depth-64 cap), `context.ts`, `function-registry.ts` (`selected` public only), `evaluator.ts` (short-circuit logic, `selected`/`contains` membership, the §6.2 equality rules keyed on **node kind** not runtime value), `ast-builders.ts` + `lowering.ts` + `structured-rule-evaluator.ts` (the 11 `ValidationRuleType`s in 3 families; anchored `/^(?:…)$/u` pattern with unescaped-`/` escaping; PHP `(int)`-cast + code-point length), `semantic-validator.ts` (the bounded fixed-point relevance settle → `SemanticResult`), `index.ts` barrel + `makeExpressionEvaluator()`/`makeSemanticValidator()` factories. **Golden runners** (`engine/__tests__/golden-expressions.test.ts` + `golden-validation.test.ts`) iterate the SAME repo-root `tests/golden/**` fixtures the Pest runners use and mirror them exactly — per-vector assertions **plus the count-parity / per-file / name-uniqueness manifest guards** so neither engine can silently drop a vector — **109 expression + 38 validation vectors** + a 5-test public-API smoke = **154 Vitest tests, all green on the first run** (the byte-exact spec paid off: zero drift). **Toolchain:** added **Vitest 4.1.10** (devDependency) + `vitest.config.ts` (node env; no jsdom/Vue plugin needed until F6b's component tests) + `test`/`test:watch` scripts; wired a **Vitest step into the CI `frontend` job** (after type-check, before build); type-check the engine via a `tsconfig.json` `include` add (`resources/public-runtime/**`, `*.test.ts` **excluded** — vitest transpiles them, vue-tsc doesn't need the node/vitest globals). **Also fixed the recurring lockfile trap:** added `"name": "dev_formbuilder_app"` to `package.json` so in-container `npm install` stops rewriting the lockfile root name to `"html"` (the 2026-07-09 hygiene note's optional follow-up — done now since this increment's container install hit it). **No `vite.config.ts` entry / no blade / no route** — the standalone SPA Vite entry + `/f/{slug}` shell + renderers are **F6b**; the engine is consumed only by Vitest so far (`npm run build` is untouched). **Verification (Docker `node` container = Linux, mirrors CI, since the Windows host `npm install` hits the documented DS-esbuild-bin file-lock): `npm test` = 154 passed; `npx vue-tsc --noEmit` = clean.** No PHP/composer/golden-JSON changes (so the `static-analysis` + `tests` + `design-system-a11y` CI jobs are unaffected). **Squash-MERGED (`a938e5c`), branch deleted, local `main` synced — all 6 CI jobs green first run (the `Frontend build & type-check` job ran the new Vitest step in 31s). consume-time `allow_guest_submissions` re-check = 403 `guest_disabled` revocation lever; submit's `current_published_version_id !== token.vid` → 409 `form_updated`; builds `SubmissionPayload(source:Guest, respondentUserId:null, guest_ip/ua/email at submit-time, guestToken:fingerprint)`), thin `PublicFormPresenter` (wraps the already-render-ready `schema_snapshot`+checksum), `GuestSubmissionRequest`, `ReadsGuestShareToken` trait. **Routes:** new unauth subdomain group in `tenant.php` (mint, `throttle:guest-mint`) + new **Group C** `api/v1/public` in `api.php` (`throttle:guest` + `EstablishGuestTenantContext` + `SubstituteBindings`). **`bootstrap/app.php`:** 3 new `$isApi`-gated render closures — `Invalid`/`ExpiredShareTokenException` → 401 `invalid_share_token`/`share_token_expired`, and **`SubmissionException` → 409 `submission_version_superseded` (fixes a PRE-EXISTING 500 gap** — Stage 2a had no closure). **Rate limiters** `guest` (per-token + per-IP) + `guest-mint` (per-IP) in `AppServiceProvider`. **Scramble:** the two public ops get `@unauthenticated` (→ `security:[]`, overriding the global sanctumToken) + a `summary` line (Redocly requires it); schema 200/401 tamper/401 expired/403 revoked/superseded-still-reads; submit 201 full-provenance DB assert [source=guest, respondent null, tenant auto-stamped, guest_ip/ua/email, guest_token=fingerprint, answer doc + typed index] / 422 nothing-persisted / idempotent replay / **409-not-500 republish** / forged-token 401 / revoked 403; cleared cache + `--memory-limit=2G` gives the true count — **F5 branch = 78 = clean `main` (all pre-existing environmental model-introspection errors CI resolves), F5's own files phpstan-clean**. - **2026-07-09 (Phase 1 — Increment F4a: unified Submission Pipeline backend; MERGED to `main` PR #21 `4a3bee5`, all 6 CI jobs green first run)** — User: "continue F4." Planned in plan mode (3 Explore agents mapped the §4.1 pipeline spec + data model, the controller/routing/UI patterns, and the service/transaction/exception patterns; approved plan `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\goofy-singing-forest.md`). Confirmed 3 forks via AskUserQuestion: **split F4 into F4a (pipeline backend) → F4b (manual UI)**; **render-supported-mark-the-rest** field coverage (F4b); **submit-only** (no draft save; Phase 3). **F4a = the single write path every ingest channel funnels into (technical-architecture.md §4.1), backend-only, no HTTP caller yet** (F4b's manual channel + F5 guest both call it). New namespace **`App\Services\Submissions\`**: **SubmissionPipeline** (`submit(SubmissionPayload): SubmissionResult`, singleton) runs the 4 stages — **(1) Structural** `StructuralAnswerNormalizer` (per-`FieldType` coercion via shared `Coercion`; rejects unknown keys + type-mismatch, aggregating all errors; drops empties/`note`/`page_break`/`calculated`; yes_no→bool, multi_select→`list<string>`); **(2) Integrity** version-is-published + `client_submission_uuid` idempotency (replay → `created:false` no-op, incl. a `23505` race-catch); **(3) Semantic** delegates to F3 `SemanticValidator::validate()` (`!passed()` → throw), keeping `effectiveAnswers`; **(4) Persist** one `DB::transaction` → `Submission` (status=Submitted, source, respondent, `submitted_at`) + `SubmissionAnswer` (relevance-pruned jsonb + `answers_schema_checksum` copied from `form_versions.checksum`) + one `SubmissionAnswerIndex` per queryable scalar via **AnswerIndexProjector** (author-set `indexed_data_type` → the typed `value_*` column; guarded `Carbon` parse for date/datetime), then `SubmissionCreated` event **post-commit**. **Stage-1/Stage-3 reconciliation (decision):** required/constraint/relevance is delegated entirely to F3's relevance-aware Stage 3 (a skip-hidden field is not required — strictly more correct than a naive Stage-1 presence check); Stage 1 is structural-only. **Persist scope:** submissions + answers + index only — the architecture's `INSERT attachments`/`INSERT audit record` are stubbed TODO (no `attachments`/`audits` tables in Phase 1, same posture as PublishService). DTOs `SubmissionPayload`/`SubmissionResult` (both `final readonly`); exceptions `SubmissionValidationException` (per-field `{field,rule,message}` + `stage` slug, ExpressionException accessor style — for F4b's 422 envelope) + `SubmissionException` (state, e.g. not-published); `SubmissionCreated` event (no listeners yet — forward hook); registered via new **`SubmissionServiceProvider`** singletons (in `bootstrap/providers.php`). **Verified locally: 414 Pest passed (+24 — `StructuralAnswerNormalizerTest` + `AnswerIndexProjectorTest` pure over unsaved models; `SubmissionPipelineTest` real-Postgres: happy-path persist + typed index, relevance pruning drops irrelevant answers from doc+index, missing-required + failed-constraint → `SubmissionValidationException` w/ nothing persisted (tx rollback), unknown-key structural reject, client-uuid idempotent no-op, non-published refusal); Pint clean, controller-gate + migration-lint pass.** **Larastan gotcha (env, NOT a code defect):** a cold local `phpstan` reports **74 "undefined property" errors on DB columns in PRE-EXISTING files** (SchemaSnapshotSerializer/BuilderPresenter/…) — **identical count on a stashed clean `main` tree**, so F4a adds ZERO (grep confirms none in the new namespaces; all my model access is `@property`-annotated). This local env's Larastan can't introspect model columns (though `Schema::getColumnListing` sees them) even after clearing all caches; **CI's Larastan works cold (green at F3/PR #20), so it's the authoritative gate here.** No migrations, no JS, no `ci.yml` change. **Pushed → PR #21 → all 6 CI jobs green first run (incl. the Larastan `quality` job — confirming the local 74 was purely env) → squash-MERGED (`4a3bee5`), branch deleted, local `main` synced. No blockers. Next: F4b (manual-encoding UI — `SubmissionController` + `EncodeFormPresenter` + `SubmissionPolicy` + Encode.vue + routes + the `bootstrap/app.php` submit-time exception closures + Playwright).**

- **2026-07-09 (Working-tree hygiene — Docker HMR fix committed; lockfile drift reverted; `main` clean & synced, commit `81377f2`)** — User flagged a lingering "uncommitted" state and asked to make git identical across working copies. (2) **`package-lock.json` — environmental noise, reverted** (`git restore`): because `package.json` has **no `name` field**, an `npm install` run *inside the container* (`/var/www/html`) had rewritten the lockfile's `name` → `"html"` plus peer/optional-dep (`@emnapi/*`) churn; discarded (npm regenerates it cleanly from the correct dir). **Both actions user-confirmed via AskUserQuestion (commit+push / revert).** Verified after: `git status` clean, `HEAD == origin/main == 81377f2`. Also recorded the two gotchas in `README.md` §"Windows / Laragon gotchas" (the HMR/blank-page fix + the `name:"html"` lockfile-drift trap). **Recurring-drift caveat left open (low, optional):** every in-container `npm install` will keep re-dirtying `package-lock.json` with `name:"html"` until `package.json` gets a `"name": "dev_formbuilder_app"` field — a one-line follow-up if the churn becomes annoying. No blockers; Phase 0 still COMPLETE, next = Phase 1 (MVP).

- **2026-07-07 (Follow-up fix — tenant routes on the central host redirect to the app home instead of 500ing; MERGED to `main` PR #16 `421d103`)** — While testing the merged app locally the user hit a raw 500 (`NotASubdomainException`, "Hostname localhost does not include a subdomain") at `http://localhost:8080/dashboard`. **Root cause is pre-existing, NOT from Increment E** (verified: the E merge touched none of `routes/tenant.php`, the tenancy middleware, or `config/tenancy.php`): `/dashboard` is a tenant-**subdomain** route, and `localhost`/`127.0.0.1`/the apex are central domains with no subdomain, so `InitializeTenancyBySubdomain::makeSubdomain` returns `NotASubdomainException`. It only surfaces once **authenticated**, because `auth` is priority-ordered BEFORE the tenancy pipeline (an unauthenticated request just 302s to `/login`; a logged-in user's post-login redirect to `/dashboard` on the central host is what throws). **Fix:** extended the `withExceptions` handler (which already answers the `/api/v1` surface with a `tenant_not_identified` 404) so a **web** request for a tenant route on the central/non-subdomain host — or an unknown subdomain (`TenantCouldNotBeIdentifiedOnDomainException`) — redirects to the central app home (`config('app.url')`) instead of 500ing. Behavior chosen with the user (redirect-to-central over a bare 404). **+2 tests** (`tests/Feature/Tenancy/CentralHostFallbackTest.php`, `actingAs()` so the request reaches the tenancy pipeline); **150/150 Pest**, `composer run quality` green, no API-spec change; all 6 CI jobs green → squash-merged (`421d103`), branch deleted, local `main` synced. **The demo tenant is reachable at `http://acme.localhost:8080` — log in there and use `/dashboard`; bare `localhost:8080` is the central host** (`*.localhost` auto-resolves in most browsers; otherwise add `127.0.0.1 acme.localhost` to hosts). Known rough edge left for later: Fortify's post-login redirect to `/dashboard` isn't tenant-subdomain-aware, so logging in on the central host now lands on the central Welcome rather than the dashboard — a Phase-1 UX polish if wanted. No blockers; next = the deferred form-engine spike (ADR-0004) or Phase 1.

- **2026-07-07 (Increment E — OpenAPI 3.1 scaffold + `/api/v1` token surface — built, verified, adversarially reviewed, MERGED to `main` PR #15 `226bef7`; Increment E COMPLETE)** — User: "continue increment E." Planned in plan mode (2 Explore agents + 1 Plan agent validating the crux against Sanctum 4.3.2 vendor source), confirmed scope via AskUserQuestion: **Standard surface** (Forms read + versions read + publish + tenant profile) + **backend-only key issuance** (no UI). **Architecture A (validated):** the API is served on the tenant subdomain (`acme.meridian.test/api/v1/…`) and reuses the whole RLS pipeline; a new plain **`AuthenticateApiToken`** middleware (NOT `auth:sanctum`, which the priority sorter would float ahead of tenancy) is inserted in the priority array right after `EstablishTenantDatabaseContext`, so the Sanctum token lookup runs with `app.current_tenant_id` already set — **the strict RLS on `personal_access_tokens` reveals a token only on its own tenant's subdomain (cross-tenant tokens are invisible → 401), and a removed member's token 401s automatically** (its tokenable User is hidden by the `users` join-shape policy). **No new DB role, no PAT RLS change.** Built: the `sanctum` guard (config/auth.php); a custom **`App\Models\PersonalAccessToken`** (`BelongsToTenant`+`TenantScoped`, registered via `Sanctum::usePersonalAccessTokenModel`) so a mint auto-fills `tenant_id` and passes the strict `WITH CHECK`; the `{error:{code,message,details}}` envelope (§2.3) as `withExceptions` render closures; `throttle:api` (600/min, keyed on the bearer-token hash because throttle sorts ahead of auth); **`ApiAbilities`** catalog (§2.6) + `intersect()` (a key can never exceed its issuer's RBAC); thin controllers (`FormApiController`/`FormVersionApiController`/`TenantApiController`/`ApiTokenController`) reusing `PublishService`+`FormPolicy` verbatim; `FormResource`/`FormVersionResource`/`TenantResource` (+ an `ApiResource` base with typed `iso()`/`jsonObject()` helpers so Scramble emits nullable timestamps + jsonb-as-object); cursor pagination (`{data, meta:{next_cursor, has_more}}`). **OpenAPI:** `dedoc/scramble` ^0.13.30 (resolves clean on L13/PHP8.4) generates the committed **`openapi.json`** (deterministic title + `{tenant}`-templated server pinned via `Scramble::extendOpenApi`, guarded by `class_exists` since Scramble is dev-only); the stub CI **`contract-tests`** job now exports → **Redocly lint** (`redocly.yaml`) → **drift-diff** vs the committed spec (no DB needed). **Two crux bugs found & fixed during testing:** (1) **guard resolution** — `Auth::shouldUse('sanctum')` mutates `config('auth.defaults.guard')` to `sanctum`, so Spatie resolved permissions against the `sanctum` guard and every `can()` threw `PermissionDoesNotExist` against the `web`-seeded catalog → fixed with **`User::guardName()` returning `'web'`** (idiomatic Spatie multi-guard pin; also hardens the session path); (2) **error envelope** — render callbacks run AFTER `Handler::prepareException`, which rewrites `AuthorizationException`/`MissingAbilityException`→`AccessDeniedHttpException` and `ModelNotFoundException`→`NotFoundHttpException`, so the closures had to match the **post-conversion** types (recovering `MissingAbilityException` via `getPrevious()`). **Adversarial review (20-agent workflow) → 8 confirmed fixes applied:** contract-type accuracy (nullable timestamps/cursor as `["string","null"]`, `capability_flags`/`schema_snapshot` as `object` — fixed at the source via the `ApiResource` helpers so the drift-check stays green), envelope-escape robustness (a tenancy-identification **404** on the central host, a **`whereNumber('id')`** route constraint so a non-numeric token id 404s instead of 500ing, and a final **`Throwable` fallback** so nothing on `/api/v1` — incl. a 419 CSRF on the session-mint routes — escapes the envelope as a raw 500), plus **+5 tests** (token list/revoke + cross-user isolation, version list/show, scoped-binding 404, central-host 404). **Verified: 148/148 Pest on real Postgres** (+23 API tests: auth happy/sad, cross-tenant token invisibility, removed-member invalidation, ability + policy gates, error envelope shapes, cursor paging, publish-via-API, mint + ability∩RBAC trimming + tenant_id persistence, list/revoke, version endpoints, central-host); - **2026-07-06 (Increment D4b — drag-and-drop reordering + full a11y hardening — built, verified, MERGED to `main` PR #14 `3c86e93`; Increment D COMPLETE)** — User: "continue to next. D4b." All 6 CI jobs green on the first run, incl. the **merge-blocking interaction-driven `builder-axe` job** (E2E, 3m1s — the keyboard reorder + aria-live assertions + config-tab mounts + empty-canvas scan all passed at 375/834/1440px in light+dark on the real composed page). Squash-merged (`3c86e93`), branch deleted, local `main` synced. **Increment D (form_versions draft/publish + the full interactive builder) is COMPLETE across D1→D4b.** The last builder sub-increment: layered pointer drag onto D4a's keyboard foundation, meeting WCAG 2.2 AA. **Design decision worth remembering** — replaced D4a's per-row Move-up/down buttons with a single **left drag grip** that carries BOTH reorder modalities, which (a) declutters the row (fewer controls → no 375px overflow risk) and (b) gives a keyboard grab-mode that crosses section boundaries, which up/down pairs structurally can't (the WCAG 2.5.7 non-drag equivalent the plan requires). **Hand-rolled, no DnD library** (they inject aria-hidden drag-mirror DOM + steal focus → fail the merge-blocking axe gate): `useCanvasReorder.ts` owns both paths over the same store primitives. **Pointer drag**: pointerdown on the grip begins a reorder session; each pointermove `document.elementFromPoint`s the `[data-drop-group]` container (section id or `ungrouped`) + compares Y to the non-dragging row midpoints to live-reorder (the dragged `<li>` gets `pointer-events:none` so it doesn't shadow the hit-test); pointerup commits, a window Escape listener cancels; `touch-action:none` on the grip stops touch-drag from scrolling. **Keyboard grab-mode**: Enter/Space grabs → Arrow keys walk the field through the *flattened visual order* (`flattenedFields()` = ungrouped then each section's fields) crossing boundaries by adopting the neighbor's group → Enter drops, Escape cancels; focus stays on the grip throughout (the plan's "focus moves to the item, not a vanished button" — solved by there being no disabled button to vanish). Every transition is announced through a canvas **assertive `aria-live` region** (`Grabbed … position N of M in {section} … Dropped …`). A drag/grab is ONE store **reorder session** — `beginReorder()` snapshots order, local `placeField`/`stepFieldAcross`/`placeSection`/`stepSection` mutate live with no persist, then `commitReorder(label)` does one `persistOrder` + one undo entry (skipped if net-unchanged), or `cancelReorder()` restores. Dropped the now-unused `moveField`/`moveSection`; `moveFieldToSection` (config Section select, covers empty sections) stays. `BuilderCanvas` refactored to take the `store` directly (was emit-based). **`builder-axe.spec.ts`** (the merge-blocking gate D4b exists for): navigates via the list link, drives the config panel + all tabs (mounts MdsSelect/MdsTextarea/MdsNumberInput/MdsCheckbox/MdsSegmentedControl), selects the seeded **repeatable** section (so min/max NumberInputs render), executes a full keyboard reorder asserting the `.canvas__sr` aria-live text (Grabbed→position→Dropped), and scans the **empty-canvas** state — at 375/834/1440px in light+dark. E2eSeeder: the demo section is now repeatable + a second empty **"Blank Intake Form"** seeded for the empty-canvas scan. **Verified locally: 125/125 Pest, `composer run quality` (Pint + Larastan L8 + controller-gate + migration-lint), `vue-tsc`, `npm run build`, `npm run ds:test` (108 axe stories, 0 violations).** The one gate not re-run locally was the live Playwright/`builder-axe` (needs the `/etc/hosts` + host-serve setup prior increments deferred to CI; the Docker `web` service publishes no host HTTP port) — the builder was engineered axe-clean (grip buttons have `aria-label`, aria-hidden icons, no dangling aria refs) and **the merge-blocking `builder-axe` job passed on CI first run**. No blockers. **Next: Increment E (OpenAPI 3.1 scaffold) or the deferred form-engine spike (ADR-0004) — confirm scope with the user before starting.**

- **2026-07-06 (Increment D4a — interactive builder + config panels + undo/redo — built, verified, MERGED to `main` PR #13 `250911a`)** — User: "continue D4a." Built the three-pane form builder over the form's draft version (see the Status Summary / roadmap D4a entry for the itemized design). **Design system:** built the two D3-deferred primitives `MdsSelect` (native `<select>` + chevron; `inheritAttrs:false` so a bare `aria-label` forwards to the select, needed for dense editor rows) + `MdsNumberInput` (empty → `null`, not `0`), both with light+dark axe stories, and added builder glyphs to the icon registry (`chevron-up`/`copy`/`undo`/`redo` + category icons `type`/`hash`/`list`/`map-pin`/`image`/`layout`/`sliders`). **Frontend** (`Pages/forms/Builder.vue` + `components/builder/*`): `FieldPalette` (31 types grouped by category, advanced flagged), `BuilderCanvas` (selection + explicit Move-up/down + duplicate + delete; sections group their fields), `ConfigPanel` (real `role=tablist/tab/tabpanel` + arrow-key roving — NOT the radiogroup segmented control; `RequiredMode` IS the segmented control), `ChoicesEditor`, `ValidationEditor` (rule XOR expression), `ConflictDialog`. **`useBuilderStore.ts` is the undo/redo state core (locked decision)** — every add/delete/duplicate/move/config-edit is a reversible command on an undo+redo stack; rows keyed by a stable client `uid` decoupled from the server id so a delete→undo (which mints a NEW server row) still resolves; optimistic persistence through a serialized promise queue; config edits debounced into one history entry per burst. **`builderClient.ts` is the CSRF fetch seam** (XSRF-TOKEN cookie → `X-XSRF-TOKEN`, same-origin, `Accept: application/json`) — the only write-`fetch` in the app; Inertia can't be used or every edit would full-reload and wipe builder state. **409 optimistic-concurrency**: content PATCH carries the row's microsecond `updated_at` token; drift → 409 + fresh row → ConflictDialog preserves in-flight input. **Backend**: thin `FormBuilderController` (9 JSON methods, exceptions→status in one `respond()` helper) → `FormBuilderService` (draft-only via `assertDraftChild` + `forms`-row lock on structural ops; **reorder writes via the query builder so it does NOT bump `updated_at`** and can't spurious-409 the next content PATCH) → `BuilderPresenter`; `StoreFieldRequest`/`UpdateFieldRequest`/`UpdateSectionRequest`. `AppLayout` gained a full-bleed **fluid** variant (off the page component, single instance kept). **Fix worth knowing — `shouldRenderJsonWhen`**: the builder's JSON validation failures were rendering as HTML redirects (302) because `bootstrap/app.php` only rendered JSON for `api/*`; a FormRequest validation failure then crashed on the redirect path (`errors()->all()` on the HTML branch). Broadened the predicate to `is('api/*') || $request->expectsJson()` — Inertia visits don't `expectsJson()` so their redirect-with-errors flow is untouched; only true XHR (the builder) now gets 422 JSON. **Verified locally: 125/125 Pest on real Postgres (+9 — `BuilderRoutesTest`: viewer-forbidden / admin add-configure-duplicate-reorder-delete / stale-409-returns-current / no-token-overwrite / key-format+uniqueness-422; `BuilderDraftGuardTest`: published-field edit+delete rejected via HTTP (422) / draft still editable / RLS raw-UPDATE-on-published = 0 rows), `composer run quality` (Pint + Larastan L8 + controller-gate + migration-lint) green, `vue-tsc` + `vite build` green, `npm run ds:test` 108 axe stories 0 violations. Builder page added to `responsive-axe.spec.ts` (E2eSeeder demo form now seeds a section + 3 fields so the builder auto-selects one and the config panel mounts).** Playwright was the one gate not re-run locally (needs the `/etc/hosts` + host-serve setup prior increments deferred to CI) — the builder was engineered axe-clean (tab semantics, `aria-label` on every icon button, no dangling aria refs, panes linearize ≤1024px with no h-overflow) and **the live E2E Playwright+axe job passed on CI first run** (builder page scanned at 375/834/1440px in light+dark, zero WCAG 2.2 AA violations, no horizontal overflow). All 6 CI jobs green first run → squash-merged (`250911a`), branch deleted, local `main` synced. Pointer drag + the interaction-driven `builder-axe.spec.ts` are **D4b**. No blockers. **Next: D4b — drag-and-drop reordering + a11y hardening.**

- **2026-07-06 (Increment D3 — forms list + draft/publish UI (first UI surface) — built, verified, MERGED to `main` PR #12 `d1f146d`)** — User: "continue d3." Built the first Forms UI (see the Status Summary D3 entry): `forms/Index.vue` (DataTable + icon row-actions + create/rename/archive/publish/version-history-restore modals), thin `FormController`/`FormPublishController` + `FormMetadataRequest` + `FormPresenter`, `FormService::updateMetadata`/`archive` (§9), gated routes, the `manageForms` ability + enabled Forms nav, and two new DS components (`MdsTextarea` + `MdsIconButton`, both axe stories). Scope call: `MdsSelect`/`MdsNumberInput` deferred to D4a (built against real config-panel usage). **Three fixes worth knowing** — (1) **middleware-priority foundational fix**: route-model binding of a tenant-scoped model (`/forms/{form}`) 404'd because `SubstituteBindings` ran before `EstablishTenantDatabaseContext` (default priority floats bindings ahead of non-priority middleware) → set `$middleware->priority([...])` in `bootstrap/app.php` so the tenancy pipeline (Initialize→PreventAccess→Establish) runs after Authenticate but **before SubstituteBindings**; fixes binding for every tenant-scoped model. (2) **FormPolicy `hasPermissionTo`→`can`**: `hasPermissionTo('forms.create')` THROWS `PermissionDoesNotExist` when no tenant catalog is seeded, and `HandleInertiaRequests::share()` calls `viewAny` on every response incl. the central admin console (no team) → 8 admin-console tests 500'd → switched to the exception-safe `$user->can()`. (3) **responsive-gate overflow**: the Forms table overflowed at tablet — 4 text row-buttons + a raw-ISO `Updated` column made the table 851px inside a 706px scroll box, and Chromium reports a clipped inner-scroll container's content on `documentElement.scrollWidth` (931px) even though `<body>` was contained; reproduced locally with a Playwright DOM probe → fixed with the compact `MdsIconButton` row actions (icon-only, `aria-label` required, ≥24px target) + a short localized date, table now fits (706px). Larastan also needed `@property Carbon` on `FormVersion.published_at`/`superseded_at` and an `array_values()` for the presenter's `list<>` return. **Verified: 116/116 Pest (+4 `FormRoutesTest`: viewer-forbidden / admin list+create / publish+archive / editor `.own` confinement), `composer run quality` green, `vue-tsc` + `vite build` green, design-system axe 95/95 (no violations), and the Playwright responsive/axe gate green with the Forms page added + a demo form seeded via the real publish path (all reproduced locally before pushing).** All 6 CI jobs green → squash-merged (`d1f146d`), branch deleted, `main` synced. No blockers. **Next: D4a — interactive builder** (section/field CRUD + config panels, all 31 field types, the client undo/redo edit-history stack designed in from the start, the 409 optimistic-concurrency contract + CSRF PATCH seam, and `MdsSelect`/`MdsNumberInput`). Approved plan: `please-check-what-is-serialized-star.md`.

- **2026-07-06 (Increment D2 — publish transaction + versioning services + FormPolicy — built, verified, MERGED to `main` PR #11 `bc2941f`)** — User: "build d2 now." Built the pure-backend publish machinery (no HTTP/JS — user-invisible, an accepted backend increment like A/B2a). See the Status Summary D2 entry for the itemized design: **FormService::create** (form + v1 draft + creator editor-collaborator row) · **PublishService** (the 9-step §3.2 transaction under `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` — snapshot+checksum taken while the version is still a draft, then flip→published, supersede the prior, point the form + recompute capability_flags, clone-forward a fresh draft) · **RestoreService** (forward-only draft repopulation) · **SchemaSnapshotSerializer** (the load-bearing canonical/deterministic snapshot + SHA-256 — id-stripped, FK-by-key, recursively ksorted, fixed JSON flags) · **SchemaTreeCloner** (the must-fix FK-remap so a clone never dangles on the immutable source) · StructuralValidationGate (§4) · SchemaChangeClassifier (§5) · CapabilityFlags · exceptions · **FormPolicy** `.any`/`.own` (registered in AppServiceProvider; no permissions added). **Verified: 112/112 real-Postgres Pest (+17 new — serializer determinism proven by inserting the same content in reversed order → identical checksum; the publish lifecycle asserts the cloned draft's fields+validation FKs resolve within the NEW draft, capability_flags recompute (geopoint → has_geofields/!ocr_compatible), first-publish no-op supersede, and forward-only restore leaves history intact; FormPolicy admin `.any` vs form_editor `.own`-via-collaborator vs viewer-denied), `composer run quality` (Pint + Larastan L8 + controller-gate + migration-lint) green.** Two fixes worth knowing: (1) a caught `QueryException` aborts the test transaction pattern didn't apply here (no throws mid-test), but (2) **Larastan L8 mis-inferred the nullable enum casts in the serializer** — notably `LogicOperator` (its `And`/`Or` reserved-word case names) resolved to `string`, tripping `?->value` — resolved with a small `enumValue(\BackedEnum|string|null): ?string` helper that is robust to the inference either way. Also ran Pint's fixer to convergence locally (two passes → 0 changes) before pushing, so — unlike D1 — the static-analysis job was **green on the first CI run**; all 6 jobs green → squash-merged (`bc2941f`), branch deleted, `main` synced. No blockers. **Next: D3 — forms list + publish flow** (the first UI surface: thin FormController/FormPublishController + gated routes + `forms/Index.vue` + the 4 shared DS components `MdsSelect`/`MdsTextarea`/`MdsNumberInput`/`MdsIconButton` + the `manageForms` ability prop + nav enable + E2eSeeder demo form + Playwright pages). Approved plan: `please-check-what-is-serialized-star.md`.

- **2026-07-06 (Increment D — planned in full; D1 (data foundation + RLS guard) built, verified, MERGED to `main` PR #10 `01e424b`)** — User: "check what is next in the pipeline." Found `next = D` and, since D-with-full-builder is large, planned it in plan mode across a fan-out design + **3 adversarial critiques** (Workflow), then confirmed 4 scope levers via AskUserQuestion: **split into D1→D4b** / **undo-redo designed into D4a** / **defer live preview** (blocked on the unbuilt form engine, ADR-0004) / **expose all 31 field types**. The critiques earned their keep — they caught a **real correctness hole** (published `form_versions` deletable under strict RLS → FK CASCADE bypasses RLS → wipes the "immutable" children) and a **live trap** (the pre-emitted `draftGuardSql`'s `status <> 'published'` UPDATE guard would make publish→supersede a silent zero-row no-op), plus the missing `tenants.default_locale` column. Approved plan: `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\please-check-what-is-serialized-star.md`. **Built D1** (see the Status Summary D1 entry for the itemized design): the 6 tables (thin `forms`; append-only `form_versions`; the three `draft_child`-guarded content tables; `form_collaborators` deferred here from B2c) with the forms⇄form_versions cycle broken by the 3-migration split, the `tenants.default_locale`/`supported_locales` add, 9 string-backed enums (+`FieldCategory`, `FieldType::category()`), 6 `BelongsToTenant`+`HasUuidv7` models + factories. **The load-bearing RLS work**: two NEW `TenantIsolation` generators — **`formVersionGuard`** (strict SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE so publish can move draft→published→superseded, **DELETE only a draft** so a published version is undeletable and its cascade can never reach the immutable children) and **`draftChildGuard`** (strict SELECT; INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE gated on `EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM form_versions fv WHERE fv.id = <child>.form_version_id AND fv.status='draft')`, emitted as the SOLE write policy per command so Postgres's permissive-OR composition leaves it restrictive) — wired via new `withTenantIsolation` variants `'form_version'`/`'draft_child'`; **`draftGuardSql` and its `form_versions`-named unit test retired** so the trap can't be re-wired. **Verified: `migrate:fresh` clean; full suite 95/95 on real Postgres** (11 new `FormVersionRlsTest` fuzz cases RED-then-GREEN — draft content writable / published INSERT rejected (WITH CHECK) / published UPDATE+DELETE silent no-op (USING) / published content still readable / published→superseded UPDATE succeeds (the anti-trap) / published version undeletable + its child survives / draft discardable / cross-tenant isolation + fail-closed / **exactly one write policy per command per child table via `pg_policies`**; + 3 new SQL-unit cases for the two shapes); **`composer run quality` (Pint + Larastan L8 + controller-gate + migration-lint) green.** One test-structure fix worth knowing: a caught `QueryException` aborts the surrounding Postgres transaction, so the fail-closed assertions had to move out of the same `it()` as the throwing insert (the throw must be a test's final DB interaction — same idiom the existing cross-tenant pack uses). Pint auto-imported the factory classes into the `@use HasFactory<…>` generics. **Pushed → PR #10 → CI red on the first run** (Static-analysis job: Pint `class_attributes_separation`) — a **two-pass Pint convergence** the **local Pint cache masked** (the fix run cached the files clean, so the immediately-following `pint --test` trusted the cache; CI runs cacheless). It wanted a blank line after the annotated `use HasFactory;`; a second fixer pass (confirmed idempotent — 0 further changes) added it, one follow-up commit, **then all 6 CI jobs green → squash-merged (`01e424b`), branch deleted, local `main` synced.** No blockers. **Next: D2 — services + FormPolicy** (pure backend; the publish 9-step tx + checksum + clone-forward FK-remap + the `.any`/`.own` policy).

(2) the dark-emission is bracketed by automated tests (guest omits the attribute; an explicit dark prop renders `data-theme-mode="dark"`). **Larastan fix worth knowing**: it types a `HasOne` property access as non-null, so `?->` on `uiPreference` read as "unnecessary nullsafe" → switched to `->uiPreference()->value('theme_mode')` (cleaner AND genuinely null-safe under RLS). **Deferred to C2** (recorded): `icon-only` Button + `Checkbox` + icon system; the `user_ui_preferences` `accent`/`font_size`/`dyslexia_font` columns + the Appearance toggle (blade + theme CSS are already accent-ready); the ds-governance same-PR CI gate. **Pushed → PR #6 → all 6 CI jobs green on Linux Postgres (incl. the axe gate on CI's chromium) → squash-merged (`48fc7c4`), branch deleted, local `main` synced.** No blockers. **Next: C2 — app shell.** The approved C1 plan (+ the C1/C2/C3 split): `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\now-i-understand-thank-replicated-wreath.md`.

Rewrote `users` → UUIDv7 + Fortify 2FA + `is_super_admin` + consent columns (sessions.user_id → uuid); added `tenant_users`, `user_ui_preferences`, `personal_access_tokens(+tenant_id)`; amended `tenants` (owner_user_id/status/stancl `data`); re-dated the stancl `domains` migration (uuid tenant_id, linter-exempted). **The crux — RLS-on-`users` vs. login**: solved with a dedicated non-superuser **`meridian_auth`** role + **`pgsql_auth`** connection + **`App\Auth\RlsAwareUserProvider`** (resolves users on the pre-auth connection; resets the authenticated model back to `meridian_app` so no elevated connection leaks into request code; keeps the credentials lookup on `pgsql_auth` for the password-reset save). Applied `TenantIsolation::usersVisibility()` (the join-shape SELECT) + `usersWritePolicies()`. **Design decision**: user WRITE policies are **permissive** (`USING/WITH CHECK (true)`), per RBAC §6 ("user writes are app-layer authorized, not tenant RLS") — the SELECT visibility policy enforces read isolation, and permissive writes let central account-management (2FA/profile/password, no tenant context) work. Breached-password check (`Password::uncompromised()`, ASVS L1) wired via `Password::defaults()`; email-uniqueness resolves on `pgsql_auth`. **Verified: `docker compose exec app php artisan test` = 31/31 on real Postgres** — login works end-to-end, the user-RLS fuzz pack (fail-closed / membership-join / active-only / belongs-to-me / PAT-strict) passes, registration + breached-password rejection pass, and the Increment-A fuzz pack still passes. `composer quality` (Pint, Larastan L8, controller-gate, migration-lint) green. **Test-harness note captured in `docs/testing-strategy.md`**: auth tests that log a user in must seed a COMMITTED user on `pgsql_privileged` (RefreshDatabase's transaction is invisible to the separate `pgsql_auth` session). **What's LEFT**: push branch → PR → merge (was about to). **Deferred within B**: the full 2FA enrolment-flow test (backend + columns are wired and the feature is registered; the styled enrolment UI + flow test land in C). No blockers. **Next: B2 (Spatie RBAC)** — see the "Next Session" Track A list.

## 2026-07-16 — G8b (offline outbox + Background-Sync replay + media queue + authenticated sync API) MERGED (PR #40 2b476e1)

Built the offline engine on the G8a shell. Dexie DB (4 stores) as the client source of truth; one framework-free `replayOutbox(db,fetch)` shared by the app driver (`useSyncOutbox`, triggered on online/visibility/manual "Sync now") AND the service worker (switched `generateSW`→`injectManifest`, `sw.ts` owns a Background-Sync `sync` handler for no-tab replay; classic bundled `sw.js`, WebWorker-lib `tsconfig.sw.json`). `RuntimeSession.submit()` enqueues to the outbox first (durable intent) then replays inline/queues; `201/200`→synced, `422`→needs_attention, `409`→conflict (G8c), network→retry w/ 5-attempt escalation; media uploads first + placeholder-ref rewrite. UUIDv7 client ids; autosave localStorage→Dexie `draft_answers`; `device_id`/`app_version` wired through `SubmissionPayload`→`persist()` + guest request/controller. New Group-B sync API: `GET /api/v1/sync/manifest` + batch `POST /api/v1/sync/submissions` (per-item results); guest PWA replays via guest endpoints. plainClone() fix: reactive proxies can't be structured-cloned into IndexedDB. Gates: Vitest 404, Pest 759, vue-tsc (app+SW), classic `sw.js` build, Redocly+no-drift, Pint/controller-gate/migration-lint; Larastan phantom-only locally (CI-green). Next = G8c (409 conflict-resolution UX).

## 2026-07-16 — G8c (409 conflict-resolution UX + answers-content-checksum conflict) MERGED (PR #41 6211916)

The last G8 slice; **G8 (PWA/offline) is now COMPLETE**. Two forks landed together (user-chosen): the conflict-resolution UX AND the optional answers-content-checksum 409. **Frontend (reuse the fill screen):** the parked `outbox status:'conflict'` rows (flagged by G8b's replay 409) get a **Review** CTA on `SyncStatus.vue` (`ConflictReviewKey`); `App.vue` `beginConflictReview()` re-mints + re-fetches the current schema and remounts `RuntimeSession` seeded with the saved answers (existing `restoreAnswers` drops stale keys + mints a fresh uuid) + a resolve notice — the same online-drift machinery from a parked-row entry point. Successful resubmit discards the old row (`discardConflict`); a "discard this response" hatch exists. `RuntimeSession` gains a `resolving` mode that suppresses autosave (new `enabled` guard on `useAutosave` — the durable copy is the outbox row) and hides `SyncStatus`. New: `listConflicts(slug)`, `nextConflict()`/`discardConflict()`; `conflict_code` on `OutboxRow` (non-indexed → no Dexie bump). Interactive resolution is current-form-slug-scoped (App's single share-token client is bound to `bootstrap.slug`). **Backend (content-checksum 409):** nullable `submission_answers.answers_content_checksum` + `AnswersContentChecksum` (canonical SHA-256, key-sorted, list-order-preserving); `SubmissionPipeline` hashes the normalized answers and, on a duplicate `client_submission_uuid`, compares stored-vs-incoming → match/null = the existing 200 no-op, differ = new `SubmissionConflictException` → **409 `submission_conflict`** (distinct from the version-drift `submission_version_superseded`; batch `sync/submissions` maps it per-item). **Narrowing:** no server-side merge (409 body omits the server's answers) → resolution resubmits the user's LOCAL copy as a fresh response. Gates: Pest 769 (+10), Vitest 414 (+10), type-check/build(sw.js)/Pint clean, OpenAPI no-drift, e2e conflict Review→resubmit→drains + axe. Larastan phantom-only locally (pre-existing `$user->id` param-type). CI 6-green on #41. Next = G9 (question library / templates).

## 2026-07-17 — G9a follow-up: post-merge review of PR #42 + hardening fixes

Post-merge review of the merged G9a diff (~2,577 lines / 37 files). **Verdict: the security-critical parts are correct** — `withTenantIsolation(...,'nullable_global')` emits the shared `TenantIsolation` policy SQL (no hand-rolled policies); cross-tenant instantiate 404s at route-binding time (the `{template}` binding resolves on the tenant connection, so class-level `can:create,Form` is sufficient); `saveAsTemplate` with no tenant context fails closed (`NULL = NULL` is NULL, not true); `FormTemplate` correctly omits `BelongsToTenant` per the `GlobalProbe` rationale. Findings were hardening/efficiency, none a live vulnerability.

**Fixed:** (1) `PlatformRowCounter` — the only request-reachable `pgsql_privileged` (superuser, RLS-bypassing) path in `app/`; the platform-row invariant lived only in the caller's PHP `tenant_id === null` check, so it now carries `whereNull('tenant_id')` in the query itself plus a `COUNTABLE` table/column allowlist; `config/database.php`'s "never used by request code" comment was stale (true until G9a) → rewritten to name this standing exception. (2) `TemplateGalleryPresenter` + `FormTemplateResource` no longer decode `schema_blueprint` in PHP just to count fields: new `FormTemplate::scopeWithoutBlueprint()` (card columns + `COALESCE(CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(...)='array' THEN jsonb_array_length(...) END, 0) AS field_count`) + `FormTemplate::fieldCount()` (prefers the projection, falls back to the in-memory blueprint for the freshly-created `store` row). The web gallery's `->get()` was unbounded and hydrated every blueprint. (3) `SchemaBlueprintMaterializer::materializeInto()` `$actor` made non-nullable — `form_fields.created_by` is NOT NULL, so the `?User $actor = null` default was a guaranteed 23502 raw QueryException past the fail-closed `BlueprintValidator` (and a trap in G9b's path; `materializeField` already required an actor). (4)+(5) Documented, not changed: the platform `usage_count` bump runs on a separate connection so it autocommits outside `instantiate()`'s transaction (accepted — a cosmetic counter drifting +1 on a rolled-back instantiate); and the API's `usage_count`-led cursor pagination can skip/duplicate mid-walk (accepted for a browse gallery).

**Dropped after investigation:** `BlueprintValidator` doesn't enforce `form_field_validations_rule_xor_chk` (exactly one of `expression`/`rule_type`) — unreachable today: no endpoint accepts a raw blueprint (`StoreFormTemplateRequest` takes only `form_version_id` + meta), save-as always round-trips through `SchemaSnapshotSerializer` reading valid rows, and `PlatformTemplateBlueprints` holds **zero** validations and is materialize-tested by `PlatformTemplateSeedTest`. **Flagged for G9b** — hand-authored library questions with validations are exactly the ingress that makes it reachable. Also dropped: `can.use` is always true (route + presenter compute the same `can:create,Form`), harmless as a future entitlement seam; and per-table RLS test duplication (the shapes are proven in `CrossTenantIsolationTest`).

**+4 tests → Pest 794 (0 fail).** The tenant-row guard test writes tenant + template on the **privileged** connection so it proves the guard bites rather than passing on row-invisibility (a row written on the app connection sits unseen inside `RefreshDatabase`'s transaction, and the test would pass for the wrong reason); **verified by mutation** — deleting `whereNull('tenant_id')` fails it. Gotcha found: `Tenant::on('pgsql_privileged')` is silently ignored (stancl's base model pins the central connection) → use the query builder. Larastan **77 errors before == 77 after** (all pre-existing elsewhere; 0 on touched files); Pint/controller-gate/migration-lint pass. Verified against real seeded data: all 10 platform templates' SQL `field_count` matches the PHP truth (4–7 fields each) and the blueprint never enters the model.

## 2026-07-18 — G9b Question Library (`field_library`) BUILT on branch `g9b-field-library` (PR pending)

Second half of G9. The builder's Fields ⇄ Library loop + `/api/v1/field-library`, on G9a's proven nullable-global plumbing — **no new infra**. New `field_library` migration (data-dictionary §11: nullable-`tenant_id` + `withTenantIsolation(...,'nullable_global')`; `is_active` + the per-field `field_type`/`default_label`/`default_config`/`default_validations`, no `schema_blueprint`) + `FieldLibrary` model (NO `BelongsToTenant`, like `FormTemplate`/`GlobalProbe`). Two mappers: `toBlueprintField()` rebuilds the 21-key `SchemaSnapshotSerializer` field shape from the stored columns (synthesizing defaults for the ~14 it doesn't persist) so `materializeField` can insert it; `fromField(FormField,$meta)` captures a draft field back into columns via a new public `SchemaSnapshotSerializer::snapshotField()` and **drops cross-field validations** (a non-null `related_field_key`) so a library question stays portable.

Reuses G9a's `SchemaBlueprintMaterializer::materializeField` (the single-field entry point written for exactly this) + `PlatformRowCounter` (now allowlists `field_library`) + `BlueprintValidator`. Two `FormBuilderService` methods: `insertFromLibrary` (mirrors `addField`: `DB::transaction` + `lockDraft` + `resolveDraftSectionId` + `materializeField` + `bumpLibraryUsage`; calls `BlueprintValidator::validateField` first because `materializeField` — unlike `materializeInto` — skips the validator) and `saveFieldToLibrary` (mirrors `TemplateService::saveAsTemplate`, `assertDraftChild`-guarded, tenant_id explicit). Collaborators resolved via `app(...)` inside the methods so the constructor-less `final` service keeps every `new FormBuilderService()` call site working.

**Carried-over #43 fix landed:** `BlueprintValidator` now enforces `rule_xor_chk` (exactly one of `expression`/`rule_type`) on both `validate()` (whole-form) and a new single-field `validateField()` — G9b's seeded/hand-authored validations are the first blueprints that make that DB CHECK reachable as a raw 23514 instead of a clean `FormException`.

Web (builder CSRF fetch sidecar): 3 routes under the builder block, all `can:update,form` — `POST fields/from-library`, `POST fields/{field}/save-to-library`, `GET library-items` — + `FormBuilderController` methods returning the same `presenter->field()` shape as `storeField` (store merges it exactly like `duplicateField`). Picker data ships as an **Inertia prop** on the Builder page (`BuilderPresenter::libraryList()`, active-only, popular-first) because the sidecar has no GET; a `builderClient.get` + the GET route were added for post-save/cross-session refresh. Vue: left-pane **Fields ⇄ Library** toggle (`aria-pressed`, not a tablist — no tabpanel to wire) + new `LibraryPicker.vue` (own + platform, "Meridian" badge, name filter, `MdsEmptyState`), store `insertFromLibrary`/`saveFieldToLibrary`/`refreshLibrary` (+ `library`/`librarySaved` state), and a one-click **Save to library** in `ConfigPanel`'s Advanced tab (names the item from the field label; transient confirmation, auto-cleared).

API (Group B): `GET/POST /api/v1/field-library` reusing `READ_FORMS`/`WRITE_FORMS` (no new ability/policy, no `can:` — not a bound model); lean `FieldLibraryResource` omits the jsonb; `store` authors from explicit attributes (jsonb cols default `{}`/`[]`, never null). `openapi.json` regenerated (+2 paths); `OpenApiContractTest` now asserts `/field-library` **and** `/form-templates` (closing the G9a in-suite gap). Seeder `PlatformFieldLibrarySeeder` upserts **6** platform questions (`Data\PlatformFieldLibraryItems`: full name, age, sex, phone, email, consent) on `pgsql_privileged`, idempotent on name, drift-guarded by `PlatformFieldLibrarySeedTest`; wired into `DatabaseSeeder` + `E2eSeeder`. Capital-`D` `Data` dir per the Linux PSR-4 gotcha.

**+24 tests → Pest 818 (0 fail, full Docker suite):** `FieldLibraryRlsTest` (RLS enabled+forced, no-author-global QueryException, read own+platform, no-update-global no-op), `FieldLibraryMapperTest` (fromField drops cross-field rules, toBlueprintField defaults, full round-trip), `FieldLibraryBuilderTest` (insert appends + usage bump for tenant AND **platform** via the privileged path, save-from-label, `childNotInDraft` guard), `FieldLibraryApiTest` (list omits jsonb, author 201, forbid read-token, reject bad field_type — uniquely-named `fieldLibraryAdmin()` helper), `PlatformFieldLibrarySeedTest` (6 validate+materialize + idempotent), `BlueprintValidatorTest` (XOR both/neither/exactly-one + validateField tolerates unresolved related_field_key), + `field-library-axe` Playwright spec. Host gates green: Pint, vue-tsc (app + SW), phpstan level-8 on changed files (`-d memory_limit=3G` — fixed 3 real errors: `findOrFail`→`whereKey`+`firstOrFail` for a single-model return, dropped a redundant `is_string`, `array_values(...)` for a `list` return), controller-gate, migration-lint. **`npm run build` + Vitest can't run on the Windows host** (only Linux `@rolldown` bindings + skipped bin-links) — CI-only; vue-tsc proved the TS/Vue compiles. Ships **ungated** (consistent with G7/G9a). Narrowing: no standalone "manage library" screen (the `is_active` toggle + rename-after-save are deferred). **MERGED — PR #44 `eb6bda7`, all 6 CI jobs green.** The E2E job took 3 test-only follow-ups: (1) a `/Full name/` `getByRole` matched 3 buttons because the seeded "Community Health Survey" already has a "Full name" field → scope the picker locators to `.library`; (2)+(3) the whole-page picker `assertClean` re-flagged a pre-existing borderline `target-size` on the toolbar's `.builder__back` at the tablet viewport (a surface builder-axe already gates at all 3 widths, yet passes) → **scope the new picker's axe scan to `.builder__pane--left`** rather than re-scan shared chrome (min-height on `.builder__back` was tried first and reverted as out-of-scope + ineffective). Lesson: a new interactive-surface axe spec should `.include()` only the NEW UI.

## 2026-07-20 — G10b1: grant + hierarchy write surface (backend + /api/v1) — MERGED, PR #46 `35a0d3a`, all 6 CI jobs green on the first push

Decomposed G10b into **G10b1 (backend + API)** / **G10b2 (UI)** with the user, alongside three other decisions taken up front: **one node per form** (keep the `forms.scope_node_id` pointer — a pivot would rewrite G10a's tested resolver core); **fix the over-broad form list in this increment**; and **one** new `manage:scopes` ability mapped to both `scopes.manage` and `forms.collaborators.manage`.

**`ScopeNodeService` gained `update`/`setActive`/`delete`/`move`/`deletionImpact`.** The keystone is `move()`, and the plan's original locking spec turned out to be insufficient: `FOR UPDATE` on node + target parent leaves a real hole, because a concurrent move of an **ancestor of the target** re-paths the target without locking it — the first mover then grafts its subtree onto a stale prefix, and `path` is exactly what authorization prefix-matches, so a corrupted path is a corrupted permission. Fixed with a **transaction-scoped advisory lock keyed on the tenant** (`pg_advisory_xact_lock(class, crc32(tenant))` — the two-int form rather than the undocumented internal `hashtext`; `_xact_` so a crashed request cannot strand it). It is the only advisory lock in the codebase — every other service serializes on a row (`forms`, `submissions`) — because it is the only operation whose correctness spans an unbounded row set rather than one aggregate root. The `FOR UPDATE` re-reads stay and still do work the advisory lock doesn't (authoritative post-lock rows; blocking a concurrent rename/deactivate of the two endpoints). Then: a **one-line cycle check** (`str_starts_with($target->path, $node->path)`, which covers move-onto-self because `path` is self-inclusive, and which is now the **only** thing preventing a longer cycle — `scope_nodes_no_self_parent` only ever covered the single-node case, so that migration's now-false comment was corrected); a depth cap measured across the **whole subtree** (a shallow node can carry a deep one); and a **single** `UPDATE` re-path over the constant-prefix `LIKE` — atomic (no window where a concurrent read sees a half-grafted tree), index-served by `(tenant_id, path)` under `COLLATE "C"`, one statement whatever the subtree size.

**`ScopeNode::booted()` was KEPT VERBATIM rather than relaxed.** The guard covers Eloquent *saves*; the re-path is a query-builder statement that fires no model events, so the invariant "no model save ever touches `parent_id`/`path`/`depth`" survives intact while the one audited writer sits behind the locks. An `unguarded`/re-entrancy flag was considered and rejected: it converts a structural invariant into something any future caller can switch off. The G10a test asserting model-level re-parenting throws is kept green as the proof, and a new test re-asserts it *after* `move()` ships.

**A genuine PostgreSQL trap, found by running the tests:** `substring(path from ?)` with an untyped bind parameter resolves to the POSIX-**regex** overload `substring(text, text)`, which returns NULL when the pattern doesn't match — it would have nulled `path` on every row it touched. The NOT NULL constraint turned it into a loud failure; fixed to `substr(path, ?::int)` with both numeric binds cast explicitly, and documented, because the same trap in a nullable column is silent data loss.

**New migration `2026_07_21_000001`** adds two CHECKs that make an incorrectly re-pathed row impossible *regardless of which writer produced it*: `scope_nodes_path_self_suffix_chk` (`path LIKE '%/' || id || '/'`) and `scope_nodes_depth_matches_path_chk` (`depth` = slashes minus 2; the design draft had minus 1, which would have rejected every valid row — verified against real values before writing it). The second also forces `path` and `depth` to be written in the same statement. Validated by `migrate:fresh` + the full suite, including `ScopeNodeScaleTest`'s hand-composed bulk rows.

**Node deletion now removes the subtree's grants in-transaction.** `resource_grants` is a morph with no FK, so nothing cascaded them and every node delete silently manufactured orphaned authorization rows. This is cleanup at the site that creates them, *not* the deferred orphan sweeper. Uses a SUBQUERY, not a `whereIn` over collected ids (PG caps binds at 65535; a PSGC region is ~44k nodes).

**New `ResourceGrantService`** — upserts so a demotion editor→reviewer **replaces** rather than accumulates (the unique key deliberately excludes `capacity`); re-resolves and **locks** the tenant-scoped target so concurrent grants cannot 23505-race and so `associate()` can never carry a cross-tenant morph pair; refuses `includes_descendants` on a form target ahead of the CHECK; `TODO(audits, Phase 1)` markers. **New `ResourceGrantPolicy`**, registered explicitly (an unmapped model class falls through to Gate closures — it fails **open**), carrying the escalation rules: base `forms.collaborators.manage`, **no self-grant** (self-*revoke* stays allowed — de-escalation), and **anti-amplification** via a new `ResourceCapacity::anyPermission()`. Rule 3 is **unreachable under the shipped 5-role matrix** (Owner/Admin are the only holders of the base permission and hold both `.any`s); that is stated in the policy docblock, in RBAC §8.4, and tested against directly-constructed permission subsets rather than seeded roles — specifically so it is not filed as dead code the way `forms.collaborators.manage` itself was for three increments.

**`ResourceGrantResolver::nodeReach()`** is the blast-radius counter, and it lives on the resolver rather than as a parallel query so the preview and the grant cannot disagree — it is the number an administrator makes an escalation decision on. Returns `{direct, descendant}`, reports **zero for a deactivated node or one under an inactive branch** (otherwise it advertises access the resolver will refuse to honour), and counts through the same `withTrashed()` scope the resolver itself uses. `escapeLike()` was made public so `ScopeNodeService` shares one escaping rule instead of copying an authorization-critical one. Also **documented and tested the `forget()` asymmetry**: the per-user variant clears only `$sets`, leaving `$formPaths`/`$inactive` stale, so `move`/`setActive`/`delete` must all call the argument-less `forget()` — a same-request-only failure of exactly the shape the G10a walkthrough caught.

**The form-list leak was fixed on BOTH surfaces.** A design pass surfaced that `GET /api/v1/forms` (`FormApiController::index`) had the *identical* "every non-archived tenant form to anyone passing the read gate" behaviour as `FormPresenter::list()` — fixing only the presenter would have moved the leak to the API rather than closed it. Both now scope via `grantedFormIdsQuery($user, ResourceCapacity::Editor)` when the viewer lacks `forms.edit.any` (Editor specifically: this is the authoring list, so a bare reviewer grant should not surface a row with every `can` flag false). All 230 pre-existing Form tests passed unchanged, plus a new 7-test `FormListScopingTest` covering both surfaces.

**API:** `manage:scopes` + 6 new paths — `/scopes`, `/scopes/{scopeNode}`, `/scopes/{scopeNode}/move` (deliberately separate from PATCH: a locked operation with its own refusals, and folding it in pushes the controller toward the complexity gate), `/scopes/{scopeNode}/impact`, `/resource-grants`, `/resource-grants/{resourceGrant}`. **Every route pairs `ability:` with a `can:` policy gate**, which is precisely what makes the single coarse ability safe — the ability map's any-of semantics would otherwise let a `scopes.manage`-only principal reach the grant routes. That constraint is recorded in `ApiAbilities::MANAGE_SCOPES` and api-spec §2.6 as a rule future routes must honour, and is asserted by a test that hand-mints a token carrying an ability its holder could not have requested (`createToken()` does not run `intersect()` — that happens in the mint controller) and shows the policy gate still refuses.

**+64 tests → Pest 966** across `ScopeNodeMoveTest` (13), `ScopeNodeMoveLockingTest` (6, deterministic lock-shape via `DB::listen`), `ScopeNodeLifecycleTest` (13), `ResourceGrantServiceTest` (13), `FormListScopingTest` (7), `ScopingApiTest` (12). Host gates: Pint green, controller-gate green, migration-lint green, **Larastan 0 errors on every G10b file**, vue-tsc green, Redocly valid, `openapi.json` regenerated (23 paths) with **no drift**. **The end-to-end walkthrough was run and all 18 checks passed** — a 4-level tree, a reviewer grant on an interior node with descendants, a subtree move between regions, an intermediate-node deactivation, the escalation refusals, and deletion impact — the exercise that in G10a found the bug the tests missed.

**Documented narrowing:** concurrent-move coverage is the deterministic pair (lock-shape + a sequential mutual-move test proving the decision is made on **re-read** state), not true two-session contention. `RefreshDatabase` wraps each test in an uncommitted transaction so a second connection cannot see the fixtures, and the suite has no committing-Feature-test precedent to follow; shipping a flaky race test was judged worse than shipping an honest narrowing. Ships **backend-only** — no `tenant.php` routes, no Vue, no Playwright, all G10b2.

## 2026-07-21 — G11: richer personalization (accent / text-size / dyslexia font) — PR #48, all 6 CI jobs green

The last increment of Phase 2, and mostly an exercise in *executing* a spec that already existed (PRD Feature #9, DSR §2.9, data-dictionary §19) — except that three of the things the spec and the tracker asserted turned out not to be true.

**The tracker's premise was wrong.** PROGRESS said "the columns already exist (C2 shipped `theme_mode` + `accent`), so this is not a schema increment." The only migration for `user_ui_preferences` shipped `theme_mode` alone; `accent` was a hardcoded `'blueprint'` literal in `User::uiTheme()` that had never touched the database. The claim traced back to the G-map plan's exploration notes, which had read data-dictionary §19 — the *target* schema — rather than the migration. General lesson worth keeping: **§-numbered docs describe intent; migrations describe reality.** Building on the stated premise would have skipped the migration and produced a feature that silently discarded every choice but theme.

**The shipped accent was broken, and structurally unobservable.** `[data-accent='teal']` had a blue hover (`--mds-primary-700`) on a teal button, no `-bg-active`/`-tint` override, and no dark variant at all — and because `[data-accent]` carries the same specificity as `[data-theme-mode='dark']` but sat later in source order, teal *won* in dark mode and painted light `#1B5E5E` on the `#123350` ground: **1.74:1** for both `action-primary-fg` and the focus ring, against minimums of 4.5 and 3. It survived two increments for a simple reason: **nothing in the product could set `data-accent`, and no test had ever set it.** The fix extended the ramp from one step to seven and verified all 17 resulting pairings independently; teal is never worse than Blueprint at the equivalent role and is better in dark (its hover clears 4.82:1 where Blueprint's fails at 3.96:1). The dark payload is now defined once as private `--mds-_accent-teal-dark-*` customs that both dark selectors assign from — the neutral dark block is *already* copy-pasted across the explicit and `prefers-color-scheme` sites, and adding a third was the obvious future divergence.

**The documented type-scale rule could not ship.** §2.9 specified "+2px per level, never changing line-heights". Holding line-height constant while growing font-size doesn't preserve a ratio, it destroys one: at extra_large that yields `body-sm` as 17px text in an 18px line box (1.06) and `caption` at 16-in-16 (1.00) — clipping descenders and failing WCAG 1.4.12. An accessibility feature shipping an accessibility regression. Replaced (user decision) with proportional ×1.125/×1.25 scaling of both properties, font-size rounded and line-height then *derived* from the role's original ratio — independent rounding would have compressed `caption` from 1.333 to 1.286. §2.9 was rewritten with the authoritative table and the reasoning.

**What the plan got right, and it mattered:** the reflow risk looked like the hardest part and wasn't. `.app-shell__content` is `overflow-y: auto`, which makes the other axis `auto` too, so it is its own scroll container and page content never reaches the document overflow chain the E2E measures. Only `TopNav` was exposed — ~344 of 375px at extra_large, about one long tenant name from failing. Hardened, plus an `overflow-x: clip` belt on `.app-shell` that makes document-level horizontal overflow structurally impossible.

**Restraint that paid for itself:** zero touches to `packages/design-system/src/components/**`. The accent picker is a label-only segmented control — a colour swatch would be a colour-*only* signifier (WCAG 1.4.1), and the control already previews the accent for free because its selected chip is painted with `--mds-color-action-primary-bg`. The dyslexia toggle reuses `MdsCheckbox` rather than building the specified-but-unbuilt `MdsSwitch`, whose own DSR row requires visible text anyway — i.e. a checkbox with extra steps. That kept the DSR §1.2 governance gate and the flakiest CI job out of the diff entirely. `ThemeQuickToggle.vue`'s diff is literally zero lines: `useThemePreference()` survives at its exact C2 signature as a mode-only facade.

**The anti-clobber guarantee is server-side.** Every rule on `UpdateAppearanceRequest` is `sometimes`, so an absent key is never validated and never reaches `updateOrCreate` — the quick toggle's `{theme_mode}`-only PATCH cannot wipe the other three axes even from a stale client. Verified by mutation: making one column write unconditional fails the partial-PATCH test exactly where expected.

**Four new guard suites make the hand-authored CSS mechanically checkable** — `type-scale` re-derives all 44 declarations from the token JSON, `theme-overrides` asserts the duplicated dark blocks stay identical (catching the pre-existing neutral copy-paste risk as a bonus), `token-references` resolves every `var(--mds-*)` in the tree, and `useTheme` pins the "default = absence of the attribute" convention. The dead-token sweep folded in at the user's request found **16** undefined tokens across 10 files, not the 2 the tracker had flagged — and it was load-bearing rather than cosmetic: the ones without fallbacks were invalid at computed-value time and inherited instead, so they'd have scaled by inheritance rather than by type role under `data-font-size`. Sites *with* fallbacks were collapsed to the fallback actually rendering, since re-pointing them to the intended colour is a design change with contrast implications, not a cleanup.

**The E2E found a real bug that no unit test would have — and it was not in G11's code.** Running the new spec locally at 834px, `extra_large` plus the dyslexia face gave the page **50px of genuine horizontal scroll** (`window.scrollTo(500,0)` really moved it). Each axis alone was fine; only the combination reached it. The diagnosis was the interesting part, because two plausible assumptions were both wrong: the `overflow-x: clip` belt on `.app-shell` made **zero** difference (identical with `visible` and `hidden`), and `body.scrollWidth` was a clean 834 while only `html.scrollWidth` read 884 — every overflowing element sat correctly inside the table's own `overflow-x: auto` wrapper. The culprit was `.mds-table__sr`, the visually-hidden "Actions" label: `position: absolute` inside a `position: static` scroll wrapper, so its containing block resolved *outside* that wrapper and no amount of ancestor `overflow` could clip it — an absolutely positioned box is clipped by its containing block, not by whichever ancestor happens to scroll. A 1px span past the last column was extending the document's scrollable width. One line (`position: relative` on `.mds-table__scroll`) fixed it. It is a latent `MdsDataTable` bug that personalization only made reachable by widening tables, so it was fixed at source rather than papered over per page — and it is the increment's single touch to `src/components/**`, which the DSR §1.2 governance gate accepts because the DSR is updated in the same commit. Locally verified afterwards: **114 E2E runs green** (18 personalization + 60 responsive + 36 public-runtime/templates).

**OpenDyslexic is the system's first webfont** and costs nothing on the default path: browsers fetch a `@font-face` src only when a rule using the family matches, and the only such rule is attribute-gated, so a user who never opts in downloads zero font bytes. It lives in a new `fonts.css` imported only by the admin app — putting it in `theme-overrides.css` would have shipped it to the guest bundle, which imports that file.

Gates: Pest **1001** (+13), Vitest **457** (+43), Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint green, PHPStan **0 on every G11 file**, vue-tsc clean, no `openapi.json` drift.

**CI needed two follow-up fixes, and both were harness-shaped — the two jobs actually feared passed first time.** `design-system-a11y` (story attribute leakage across the shared preview iframe) and `e2e` (the font-loading race and 375×extra_large reflow) were called out as the top risks and both went green on the first run, so the mitigations were the right ones. What broke instead:

1. `token-references.test.ts` threw `ERR_INVALID_URL_SCHEME` on CI while passing locally. The suites run under `environment: 'happy-dom'`, whose DOM `URL` global shadows Node's, and resolving a five-level **directory** URL (trailing slash) through it does not preserve the `file:` scheme — its two sibling suites use the identical `import.meta.url` pattern and passed, because they resolve concrete **file** paths. Switched to `process.cwd()`, which under Vitest is its config file's directory.
2. The new guest-shell test threw `Vite manifest not found`. It is the only test in `GuestRuntimeTest` that renders the shell rather than hitting a JSON endpoint, hence the only one evaluating `@vite`, and the Pest job builds no assets. It had passed locally purely because assets happened to be built — a silent local/CI divergence. `withoutVite()` (the existing convention), verified by deliberately moving `public/build/manifest.json` aside to reproduce the CI condition rather than trusting the fix.

Both are now recorded as standing gotchas.
