## 2026-07-21 — G10b2: the scoping hierarchy UI (MERGED, PR #47 `ae14bc2`) — G10 COMPLETE

The UI half of G10b, over the backend merged in G10b1 — and the increment that finally makes the Reviewer role assignable by a human: build a hierarchy, assign forms to it, grant a teammate access to a whole branch. **Two user decisions up front:** ship as ONE PR (the backend carries every security-critical decision and is already CI-proven, so the risk is review size, not correctness); and the form→scope picker **requires `scopes.manage`**.

**Designed with a 12-agent workflow** (3 competing architectures → 3 deep dives → a verifying judge → 4 adversarial critics). The judge verified 35 load-bearing claims against real source — `axe-core` 4.12.1, the `@inertiajs` dist, `@vue/runtime-dom`, Laravel's exception handler — and **five were false**, three of them in the direction that would have shipped a bug: `nested-interactive` cannot fire on a `treeitem` (its matcher keys on `childrenPresentational`, which `treeitem` lacks), so one agent's "blocking finding" had deleted the drag grip and rewritten both keyboard specs against a design built on a non-sequitur; `vue-tsc` does accept custom properties in an object `:style` (`CSSProperties` declares `` [v: `--${string}`] ``); and Vue does **not** strip a `false` aria attribute, so the omit-on-leaves rule was right for a reason nobody had stated correctly.

**The merge-blocker came from an adversarial critic, not the design pass:** axe's `getOwnedRoles` pushes any child carrying a role, a **global `aria-*` attribute**, or focusability as an *owned* child of `role="tree"` rather than recursing past it — so the assertive live region and the empty state had to be **siblings** of the `<ul>`, and the scan target became `.scopes__pane`, not the `.scopes__tree` PROGRESS had specified. A second critic inverted the transport reasoning: `preserveState: true` is **already the default** for post/patch/delete, so hardening mutations was a no-op while the genuinely unsafe path — a GET visit for selection — would have remounted the page, collapsed the tree and dropped focus to `<body>` on the page's most common interaction. Selection became local state.

**Transport is forced, not stylistic.** `bootstrap/app.php`'s `$isApi` keys on the `api/v1/*` **path**, and Laravel runs custom render callbacks before JSON negotiation, so a refused move on a web route returns a 302 with a flash toast **even to an XHR** — `builderClient` would see `response.ok`, skip its guarded branch, and throw a raw `SyntaxError` parsing HTML. Every mutation is therefore an Inertia visit; the only fetches are the two read-only sidecars.

**Two real bugs the E2E found that no unit test would have.** Grab-mode entered from the detail panel's "Move…" focuses the *row*, whose keydown was still doing tree navigation — so the move could be started but never aimed. And the outcome was announced from `onSuccess`, but a no-op or refused move returns props identical to what is already rendered, so the reference watcher never fired: the announcement went silent **and the tree stayed stuck in grab mode**. Settled from the refreshed props with an `onFinish` backstop. Both were only reachable by driving the real widget.

**A day lost to a stale Vite dev server** is worth recording: the container's `npm run dev` does not see host file edits through the WSL2 bind mount, so it served a cached module and three separate "bugs" were phantoms. Stop the `node` service, delete `public/hot`, and build inside the container (`docker compose run --rm --no-deps node npm run build`) — which is what CI does anyway.

**Gates:** Pest **988 (+22)**, Vitest **414 unchanged**, Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint green, PHPStan **0 errors on all G10b2 files**, vue-tsc clean, **no `openapi.json` drift** (tenant-web routes are outside the surface). New `scopes-axe` **24/24 across all three viewports**; `/scopes` added to `responsive-axe` (6/6). `E2eSeeder` gains a **5-level** hierarchy — deliberately deeper than the minimum, because a shallow fixture leaves the 375px overflow case unreachable and makes `responsive-axe` a false negative — plus forms at differing depths, a deactivated branch, a second **ACTIVE** member and a descendant-inclusive reviewer grant.

**Local-environment caveat, verified not a regression:** `builder-axe` fails locally with `color-contrast` in a theme/viewport combination that changes between runs, and it fails **on `main` at `ca92379` too** (checked out and re-run to confirm). CI is green on main; this is a local rendering artifact.

**Narrowings:** the tree ships whole, capped at 2,000 nodes with an honest notice (expand-on-demand is the follow-up for a ~44k-node PSGC shape); grants are managed per node only. Found and deliberately **not** folded in: `--mds-color-text-muted` and `--mds-type-heading-sm-*` do not exist and are referenced at 4+ live sites.

**Merged with all 6 CI jobs green, after two fixes for failures this branch did not cause.** `composer audit` broke on three guzzle advisories published the same day (`<7.15.1`: host-only cookie scope, unbounded response cookies, Proxy-Authorization forwarded to origin) — bumped 7.13.2 → 7.15.1, transitive via `laravel/framework`, no constraint change. `npm audit --audit-level=high` then broke on `brace-expansion` and `shell-quote` DoS advisories reachable only through `@storybook/test-runner`; plain `npm audit fix` cleared both, and the 8 remaining moderates sit below the gate.

The Pest failure was the **known-flaky tampered-token test**, and it was worth fixing rather than re-running: `GuestShareTokenServiceTest::tamperSegment` flipped the LAST base64url character while calling itself deterministic, but a 32-byte HMAC is 42.7 six-bit groups, so the trailing character carries 2 padding bits and several distinct trailing characters decode to the same raw bytes — the "tamper" could leave the signature intact. It flaked once locally and once on CI the same day. `GuestRuntimeTest` already carried the first-character fix for exactly this reason; the unit helper had been missed. **The repo now has no known flaky test**, and that gotcha entry has been retired.

**E2E passing on CI is also what settled the local `builder-axe` `color-contrast` failures as environmental** — they reproduce on `main` at `ca92379` and not on CI's Linux runner.

## 2026-07-21 — G11 MERGED (PR #48 `506ff97`) — Increment G and PHASE 2 COMPLETE; Track B (Windows Server host) deferred by user decision

`gh pr merge 48 --squash --delete-branch` against head `7fe07be`, all 6 CI jobs green. `main` fast-forwarded `2c9bede..506ff97`; branch `g11-personalization` deleted. 56 files, +2349/−144. **With it Increment G (G1→G11) and Phase 2 (Kobo-style rigor + offline) are COMPLETE** — the roadmap table's Phase-2 row is now ✅ and Phase 3 is marked ⏭️ NEXT.

**One process note worth keeping:** head at merge time was `7fe07be`, a docs-only PROGRESS.md commit pushed after the run PROGRESS recorded as green (`de599f2`/`1603c6b`). That commit triggered a *fresh* CI run which was still in-flight when the session opened, so `mergeStateStatus` read `UNSTABLE` and three jobs read `pending`. Waited for it rather than merging on the older green run or forcing it — the standing "CI green is the source of truth" rule means green **on the commit you are merging**, and a docs-only diff is not a licence to skip that. It went green in ~8 minutes (E2E is the long pole at 7m34s).

**Track B — stand up the Windows Server host (`docs/deployment-infrastructure.md` §8) — DEFERRED until app development is done (user decision).** Asked whether §8 targeted the Server 2016 box or this Windows 11 Laragon dev machine, since this session has no shell on any server and §8 is install-software-on-a-real-host work; the user's answer was to postpone it entirely rather than pick a target. Recorded in PROGRESS.md's "Next Session" section, and the "Alternative track" line — which had offered Track B at the top of every session — was rewritten so it is no longer surfaced as an option. **Nothing is blocked:** the deploy *pipeline* has been committed and dormant since 2026-07-04 — `deploy.ps1` at the repo root plus `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, whose `if:` requires the repo variable `DEPLOY_ENABLED == 'true'` (never set), so it skips on every green `main` today. §8 is only the one-time box topology. Two things to carry forward: work §8 steps 1–8 (app stack) before 9–10 (self-hosted runner + `DEPLOY_ENABLED`), because step 9 is precisely what arms automatic production deploys; and ADR-0005 puts Windows Server 2016 end-of-support at ~Jan 2027, so a long deferral likely converts this into a newer-Server-OS task.

**Next: decompose Phase 3 into the H-map** — deliberately decompose, not build. Phase 3 is one roadmap bullet (visual workflow builder, piping, save-and-resume, payments, integrations, custom domains, analytics, OCR); payments, OCR and custom domains each carry third-party/infrastructure decisions that want an ADR before an increment, the same gap that made G10 hard.

## 2026-07-21 — H1a: ADR-0007 (async execution substrate) + 12 doc reconciliations — MERGED (PR #49 `0d9df25`), docs-only, PHASE 3 BUILDING STARTS

**Phase 3's entry point, on the G5a/ADR-0006 docs-only precedent (PR #32 `d2a3ff7`): retire a cross-cutting design fork in a reviewable document before any code depends on it.** Six of Phase 3's twelve verticals dispatch jobs and every rollup/reaper/sweep in the phase is cross-tenant — a pattern with no precedent in the codebase — so the async contract had to exist before H2. The audit (three Explore agents, every `file:line` re-read against source) found the foundation is **almost entirely prose**: exactly one queued job (`ScanAttachmentJob`, `final`, defining none of `$queue`/`$tries`/`$backoff`/`$timeout`/`failed()`/`middleware()`); no worker or scheduler service anywhere (the only committed worker invocation is `queue:listen` in `composer.json`'s `dev` script); no scheduler at all (`routes/console.php` is 8 stock lines, no `withSchedule`, no `app/Console/`) against **four documents asserting one**, most bluntly `feature-backlog.md:85`'s *"scheduler + email + async export already exists"*; and `onQueue(` with **zero hits** against a five-name queue catalog documented as binding in two places.

**The keystone: a live defect two documents called "Mitigated".** `TenantContext::$tenantId` is a process-lifetime PHP static; `applyLocal()` scopes only the *database* GUC to the transaction and `set()` assigns the static unconditionally, with nothing rolling it back — and there is no `Queue::before`/`JobProcessing` listener anywhere. On a long-lived `queue:work` worker the GUC therefore goes NULL while the static still holds the previous tenant, so `BelongsToTenant` injects it into the next context-less job's reads and auto-fills it on `creating`. RLS still fails closed, so the practical effect is zero-row reads and confusing `QueryException`s rather than cross-tenant disclosure — but ADR-0002:125 and security-threat-model:40 both promised *"fails fast (explicit exception)"* and it did not, and it escaped notice only because no second job type exists. ADR-0007 §D4 closes it; both documents were corrected to cite the mechanism rather than assert an unbuilt one.

**ADR-0007 is `D1…D13` and deliberately departs from ADR-0006's skeleton (user decision): no weighted scorecard.** ADR-0006 scored a *spike* with measurements; this ADR has none, and §D9's rate ceilings are explicitly unvalidated planning assumptions on a queue nothing has ever consumed — a rubric would have manufactured false precision. It uses ADR-0005's plain drivers + Concern/Choice table with ADR-0006's `**DN — …**` bullets and `**Method note.**`, which states plainly which parts are audited evidence and which are reasoning. **D1** is the `database` driver on Postgres with a plain `queue:work`, **superseding ADR-0005 rows 27–28 in part** — the repo's first *row-level* supersession, chosen over amendment because it is the honest description and avoids retiring an otherwise-live ADR (the whole-ADR precedent, ADR-0005→ADR-0003, was followed for wording across title/Status/Related/References). Redis+Horizon is named as a non-blocking upgrade path with an explicit revisit trigger, not a rejection. **D8** decides `after_commit => true` here but implements it in H2, resolving the tension between the docs-only precedent and not specifying a rule the ADR leaves broken — it closes a real bug where `AttachmentStorageService:82` dispatches outside any transaction, letting a worker beat the row and strand `virus_scan_status` at `pending` forever, structurally untestable under `sync` and therefore under every current CI job. **D10** refuses a fairness table because both escapes are worse than the problem (RLS in the job-reservation hot path is a self-inflicted R9; an `EXEMPT_TABLES` entry repeats the `failed_jobs` hazard). **D12** records a constraint no document stated: any tenant-facing failure surface is itself a cross-tenant read of an RLS-free table, sanctioned only via `pgsql_superadmin` + `superadmin_bypass`.

**Verification caught six citation defects in the source plan before they were written into the corpus** — `handle()` is a `function (): void {}` closure, not `fn() =>` (syntactically impossible for a statement list); "no guzzle / no Redis" was false (guzzle is transitive at `composer.lock:802`, Redis exists in infra) and was narrowed to *no first-party outbound call site* / *no Redis-backed queue in use*; `horizon:terminate` is **not in `deploy.ps1`** at all, only in `deployment-infrastructure.md:43`, so that correction stood but inverted; migration-lint's Rule 1 is `:105-117`, not `:99`; `TenantInvitationNotification` interpolates at `:33-35`, not `:44`; and the webhook retry ladder lives at §1 `:13`, not §4 `:49-61`, so the unbuilt banner was retargeted to the doc head (user-approved). **Two H-map claims were refuted and deliberately not written:** the *"RBAC docs say a catalog of 27 permissions"* correction is wrong — `multi-tenancy-rbac-design.md` §5 already enumerates 28 and `:112` names `scopes.manage` as the 28th, while §3's "fixed catalog" refers to the five **roles** — so writing it would have *introduced* an error, and the R6 dangling-pointer fix was substituted as the tenth reconciliation; and the *"PRD lists 8 roadmap items"* claim is unfounded (the 12 is verbatim at `PRD.md:405`; the 8 came from PROGRESS.md itself). **Two further defects found during verification were folded in:** ADR-0002:76's dangling `[D7]` anchor (the section is D6) and technical-architecture §5 layer 5, which described a "job middleware" that was never built.

**R6 is now RESOLVED** following the R7/R11 strike-through precedent. It had carried three mutually contradictory commitments: a mitigation citing §7.4, which is webhook reliability and contains no rate-limit mechanism (a dangling pointer); a §5 claim that limiter buckets were already per-tenant, untrue of the HTTP limiters as built (`AppServiceProvider:105-127` keys on token-hash/IP, deliberately, since `throttle:api` sorts ahead of auth); and an NFR deferral of per-tenant limiting to "Phase 2–3 … not before". All three are corrected, and §D9 replaces them with job middleware counting executions rather than requests.

**Next: H2** — the async substrate proper, with ADR-0007 as its specification. The single highest-value test is one integration test driving a real `queue:work --once` under `QUEUE_CONNECTION=database`: it is the only way to exercise serialize → enqueue → reserve → unserialize → context-re-entry, the path `sync` structurally cannot reach, and the only thing that would catch a regression in §D2/§D4/§D5 or the §D8 dispatch race.

## 2026-07-23 — H5b (entitlement enforcement)

H5a merged (squash → PR #54 `5e3ea4e`); H5b built on `h5b-entitlement-enforcement`. Made the H5a read-model *bite* along ADR-0008 §D4's three classes. **Enforcement classifier is now code** (`EnforcementMode` enum + `UsageMetric::enforcementMode()`/`isGauge()`, promoted from the H5a docblock; `values()` untouched). **Gauges (forms/storage/seats) enforced by a LIVE COUNT/SUM under RLS, never a running aggregate** — the H12a `max_responses` precedent + the G10a/H2 "synced counter is a landmine" lesson (gauges decrease on archive/delete/remove): `EntitlementService::countGauge()` (non-memoized, the guard's read) + `liveGauge()` (memoized, read-model); `usage()` branches gauge→live/flow→counter. `QuotaGuard::assertCanCreate` throws `QuotaExceededException` (402, metric code, {limit,used}); null quota (unlimited/off-tenant/unseeded) never blocks — so the pre-H5b suite (no plans) is inert. Sites: `FormService::create` (covers template-instantiate transitively), `AttachmentStorageService::store` (**staff only — guest respondent uploads never blocked**, same choke point), `TenantMembershipService::invite` (**reserve-on-invite** = Active+Invited). **Never-block `submissions_count`**: a synchronous, **auto-discovered** `MeterSubmissionUsage` listener on post-commit `SubmissionCreated` → `UsageMeter` (`ON CONFLICT` upsert, swallow-and-logged). Exports metered at `SubmissionExporter::stream`; api_requests via a new `MeterApiUsage` middleware (meter only — the monthly 429 is H5c). **Cross-tenant rollup**: `RollUpUsageCountersJob` (MaintenanceJob) fans out `ReconcileTenantUsageJob` per active tenant — reconciles submissions_count from source (never-block self-heal), materializes gauges + stamps limit_snapshot, sends the `QuotaOverageNotification` upsell once on crossing; `dailyAt('02:40')`. **Rate-limit = forward seam** (matrix §4 uniform ceilings; §D9 tier hook left for Phase 4). **Decisions this session:** reserve-on-invite seats; staff-only storage block (guest exempt); rate-limit forward-seam-only + defer api 429 to H5c; one combined PR. **Build finding:** Laravel 11+ auto-discovers `app/Listeners`, so a manual `Event::listen` double-counted — removed it. Gates: Pest +37 across 7 files (rollup is a committing `workOneJob` test); PHPStan L8 zero on every new file; no migration; no openapi drift. Narrowing: feature-gating + api/webhook monthly-quota 429 + the grandfather override storage are **H5c**.

**Next: H5c** — retro-gate the ungated Phase-2 features (xlsform/offline/templates/field-library/api_access) behind the grandfather override (`legacyOverrides()` + merge-day backfill), and enforce the api_requests/webhook_deliveries monthly quotas as 429s.

## 2026-07-23 — H9a (server-side draft substrate)

H5c merged (squash → PR #56 `a65437d`; entitlement track COMPLETE); H9a built on `h9a-draft-substrate`. The H-map's next move: the server-side DRAFT substrate shared by save-and-resume (H10) + OCR (H18/H19). Backend-only — no route, no feature gate, no openapi change; the suite stays inert. **The linchpin:** a draft is written repeatedly under one `client_submission_uuid` with changing content, so the pipeline's same-uuid-different-content **409 is SUSPENDED for drafts and RE-ARMED on promotion.** New **`SubmissionDraftService`** (singleton seam, not only a route — an OCR job stages a draft with no guest request/uuid): **`saveDraft()`** runs Stage-1 normalize + the Stage-2a published guard, SKIPS Stage-3 + the 409, find-or-creates by uuid (first save creates a `status=draft` row stamping `completeness_percent`/`last_saved_at`/`draft_expires_at=now()+30d`; later saves OVERWRITE in place under `lockForUpdate` + a `status===Draft` re-check; a 23505 create-race folds into update = last-writer-wins). **`promote()`** runs the FULL Stage-3 EXACTLY ONCE (before any txn, so a failure leaves the draft resumable), flips the SAME row `draft→submitted` in place (stable `submissions.id` for H9b's resume token; source preserved; `draft_expires_at` nulled), and fires `SubmissionCreated` once (so H5b metering counts a draft only on promotion). **Re-arm is automatic** — the finalized row stores `AnswersContentChecksum::of($normalizedDraftAnswers)`, byte-identical to `submit()`, so a later same-uuid replay is the ordinary 200/409. **Stage-4 tail extracted into `SubmissionFinalizer`** (media re-point + `submission_answers` upsert + typed/geo index + `created` audit), reused by BOTH `submit()` and `promote()` — **`submit()` stays byte-identical** (proven by `SubmissionPipelineTest` passing unmodified); `create`→`updateOrCreate` for the answer doc. Alter-only migration adds the three per-SUBMISSION columns (no RLS re-emit, no status CHECK, migration-lint skips it). New pure **`DraftCompleteness`** (relevance-unaware %; excludes note/page_break/calculated/hidden; repeat section = one unit; div-0→0). **Decisions:** in-place promotion; stamp-once expiry; preserve source; double-promote = idempotent no-op; leave `submit()` byte-identical (a same-uuid finalize goes through `promote()`, never `submit()`). Gates: Pest +17 (`SubmissionDraftServiceTest` 11 incl. the HEADLINE + `DraftCompletenessTest` 6) with `SubmissionPipelineTest`/G4/G5/G6/`AuditSitesTest` regression (80) green unmodified; PHPStan L8 at the 77 local baseline (none in new files); Pint/migration-lint/controller-gate/job-payload-lint green; no openapi drift. Narrowings → H9b/H10: resume token + pre-RLS verify middleware + Group-C/B routes + draft-expiry reaper (needs H3, should hard-delete for the softDeletes×partial-unique-index edge) + tenant-configurable 30-day setting; H12a's `max_responses` COUNT + close predicate must filter `status != 'draft'`.

**Next: H9b** — the resume token (extends `GuestShareToken`, scoped to the draft `submissions.id`) + pre-RLS verify middleware + Group-C draft upsert/resume + a Group-B admin-draft route (OCR) + the draft-expiry reaper (`MaintenanceJob`, TTL from the 30-day default); needs H3.

## 2026-07-23 — H9b (save-and-resume surface over the H9a draft substrate)

H9a merged (squash → PR #57 `bfbeab5`); H9b built on `h9b-resume-token` (PR #58). Makes the inert H9a substrate reachable. Scope confirmed against the H-map (row 230) + three decisions locked with the user: **gate the guest draft routes now** (save_and_resume Starter+), **the Group-B admin-draft route = the encoder promote/confirm** endpoint, **send the resume email in H9b** (H3). **(1) Resume token** — extends `GuestShareToken`/`GuestShareTokenService` (still stateless HMAC, no DB row) with a `sid` claim + a `typ:'resume'` discriminator, signed with a **second domain-separated key** (`guest-resume-token.v1`; `config/guest.resume_token`, 30-day TTL) so share↔resume are non-interchangeable at BOTH the key layer and the claim layer (`mintResume()`/`verifyResume()`; `verify()` rejects a resume-typed payload). **(2) Pre-RLS `EstablishGuestDraftContext`** (the `EstablishGuestTenantContext` sibling) verifies the resume token → `TenantContext::apply` → `forget()` in `terminate()`. **(3) Group-C** (`api/v1/public`): `POST f/{shareToken}/draft` (`GuestDraftController` → `saveDraft`; mints+returns a resume token; on `finish_later`+contact-email queues the resume email) + `GET drafts/{resumeToken}` (`GuestDraftResumeController` → the draft's answers + a fresh short-lived share token, so a resumed device drives the ordinary schema/save/submit endpoints — **no resume-token-keyed write routes**); both gated `feature:save_and_resume`. `POST f/{shareToken}/submissions` is now **draft-aware**: an existing draft for the uuid is `saveDraft`-then-`promote`d, honouring the H9a "same-uuid finalize goes through `promote()`, never `submit()`" invariant (skips `form_updated` on that branch; promote() runs its own version guard). Thin web shell `GET /f/resume/{resumeToken}` (`GuestFormController@resume`) opens the SPA with a fresh share token + `data-resume-token` (link works now; the answer-restore UX is H10). **(4) Group-B** `POST submissions/{submission}/promote` (`SubmissionPromoteController`, `ability:write:submissions` + new `SubmissionPolicy::promote`) records the acting encoder — the OCR review-and-confirm seam. **(5) Resume email** = a queued `ResumeLinkNotification` (`#[Queue(Mail)]`, scalar payload, on-demand notifiable — the `TenantInvitationNotification` pattern; H3's first product consumer; in job-payload-lint `EXEMPT_JOBS`). **(6) Draft-expiry reaper** = cross-tenant `ReapExpiredDraftsJob` (`MaintenanceJob`, fans out) + `ReapTenantDraftsJob` (`TenantAwareJob`, **`forceDelete()`s** expired drafts under RLS — HARD-delete so the partial-unique index frees the re-used uuid; `submission_answers` cascades), scheduled `dailyAt('03:40')`; a partial index `submissions (tenant_id, draft_expires_at) WHERE status='draft'` (alter-only migration `…000007`). **(7)** `SubmissionConflictException::draftAlreadyFinalized()` now carries a distinct `draft_already_finalized` 409 code (`code()`; `contentConflict()` keeps `submission_conflict`). Gates: **Pest 1228 all-pass** (+~26: token resume/cross-type ×5, `GuestDraftRuntimeTest` 13, `SubmissionPromoteApiTest` 4, `DraftReaperTest` 2 committing via `workOneJob`, schedule +1 cron, OpenApi +3 paths); PHPStan L8 back to the 77 local baseline (two build-fixes: annotated `Submission::$guest_contact_email`, `firstOrFail()` for the resume-URL host); Pint/controller-gate/job-payload-lint/migration-lint green; openapi.json regenerated, no drift. Narrowings → H10/H12: draft-vs-superseded-version grace window → H1f/H12a (a draft on a superseded version fails promote with `submission_version_superseded`); tenant-configurable expiry surface + per-form toggle + SPA restore UX (welcome-back banner, Dexie↔server reconciliation) → H10; reaped-draft attachment GC → a future increment.

**Next: H10** — save-and-resume UX ("Save and finish later" + welcome-back banner + completeness display, the tenant-configurable-expiry setting surface, two-tier Dexie↔server reconciliation, admin draft filter, honours the H12 draft-vs-close rule on restore); needs H9b + H5b; `save_and_resume` gate Starter+.

own-forms via `grantedFormIdsQuery` for Editor/Reviewer; Members withheld — `null` ⇒ tile omitted — from form-scoped roles). **(3)** Thin single-action **`DashboardController@__invoke`**; the route swaps closure→controller and stays **ungated** (every role lands on the dashboard; scoping is the service's, not the route's). **(4)** New **`MdsStatTile`** DS primitive (tokenized card surface + icon badge + value + label — lifts the hand-rolled `.dash__stat` CSS out of the page, rule #2) + a Storybook story (axe-scanned) + a Vitest mount test; exported from the DS barrel. **(5)** `Dashboard.vue` `defineProps` the `kpis`, renders `MdsStatTile` tiles (Members only when non-null, `toLocaleString`'d), turns both disabled buttons into `router.visit('/forms')` actions gated on the shared `auth.can.manageForms` (hidden, never dead-disabled), and shows the empty state only when `forms === 0`. **Gates: Pest 1241 all-pass (+4)** — `DashboardMetricsServiceTest` (org-wide counts w/ archived-form + draft-submission exclusion; form-editor own-forms scope + null members) + `DashboardKpisTest` (route prop-shape, both roles); Vitest +2 (`StatTile`); PHPStan L8 0 on new files; Pint/controller-gate/migration-lint/job-payload-lint green; vue-tsc clean; Vite build green; **no migration, no new job, no `/api/v1` route → no openapi drift**. **Standing lesson (re)learned:** `php -d memory_limit=… artisan test` does NOT lift the runner's limit — `artisan test` spawns a child php process that keeps the container's 128M default and OOMs the full suite; run **`php -d memory_limit=-1 vendor/bin/pest`** directly. Narrowings: no time-window/trend metric (needs a `submitted_at` index + a chart primitive) — all-time counts only; materialized rollup deferred; no client Page-mount test (route-level Pest covers prop→render).

**Supply-chain sidebar (PR #61 `7154d5f`, merged before H11):** H11's own code was green on every gate but the repo-wide `npm audit --audit-level=high` step went red overnight from two freshly-published advisories (unrelated to H11; package-lock untouched, H10 was green the day before). Fixed on a separate branch: the one **production-reachable** high (postcss `GHSA-r28c-9q8g-f849`, via vite/@vue/compiler-sfc) is pinned by a `package.json` **override** (`^8.5.23`); the remaining highs are a **dev-only brace-expansion DoS** (`GHSA-mh99-v99m-4gvg`) whose last two copies are **override-immune** — one is a *bundled* dependency inside `@bundled-es-modules/glob` (pulled by `style-dictionary`), which npm `overrides` cannot reach. Rather than a breaking `npm audit fix --force` (which downgrades `@storybook/test-runner` + the token-build chain and risks the a11y/e2e jobs for a non-shippable DoS), the **CI audit gate was narrowed to production deps: `npm audit --omit=dev --audit-level=high`** (proven 0 vulns). Two decisions were locked with the user here (separate remediation PR; narrow-the-gate over force-bump). **Lessons:** (a) `-d memory_limit=…` / a `… | tail` pipe both hide the real exit code — check `$?` before the pipe; (b) npm overrides silently skip *bundled* deps; (c) the audit gate is intentionally prod-scoped now — don't revert without the `--force` bump it avoids.

`responsive-axe.spec.ts` adds `/webhooks` + a webhook-detail interaction scan. **Gates — verified locally via the Docker containers (the host CLI can't: no `pdo_pgsql`, DB host = the CI `postgres` compose service, and Vitest/Vite hit a missing `rolldown` win32 native binding):** Pint, PHPStan L8 = 0 on the 7 touched app/db/routes files, controller-gate/migration-lint/job-payload-lint, vue-tsc BOTH tsconfigs (host); then in-container **Pest `docker exec …-app-1 … pest tests/Feature/Webhooks/*` = 19/19 (126 assertions)**, **Vitest `…-node-1 npx vitest run` = 489/490** (the 1 fail = `token-references.test.ts` timing out under full-suite happy-dom load — passes in isolation, pre-existing flake), **`npm run build` (Vite) green**. **Only Storybook axe + Playwright responsive-axe were not run locally** (CI will) — the Badge stories reuse existing axe-clean variants and the e2e additions follow the seeded-fixture + interaction pattern H12b established. **Shipped later the same session: branch `h14-webhook-management-ui` → PR #69 → 6/6 CI green on the FIRST run → squash-merged `c5be98f`** (the code commit deliberately held back `PROGRESS.md`/`PROGRESS_ARCHIVE.md` so the merge state could be written afterwards, matching the `docs(progress):` pattern of `3bd69fa`/`70171a7`). **Two pre-emptive responsive fixes made the difference and are the session's lesson:** a pre-push read of the 375px budget caught that the shell's shared `.page-header__actions` was `display:flex` with **no `flex-wrap`** while the H14 detail page puts FIVE buttons in that slot (the H12b `.builder__actions` failure, generalized to the shared shell), and that the list's `.webhooks__url` `max-width: 22rem` (352px) exceeds the ~343px the DataTable's card-mode cell gets at 375px. Both fixed in the same commit (`flex-wrap: wrap`; `max-width: min(22rem, 100%)`), so the never-locally-run Storybook-axe and Playwright-responsive-axe suites passed without a red cycle. **Two hazards checked and cleared before push rather than by CI:** `E2eSeeder::seedWebhooks()` uses unresolvable `*.example.com` URLs but `WebhookEndpointService::create()` never invokes `OutboundUrlGuard` (SSRF lives in the `PublicHttpUrl` FormRequest rule + `DeliverWebhookJob`), so seeding cannot fail on DNS; and Scramble is scoped to `api_path => 'api/v1'`, so H14's web routes produce no OpenAPI drift and the H13a `operation-summary` trap does not apply. **Env note reconfirmed: run Pest/Vitest/build INSIDE the containers** (`…-app-1` for PHP, `…-node-1` for node) — the host has neither the pgsql driver nor the rolldown binding; `docker exec` is the working path (documented in the status memory).

**Next: H15a** — OAuth connector framework + Slack backend (`connections` table w/ encrypted tokens + RLS, callbacks on the central domain, a refresh MaintenanceJob, reusing `webhook_deliveries`); backend+api; depends H13a+H1g+H6a; gate native-connectors Starter+ + `manage:integrations`. **H1g (OAuth token custody ADR) is a prerequisite — write it first if not done.** Confirm scope against the H-map (row 238).

**2026-07-27 — H1g MERGED (PR #70 `83c923a`, 6/6 CI green on the first run) — ADR-0009: OAuth Connector Token Custody; docs-only, H-map row 217.** Planned with 3 parallel Explore surveys (webhook vertical + async/entitlement substrate; central-domain routing + docs constraints; ADR corpus conventions + token-custody prior art), then 4 forks confirmed with the user via AskUserQuestion — the two that shaped this ADR were **the number** (ADR-**0009**, the next free one: 0009 was earmarked for the cancelled payments ADR that was never written, so the sequence gets no permanent hole; the Status block says so, keeping `0008:8`/`:152`'s back-references coherent) and **no Socialite** (a hand-rolled `ConnectorProvider` adapter over the repo's guarded `Http::` idiom, since Socialite assumes a same-host session-backed redirect and the callback here is stateless and central-domain). **The finding that drove the whole design:** the app's session cookie is **host-only** — `config/session.php:159` reads `SESSION_DOMAIN`, and both `.env.example:59` and `.env:56` set it to `null` — so a tenant-subdomain session is *not* readable on the central host where an OAuth callback must land (providers register one fixed redirect URI and reject wildcard subdomains). **Two comments in the repo assert the opposite** (`routes/tenant.php:49`, `routes/admin.php:15`: "session established centrally; SESSION_DOMAIN spans subdomains") and are wrong against the config they describe — the standing "prose describes intent, config/code describe reality" lesson, third instance. The ADR therefore reuses the one proven mechanism for this exact shape (a signed, domain-separated HMAC token verified **pre-RLS** by middleware — the guest share/resume-token pattern) as the OAuth `state`, rather than widening `SESSION_DOMAIN` (which would make every tenant's session cookie cross-host on every request to solve a problem occurring twice per connection). Ten sub-decisions: §D1 tokens are `encrypted`-cast **`text`** (the `varchar(255)` trap: the cast inflates to ~290 chars), `$hidden`, and — unlike `webhook_endpoints.secret` — **never returned in any form, not even masked**, because a connector token has no user-facing consumer; §D2 one central-domain callback per provider, outside `web` (a third-party GET has no session/CSRF token; `state` is the CSRF control the spec assigns); §D3 `state` = domain-separated HMAC (`connector-oauth-state.v1`), ~10-min TTL, claims `{tid,uid,prov,nonce,exp}`, provider-bound, **stateless and therefore replayable within TTL — accepted**, bounded by single-use provider codes; §D4 context adopted from the verified state pre-RLS + `forget()` in `terminate()` (a central-domain INSERT under FORCE RLS otherwise writes **zero rows without erroring**); §D5 return host read from the `domains` table (the `GuestDraftController::resumeUrl` precedent) and the outcome handed back as a **query parameter**, not a flash, closing the open-redirect; §D6 refresh is a proactive `MaintenanceJob` fan-out, never lazy inside a delivery; §D7 disconnect destroys local tokens unconditionally, provider revoke best-effort; §D8 least-privilege scopes stored per connection; §D9 app credentials in `.env` only + the **`APP_PREVIOUS_KEYS` gap recorded** (`config/app.php:99-106` supports `previous_keys`; the variable is in no `.env.example` and no doc, so an `APP_KEY` rotation would silently brick every stored token); §D10 a new `connection` audit alias with both token columns in `AuditRedactor`'s unconditional list, registered **in the same increment that creates the table**. Gate inherited, not coined: ADR-0008 §D7 already ratified `native_connectors` as one Starter+ flag key, seeded at `PlanCatalog.php:34,54` and consumed by nothing yet. Doc reconciliation shipped in the same PR (the H1a/H1b invariant — announce it, then do it): `docs/security-threat-model.md` §2 gains two attack-surface rows (the platform-held credential; the unauthenticated inbound callback), §5 is retitled "Webhook & Connector Security" and gains a nine-row connector table (verdicts labelled **Decided (ADR-0009)** so ratified design is never read as shipped mitigation), §9 gains residual item 8; `docs/data-privacy-gdpr-compliance.md` §7 gains the **elective** connector sub-processor bullet (metadata-by-default, revisit if a per-subscription include-answers opt-in ever ships). No code, no migration, no `ci.yml` change.

**Next: H15a** — OAuth connector framework + Slack backend, now unblocked by ADR-0009: `connections` + `connection_subscriptions` (encrypted tokens, strict RLS), the central-domain callback, a refresh MaintenanceJob, and `webhook_deliveries` generalized into the shared outbound ledger (nullable owner FKs + an XOR CHECK). backend+api, no UI (H15b). Plan of record: `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\read-progress-md-h14-is-async-thompson.md`.

**2026-07-27 — H15a MERGED (PR #71 `02647db`, 6/6 CI green on the first run) — OAuth connector framework + Slack backend; H-map row 238.** Planned in the same session as H1g (3 parallel Explore surveys, 4 forks confirmed via AskUserQuestion); plan of record `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\read-progress-md-h14-is-async-thompson.md`. **The four decisions:** extend `webhook_deliveries` into a shared outbound ledger rather than mint a second table; ship a **fixed Block Kit message** and record H6a (piping) as a deferred dep rather than a blocker — today's envelopes carry ids/metadata, not answers, so there is nothing to pipe the layout doesn't already say; **hand-rolled adapter, no Socialite** (it assumes a same-host session-backed redirect, and the callback here is stateless + central-domain); ADR-0009. **The linchpin, found during exploration and load-bearing for the whole increment:** the session cookie is **host-only** (`config/session.php:159` ← `SESSION_DOMAIN`, `null` in `.env.example:59` AND `.env:56`), so the central-domain callback — mandatory, because OAuth apps register one fixed redirect URI and reject wildcard subdomains — has neither session nor tenant, and under FORCE RLS a context-less INSERT there **writes zero rows WITHOUT erroring**. Rather than widen `SESSION_DOMAIN` (every tenant's cookie becomes cross-host on every request, to fix something that happens twice per connection), identity rides the OAuth **`state`**: a signed, domain-separated (`connector-oauth-state.v1`), provider-bound HMAC token in the guest share/resume wire format, verified **pre-RLS** by `EstablishConnectorOauthContext`, which then applies tenant+user and `forget()`s in `terminate()`. Return host from the **`domains` table** (never echoed → closes the open-redirect), outcome as a **query param** because no flash can cross that cookie boundary. **Built:** `connections` + `connection_subscriptions` (credentials on the grant, routing on the rule) with `encrypted`-cast **`text`** tokens that **no API returns in any form — not even masked**, unlike a webhook secret (the platform is their only consumer); the alter-only ledger generalization (nullable owner FKs + **XOR CHECK** + **two PARTIAL uniques** — partial is mandatory, since a plain unique over a nullable column lets unlimited connector rows share an `event_id` under Postgres NULL semantics); a shared `RetryLadder` so both channels can't drift; `SlackConnector` + a pure `SlackMessageFormatter`; four NEW auto-discovered listeners → `ConnectorEventDispatcher` (separate from the webhook ones so the channels fail independently — **adding the channel touched no shipped file**); `DeliverConnectorMessageJob` on the EXISTING `webhooks` queue (a new `QueueName` case would force a `docker-compose.yml` + deployment-doc change `QueuedMailContractTest` asserts byte-for-byte); the `hourly()` refresh fan-out; and the `/api/v1/connections` surface. **RBAC:** `integrations.manage` = the 29th permission + a NEW `manage:integrations` ability — reusing `webhooks.manage` would retroactively hand every already-minted webhook token authority whose blast radius leaves this platform. **Three things the tests caught that reading alone would not have:** (a) `AuditRedactor` had no `connection` alias — an unregistered credential-bearing alias is silently un-redacted and **nothing detects it**, which is exactly why ADR-0009 §D10 says register it in the same increment; (b) an Eloquent relation named `connection()` collides with Model's protected `$connection` (the DB-connection name) — renamed to `grant()`, matching the ADR's own vocabulary; (c) `domains.domain` stores the bare subdomain LABEL, so the host builder expands it against the central domain (the invite/resume URL builders use the label directly — a defect in those, deliberately not copied). **Two test-harness lessons worth keeping:** a Sanctum token must be minted while tenant context is live (every HTTP request `forget()`s it in terminate(), so a later `createToken()` fails with insufficient-privilege, not 403), and the owner of a notification test must be an ACTIVE MEMBER or the on-demand notifiable's `users` lookup silently resolves to nothing under the job's RLS. **Gates (all in-container): Pest 1425 (+91)**, PHPStan L8 **0 on every touched file**, Pint/migration-lint/controller-gate/job-payload-lint, **openapi regenerated + `npx @redocly/cli@2 lint` clean** (every new controller method got a PHPDoc first line up front — the H13a `operation-summary` trap did not recur), migrations round-tripped up→down→up, vue-tsc + Vite build. Docs reconciled in the same PR: data-dictionary §15 + new §24/§25, api-specification §2.6, multi-tenancy-rbac-design (29 permissions), audit-compliance-logging-spec §1/§2, webhook-integration-design §4, plus a `.env.example` connector block that documents the `APP_PREVIOUS_KEYS` hazard.

**Next: H15b** — the Slack connector UI (H-map row 239): Integrations page, connect flow reading the `?connected=` / `?connect_error=` query param (no flash crosses the central↔tenant host boundary), channel picker over the granted `channels:read` scope, subscription create/edit, and the per-rule delivery log off the shared ledger. ui; depends H15a; gate `feature:native_connectors` + `integrations.manage`, hiding the surface entirely below Starter (the H14 precedent). One shared design system (rule #2), fully styled (rule #3).

**2026-07-27 — H15b BUILT on branch `h15b-slack-connector-ui` (all local gates green; PR not yet opened) — the Slack connector UI; H-map row 239.** Planned with 3 parallel Explore surveys + a 7-agent design/adversarial-verification workflow; 3 forks confirmed via AskUserQuestion (list + per-RULE detail; `MdsSelect` picker fed by a JSON sidecar rather than a new DS combobox; a synchronous test send IS in scope). Plan of record: `C:\Users\DOH\.claude\plans\read-progress-md-h1g-adr-0009-structured-garden.md`. **The finding that shaped the routing:** `ConnectionService::disconnect()` **soft-deletes the grant while keeping its rules** (paused), so nesting the rule routes under `{connection}` — the shape `/api/v1` uses — would 404 precisely when a tenant is cleaning up after a disconnect. Rules therefore bind FLAT, which forced a **new `ConnectionSubscriptionPolicy`** (H15a needed none) that repeats `integrations.manage` rather than resolving through `$rule->grant`, null in exactly that case; RLS still does the tenant scoping, so a cross-tenant id never binds at all. **The OAuth return is Post-Redirect-Get** — the index controller turns `?connected=`/`?connect_error=` into a session flash and 302s to a clean URL, so the toast rides the bridge the app already has and a refresh cannot re-raise it; the error code is **attacker-influenceable** (the callback forwards the provider's raw `?error=` verbatim) so it is used ONLY as a lookup key into a closed copy table, and an error **wins over** a success when a crafted link carries both. **Channel listing became an optional `ListsChannels` CAPABILITY** resolved through a config-driven `ConnectorRegistry::channelListerFor()` returning null when absent — not a fifth `ConnectorProvider` method, whose docblock pins it at "four responsibilities" and whose other implementors (H16's Sheets/Airtable) enumerate spreadsheets and bases, not channels. **No server cache anywhere:** `grep -rn "Cache::" app/` returns zero — the repo has never written to a cache at runtime, and a picker that fires only when a human opens a modal was the wrong place to introduce the first cache-key tenancy question; the modal holds the list for the page's lifetime with an explicit Refresh. **The sidecar always answers 200** with `error` in the body, because a non-2xx reaches `builderClient` as a thrown error whose message the caller discards — a Slack outage must degrade the picker to a manual channel id, never break the page. **The test send reuses the adapter's own `deliver()`**, so it inherits the SSRF guard, timeouts, no-redirect and Slack's `ok:false`-at-HTTP-200 classification and cannot drift from a real delivery; it writes no ledger row and burns no quota (both live in the job), and a credential rejection is reported but NOT acted on, so a blip during a voluntary test can't tear down a working grant. Its result shape is byte-identical to `WebhookTester`, so it reuses H14's `testResult` flash, `FlashTestResult` type and `TestResultModal` unchanged. **Two deliberate departures from the webhook detail page:** the log's fourth column is the **Result excerpt, not the HTTP status** (Slack fails at 200, so a status column would read "200" beside a red Failed badge), and there is no per-row redeliver (no connector redeliver endpoint exists; a permanently-disabled button is worse than none). **DS:** a `plug` glyph, two `statusVariant` entries labelled by what the tenant must DO ("Reconnect needed" / "Disconnected") + light/dark stories, **`MdsButton` gained `as="a"`/`href`** (mirroring `MdsCard`) because an action that navigates to another origin must be a link, and a **shared fix to `TestResultModal`** whose scrollable `<pre>` had no `tabindex` — an axe `scrollable-region-focusable` failure H14 escaped only because its seeded excerpt was short. A provider `configured` flag disables the connect button with an explanation when the deployment has no app credentials, which is also what makes the page render sanely in CI. **Two harness lessons, both bought the hard way:** `Http::preventStrayRequests()` in a `beforeEach` turns **every Inertia page render into a 500** (the SSR gateway POSTs to the render server while building the root view) — use it only in suites that never render a page; and running two Pest processes against `meridian_testing` concurrently makes `RefreshDatabase`'s `migrate:fresh` drop the schema under the other one, surfacing as bogus "relation does not exist" errors in `beforeEach`. **Gates (all in-container): Pest 1475** (+50 across 4 new suites; the single full-suite failure is `DatabaseWorkerPipelineTest`, which passes isolated — a load flake with no causal path here), PHPStan L8 **0 on every new/changed file**, Pint / controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint, vue-tsc both tsconfigs, Vitest 503/505 (the 2 fails are the known `token-references` timeout flake), Vite build, and **`openapi.json` verified drift-free** — no migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change. `E2eSeeder::seedConnections()` verified end-to-end against the test DB (2 workspaces incl. a `refresh_failed` one, 3 rules, a 4-status delivery spread); the Playwright specs never open the rule modal, the only control that would call Slack.

**Next: H1h** — the piping grammar + output-encoding/XSS contract design doc (H-map row 218; docs-only, the H1a/H1b/H1g shape). It is the ADR-wave entry that unblocks the whole H6a → H6b/H7/H17/H21c chain, and the seam H15b deferred connector message templating to. Independent small alternative: **H8** (`ocr_compatible` fix — add matrix/likert/repeat exclusion; backend, S, no deps).

**2026-07-27 — H15b MERGED (PR #72 `5e80872`, 6/6 CI green after one fix cycle) — the Slack connector UI; H-map row 239.** With it the **native-connector vertical is COMPLETE end-to-end** (ADR-0009 → backend → UI). The first CI run was **5/6 with `e2e` red**, and the failure is the one worth remembering: `scrollable-region-focusable` on **`.mds-table__scroll`** at tablet width, i.e. the SHARED `MdsDataTable`'s own horizontal scroller, not anything specific to the new pages — `/integrations` itself was clean at every viewport. The wrapper is `overflow-x: auto`, so any table wider than its container is a scroll region with no keyboard route to the off-screen columns (WCAG 2.1.1); the rule-detail table is simply the first with enough columns to reach it, and the webhook detail page fits at tablet so it never had. **Fixed in the component, not the page** (the precedent its own sticky-cell comment already sets), with focusability **measured rather than assumed** — a `ResizeObserver` on both the wrapper and the table re-measures on viewport and personalization type-scale changes, and the computed `overflow-x` is checked so the ≤480px card layout stays inert despite wider content — so a table that fits adds **no tab stop anywhere in the app**. `role="group"` deliberately, NOT `region`: Integrations draws one table per connected workspace, and same-named landmarks would have traded this violation for `landmark-unique`; the Integrations captions now name their workspace as well. Added `DataTable.test.ts` (the component had none): inert when it fits, a named focusable group when it scrolls, inert under the mobile card layout. **The generalized lesson, and it is the third instance of this shape:** H14 fixed `.page-header__actions`, H15b fixed `TestResultModal`'s `<pre>` pre-emptively and then had to fix `.mds-table__scroll` reactively — **any `overflow` or non-wrapping flex container in a SHARED component is a latent gate failure waiting for the first page whose content is big enough**, and the increment that trips it is rarely the one that introduced it. Audit the shared primitives a new page leans on, not just the page. Also worth keeping: the local `E2eSeeder` + Playwright specs deliberately never open the rule modal, which is the only control that would make a live Slack call, so the e2e job stays hermetic with no credentials configured.

**2026-07-30 — H8 MERGED (PR #73 `ab2de20`, 6/6 CI green on the FIRST run — no red cycle) — the `ocr_compatible` fix; H-map row 228.** Closes the oldest live correctness bug in the corpus: `ocr-pipeline-design.md` §2 specifies an **allowlist**, the code was a **denylist**, so `matrix`/`likert_matrix` published as OCR-compatible against the doc's own words, `is_repeatable` was never consulted at all (`compute()` read only `$version->fields()`), and every future field type inherited eligibility. Four forks locked with the user: **charter-exact membership** (§2's literal 12-item list is an under-enumeration — it would disqualify a form for one `email` field or one `page_break` — so the doc was AMENDED, and the net behaviour change is exactly matrix + likert_matrix + repeat groups + nothing-extractable); **ship the backfill now**; **snapshot-driven primitive**; **non-vacuity in**. **Three things worth carrying forward.** (1) **The forcing mechanism had to be a `default`-less `match`, not a composition** — every existing `FieldType` predicate (`isMedia`/`isGeo`/`isComposite`/`isAdvanced`/`hasOptions`/`configEditor`) has a `default =>` arm, so any rule built from them absorbs a 32nd type as eligible silently, which is the very defect being fixed. A closed classification in `App\Enums\OcrFieldEligibility` makes the omission PHPStan-L8-visible and merge-blocking. (2) **`PublishService` was computing the flag from a SECOND read of live rows at step 8, when step 3 had already frozen the answer** — feeding it the snapshot instead removed two queries AND made drift inexpressible, because the backfill and H18a now evaluate literally the same bytes. That is also what corrects §2's third gap bullet: an older version's eligibility IS recoverable, so the per-version column it implied was rejected. (3) **`select 1` does not prove a privileged connection is privileged.** Both precedent backfills warn about FORCE RLS reading zero rows silently and then only check that the connection OPENS — a valid-credential NOBYPASSRLS role would flip nothing and pass its own postcondition. `assertPrivileged()` asks `pg_roles` instead; asserting it from the MIGRATION rather than the body kept the body connection-agnostic, which is what let this increment write the real-rows effect test the precedents concluded was impossible (their bodies are inherently cross-tenant; this one just scans `forms`). **The monotonicity invariant is load-bearing:** H8's permitted set is disjoint from the pre-H8 denylist, so `new ⇒ old`, which is what licenses reading only stored `true`s (lossless) and writing a constant `false` — and it means a future WIDENING of the allowlist needs its own backfill. **Gates: Pest 1513 (+38, zero failures, not even the DatabaseWorkerPipelineTest flake), PHPStan L8 0 on touched files, Pint/3 linters, no openapi drift, migration round-trip clean; mutation-verified** (matrix-as-extractable reddens 3 tests, dropping the repeat clause 3, dropping non-vacuity 1, a whole-object overwrite instead of `jsonb_set` 3).

**2026-07-30 — H1h MERGED (PR #74 `e379cd1`, 6/6 CI green on the FIRST run — no red cycle), docs-only — the piping grammar + output-encoding/XSS contract; H-map rows 167 + 218.** New **`docs/piping-output-encoding-design.md` = Doc #26** (the `ocr-pipeline-design.md` house shape: 3-key front matter, `---`-separated numbered sections, "What's Already Decided" first, "Out of Scope / Deferred" last) + **13 doc reconciliations**. Four decisions locked with the user: **(1) sibling template grammar** — `TEMPLATE_VERSION = '1.0'` beside a frozen expression `GRAMMAR_VERSION = '2.0'`, because both golden runners assert `grammar_version` **per vector** and the string appears at **288 verified sites** across `tests/golden/**` (286 vectors + 2 manifests), so a bump would rewrite every one of them in files whose semantics did not change *and* falsify the corpus's own "grammar stays v2.0" marker; **(2) documented per-surface rule + one escaping test per surface**, NOT a `__toString()`-less value object — accepted as a convention rather than a compiler error, compensated by naming an owner + a required test per row, registering the obligation in `testing-strategy.md` §3, and recording the weakness as `security-threat-model.md` §9 **item 9** (connectors stay item 8, per H1g); **(3) the contract binds EVERY untrusted value** on a server-rendered surface, not only piped ones — an escaper cannot tell where a string came from; **(4) confirmation-screen piping pinned**, with the net-new `forms.confirmation_message`/`_translations` columns assigned to H6a, since the copy is three hardcoded constants in `public-runtime/App.vue:29-31` and no such column exists in any of 48 migrations. **Grammar:** `${key}` reused verbatim from `ExpressionLexer::lexReference()`; **`$${` is the only escape** and yields a literal `${` (a bare `$` stays literal everywhere else, unlike the expression lexer which throws on it — inside prose a lone `$` is a currency sign); total under leftmost-longest scanning with no escape-of-escape regress (`$$${` → `$` + `${`). **Piping-source allowlist = a `default`-less exhaustive `match` over all 31 `FieldType` cases** (the H8 `OcrFieldEligibility` device — every general predicate `isAdvanced()`/`hasOptions()`/`configEditor()`/`isMedia()` has a `default =>` arm, so a composed rule reproduces H8's defect one increment later), and it is **deliberately NOT a reuse of `OcrFieldEligibility`**: `calculated` and `hidden` move to **pipeable** (19 / 2 no-answer / 10 excluded). **The real R3 hazard is not the parser** — it is `SchemaValueFormatter::displayValue()`, which has **no TS twin**, so H6b builds one and its choice-label/`yes_no`/multi-select/decimal rendering must be vector-pinned; `Coercion::toStr()` is explicitly barred as the primitive (its non-integral float→string parity is documented as unpinned). **Scope rules:** backward-reference-only over `(section_sequence, sequence)`; same-repeat-instance; no cross-repeat and no repeat-to-flat; **a section key is never a template hole** (legal `count()` operand, illegal hole) — which is exactly why template validation **cannot** just call `assertReferencesResolve()` unchanged: its `$knownKeys` is a flat field ∪ section union carrying no scope information (`ExpressionValidationGate.php:47-61`). Unresolved at render = **empty string**, never the raw token; publish refuses an unresolvable *reference*, render tolerates an unanswered *value*. **Multi-locale:** each variant independently a template; **reference-set parity across locales NOT required**; **order is normative — resolve the locale, then render**. **The audit found three LIVE defects for non-piped strings**, all now named + assigned: Slack `mrkdwn` unescaped (raw form title → H6a), `Markdown::withSecuredEncoding()` off so `[`/`!`/`*` are live markdown in every notification (→ H3/H23), and no CSV/XLSX formula-injection prefixing (→ its own row). **One agent claim REFUTED before it was written down** (the H1a lesson, and the single most valuable verification of the session): Inertia's `{!! json_encode($page) !!}` was reported as a live `</script>` breakout sink — it is not, because `json_encode` escapes forward slashes by default. Proven empirically in-container: `json_encode` emits `<\/script>`. The doc records it as a **dependency to preserve** (a future `JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES` on that path opens it), not a defect. Also corrected in passing: **`technical-architecture.md` §4.3's vector count was drifted** ("55 validation" → the manifest says **106**), and **`PRD.md:13`'s ADR enumeration was stale** (stopped at 0007, omitting 0008/0009). **CSP is honestly recorded as no defence at all** — `PublicRuntimeSecurityHeaders` sets no `default-src`/`script-src` on 4 routes, so output encoding is the sole control; `script-src` hardening stays deferred with the `font-src`-absence tripwire named. **Reconciled:** `security-threat-model.md` (§2 +1 surface row, §7 retitled "Expression Engine Sandboxing **& Template Output Encoding**" with a 5-row template table using H1g's **Decided (Doc #26 §n)** verdict convention, §9 +item 9), `testing-strategy.md` (§1 +the template suite, §3 +the per-surface encoding bullet), `technical-architecture.md` (R3 + §4.3), `form-versioning-schema-migration.md` (§4 +the gate clause, §5's Non-breaking row now excludes hole-changing text edits), `data-dictionary.md` (`placeholder` documented at last, `form_fields.key`, +2 forward-spec `forms` rows, +a Design Note pinning the closed template-bearing column list and why `default_value` is excluded), `PRD.md`, `domain-glossary.md` (+**piping**/**template**/**hole**, arbitrating the `form_templates` collision), `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows — it had none), `xlsform-interop-spec.md` (`${}` in a `label` cell both directions + the **`sanitizeKeys()` doesn't rewrite `${…}` refs** gap now recorded as ⚠️), `non-functional-requirements.md` §6, `form-filling-ux-flow.md` §6, `webhook-integration-design.md` (the `SlackMessageFormatter` seam now has a design), `data-privacy-gdpr-compliance.md` §7 (the include-answers revisit trigger now has a named owner: H6a). **No code, no migration, no `ci.yml`, no `openapi.json` change.**

**2026-07-30 — H6a BUILT on branch `h6a-piping-backend` — the piping backend; H-map row 225 (backend, M, depends H1h only).** The code half of Doc #26, and the increment that gates H6b/H7/H17/H21c plus the connector-templating seam H15a/H15b both deferred here. **Net-new: `App\Services\Templates`** (`TemplateParser` with `TEMPLATE_VERSION = '1.0'`, `TemplateRenderer`, `TemplateScopeResolver`, `TemplateSources`), **`App\Exceptions\Templates\TemplateSyntaxException`** (own base — NOT `ExpressionException`, because `bootstrap/app.php` matches the two expression *leaf* types by name and a third leaf would match no callback and 500 without an envelope), **`App\Enums\PipingEligibility`** (a `default`-less `match` over all 31 `FieldType` cases — 19 pipeable / 2 no-answer / 10 excluded — the H8 device, deliberately NOT a reuse of `OcrFieldEligibility` since `calculated` and `hidden` diverge), **`TemplateValidationGate`** as the third gate in `PublishService` step 1 over all nine template-bearing columns × every locale variant, **`forms.confirmation_message`/`_translations`** (migration `2026_07_30_000002`, the PRD's confirmation-piping claim finally satisfiable), **`ValidTemplate`** (the repo's second custom `ValidationRule`), a **builder Confirmation modal** + `message-check` glyph, the **TS `template.ts` twin**, and **`tests/golden/templates/` — 53 vectors, the corpus's third**. **The Slack `mrkdwn` fix shipped**: escaping applied to each untrusted variable *before* interpolation (never the assembled string, whose `*` emphasis is deliberate), covering all THREE sinks; the test is net-new because the repo's only message-body assertion was a `str_contains` on trusted copy, so the defect was unpinned. **Five Doc #26 amendments (A1–A5), each recorded inline in the H8 "amend rather than implement the wrong thing" style.** A2 and A5 are the ones worth carrying forward: §3.3's `(section_sequence, sequence)` tuple is not implementable (that column is a nullable *order-within-section* value, null on nearly every row; the section's own order lives on `form_sections.sequence`), and §6's "runs before the freeze so nothing rejected reaches `schema_snapshot`" credits the ordering for what the **transaction** actually guarantees — proven by mutation, since moving the gate below the freeze leaves every gate test green. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1615 (+102), ZERO failures**; PHPStan L8 clean on every H6a-authored line (the 8 remaining are the documented local `property.notFound` baseline on untouched lines); Pint 761; migration round-trip up→down→up; vue-tsc both tsconfigs; **Vitest 547 passed / 13 skipped** (the skips are the PHP-only formatting vectors; the 2 fails are the known `token-references` full-suite timeout flake, verified passing isolated); Vite build; **`openapi.json` regenerated (+16 lines, the public form block only) and `redocly lint` clean**; both existing golden corpora byte-untouched with `grammar_version` still at exactly 288 sites. **Mutation-verified, not merely green:** `matrix`→pipeable reddens 3, capping a hole-free value reddens 2, escaping `&` last reddens 3.

**2026-07-31 — H6a MERGED (PR #75 `e04e68b`, 6/6 CI green on the FIRST run — no red cycle); H6b BUILT on branch `h6b-piping-runtime` — the piping runtime; H-map row 226 (classified `ui, M` there, shipped full-stack, M/L).** Doc #26 → **v1.2**, amendments **A7–A10**. **Four decisions locked with the user, three of which push PHP work into a "ui" increment. (A7) The scalar→string rule is PINNED in BOTH engines.** `displayValue()` ended in a bare `(string)` cast JavaScript **cannot** reproduce, and both divergent branches are reachable rather than theoretical: `ExpressionEvaluator::normalize()` passes a native bool through, so a `calculated` field holding `${age} >= 18` stores one (PHP `'1'`/`''` vs JS `'true'`/`'false'`), and any `calculated` division holds a non-integral float (PHP `'0.3'` at `precision=14` vs JS `'0.30000000000000004'`). **H6a's only float vector was `3.5` — exactly representable in binary, so it concealed the entire problem while appearing to pin it.** New rule: bool → `'1'`/`''`; non-finite → `''`; integral float → no fraction; else fixed notation to 10 decimals with trailing zeros trimmed. It avoids PHP's `precision` ini (runtime-configurable, so the old cast was not stable across deployments) and E-notation on both sides; **verified equal across 17 probed values by running both containers**, not by argument. One accepted divergence with a precise boundary — **|v| >= 1e21**, where JS `toFixed` falls back to exponential (hence the `< 1e21` guard, which documents where the mirror stops); one unfixable and never to be vectored — a `yes_no` of JSON `1.0` is `No` in PHP and `Yes` in JS. **(A8) `?string $locale` on `displayValue()`/`options()`/`TemplateRenderer::render()`** — §4's order bound the sentence but not the VALUE dropped into it, so a Filipino respondent read an English choice label mid-Filipino-sentence *and* the tenant's inbox disagreed with the respondent's own screen about the same answer. Null keeps every pre-H6b caller byte-identical, which is what made it safe to add to two shipped read surfaces. Two fallbacks at two levels, deliberately different: the locale variant falls back on missing/non-string/**blank** (`resolveText()`'s rule), the base label on **null only**. The exporter passes the EXPORT's locale, not the submission's — one spreadsheet, one language. **(A9) The inbox is repeat-aware, and closing it meant fixing a bug that was not about piping at all:** `SubmissionInboxPresenter` was repeat-**blind**, emitting one row per member field and reading `$answers[$field->key]` while a repeat member's answers live nested under the SECTION key — so **an answered roster displayed as a column of em-dashes**, piping or not. That also made "labels only" incoherent, since one row cannot carry N differently-filled labels. A repeatable section now emits one BLOCK PER INSTANCE against `array_merge($base, $instance)`, numbered as `RepeatGroup.vue` numbers it; an empty repeat shows its heading with **no rows**, because it was not answered blank — it was not answered. SPA-only was rejected: respondent and reviewer would read different question text for one submission. **(A10) A3's residue closes as a WARNING, not a 422** — the publish gate resolves against the version being PUBLISHED while a request can only resolve against the CURRENTLY PUBLISHED one, so a hard reject would refuse an author who adds the field to their draft *then* writes the message. The controller swaps its success toast for an `info` one naming the key and the reason; a form with no published version says nothing, because there is nothing to resolve against. **Engineering calls worth carrying: the SPA seam lives in the STORE, not the components** (`labelFor`/`hintFor`/`placeholderFor`/`sectionTitleFor`/`sectionDescriptionFor`) — composing per-component would have left a raw `${key}` in the aria-live announcer and the error-summary banner, invisible to sighted QA; **the render document is `{ ...effectiveAnswers, ...computed }`** mirroring `SubmissionPipeline.php:147`, because `SemanticResult.computed` is a SEPARATE map and reading `effectiveAnswers` alone — the natural, documented choice — renders every `calculated` hole blank in the SPA while the inbox shows it correctly, killing the case §3.1 cites as the *reason* `calculated` is pipeable; **a hole-FREE label takes no dependency on the answer document at all**, so every pre-H6b form keeps its exact reactive graph (pinned by a getter-evaluation-count test); **the confirmation renders inside `RuntimeSession`** because `App.vue` renders it and the session as mutually exclusive branches, so the store does not outlive the emit — it replaces CONFIRM and RESOLVED but **not QUEUED**, which reports delivery state rather than gratitude; **`placeholder` was passed through RAW**, the one live §3.4 violation the audit found in the runtime; and **dispatch is on the raw `field_type` string**, since minting a 31-case TS union to mirror the PHP enum would create a parallel catalog needing forever-sync — a *new* drift surface in the increment whose purpose is closing one. **Corpus 53 → 76 and `engines` is gone from the tree.** The TS runner had **no render branch at all** — its `expected` was cast to a segment array, so deleting the key alone would have failed all 13 on a shape mismatch rather than running them — and it was missing the fourth guard, which is itself runner drift since the runners *are* the enforcement mechanism. **New standing lesson — a vector reachable by two paths pins neither:** `fmt_hole_named_constructor_renders_empty` passed against a prototype-UNSAFE mirror because with no answer for that key the empty guard short-circuited before the unsafe lookup mattered; found by mutation, fixed by giving it an answer. **The G11 token guard earned its keep a second time:** `--mds-color-text-muted` does not exist and is literally one of the 16 names G11 removed — grepping the source found REFERENCES, not definitions; the registry is `packages/design-system/tokens/*.json`. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1667 (+52), ZERO failures** (6096 assertions); PHPStan L8 **zero new** (the 3 remaining are the `User::$name` baseline, verified identical in count on `main`; test files are excluded by `phpstan.neon` by design); Pint 763; controller-gate / migration-lint / job-payload-lint; vue-tsc both tsconfigs; **Vitest 605 passed** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite **timeout** flake — `Test timed out in 5000ms` — which passes 2/2 isolated); Vite build; **`openapi.json` verified drift-free**; both existing golden corpora byte-untouched with `grammar_version` still at exactly 288 sites. **Mutation-verified on both sides — 8 mutations, each reddening only its intended tests.** Two honest findings recorded rather than papered over: the repeat merge DIRECTION is unobservable (field keys are unique per version, so a flat key and an instance key can never collide — reversing the arguments is a green mutation; what IS pinned is that the instance map is consulted at all), and the `1e-5` float mutation survives because `String(1e-5)` already agrees. **No migration, no job, no `/api/v1` change, no DS component change.**

**2026-07-31 — H6b MERGED (PR #76 `fb2c960`, 6/6 CI green on the FIRST run — no red cycle); H7 BUILT on branch `h7-hidden-prefilled-fields` — hidden / prefilled fields; H-map row 227 (full-stack, M, depends H6a only, ungated).** The PRD's "hidden fields" scope item, and the increment that closes the oldest open trust gap in the write path: a `hidden` answer was ordinary client input — coerced to a string and stored with no cap, no provenance, and nothing preventing any caller from choosing it. **Three decisions locked with the user. (1) TWO sources, not three — the H-map's "calculated source" is AMENDED, not implemented.** `FieldType::Calculated` already *is* the expression source (mirrored in both engines, write-back persisted, already pipeable); both compute passes gate on `field_type === 'calculated'`, and widening them to `hidden` would duplicate a working path while opening a **new R3 mirror surface for zero new capability** — the H8 "amend rather than implement the wrong thing" device, applied to a plan row rather than to a doc section. **(2) URL prefill is `hidden`-only** (prefilling a visible field is a different feature with its own UX questions). **(3) The encode page renders an editable input for a `url`-sourced hidden field and read-only for a `fixed` one** — that channel has no URL, so a keyer transcribing paper is the only possible source of an external value. **The contract:** `App\Enums\PrefillSource` classifies a hidden field's *config* into `Fixed | Url | None` — explicitly **not** a `default`-less match over the 31 `FieldType` cases, because the type dimension here is the single question "is it `hidden`?" and a 32nd type is correctly `None`; claiming H8's forcing device where it does not apply would be cargo-culting it. A **`fixed`** field is server-authoritative: its value is written from the authored `default_value` and the submitted value is **never inspected**, uniformly across all six ingest channels (a "trusted channel" carve-out was rejected — it would make one form yield different data per channel and leave H20's amount rule true on `guest` and false on `api_import`). A **`url`** field is the only client-writable one, scalar-only under `MAX_VALUE_BYTES = 2000`. Sourcing is **opt-in and fail-closed**: a field key is never an implicit query parameter, which is the H8 allowlist lesson applied to a new surface. **Placement is load-bearing:** the prefill pass lives INSIDE `StructuralAnswerNormalizer::normalize()`, because both write paths (`SubmissionPipeline::submit()` and `SubmissionDraftService::saveDraft()`) call it and then immediately hash with `AnswersContentChecksum` — the one placement that covers drafts *and* keeps the checksum deterministic, so a replay omitting the injected value and one including it normalise identically and cannot raise a spurious G8c 409. It needs a **second pass over the FIELDS**, since the main loop walks the client's keys and can only visit what it was sent. **The governing rule — a hidden field never produces a respondent-facing error — is enforced twice, and both halves earn their keep:** the two-engine Stage-3 narrowing (`hidden` joins `calculated` in the early return) is what makes dropping hidden fields from the SPA's step model *safe* rather than merely tidy, since an error would otherwise put a summary-banner jump link on a row that does not render; and the four publish refusals (`hidden_field_required`, `hidden_field_has_validations`, hidden-in-a-repeatable-section, `prefill_param_invalid`) are how the author finds out — but that gate is **not retroactive**, which is precisely why the narrowing ships alongside it rather than instead of it. **Three live defects closed in passing, all one line:** `hidden`, `calculated` and `page_break` each rendered the encode channel's *"Not available for manual entry yet (Phase 2)"* notice **on a respondent's public form**, because none is in `schema-mapping.ts`'s `SUPPORTED` and `controlFor()` falls through to `'unsupported'`. Nothing pinned it. The new `RENDERS_NOTHING` set deliberately does **not** mirror `EncodeFormPresenter::OMITTED` — H7 makes the two channels asymmetric on purpose (a respondent must never see a hidden field; a keyer must). **Seeding order is the whole SPA rule:** prefill first, `restoreAnswers(initialAnswers)` second, so a restored draft always wins — a resume link carries no query string and the value it saved is the one the session was built against; both go through `restoreAnswers`, so the prefill inherits its flat-key guard free, and the store stays DOM-free with `App.vue` reading `location.search` once. **One deliberate client/server asymmetry, commented at both sites:** an over-cap URL parameter is **dropped** client-side while an over-cap submitted value is **422'd** server-side — they cannot desync, because the SPA never puts one into the answer map, so the fault is reachable only from a direct API post, where a slug is the useful answer and swallowing 2KB silently is not. **No migration** (`config` is `jsonb`, `default_value` already existed and already travelled in the snapshot — it simply had no reader until now), no `/api/v1` change. **Gates, all in-container: Pest 1703 (+36), ZERO failures** (6207 assertions); PHPStan L8 **zero in any H7-touched file** (55 in the local full analyse, all untouched, the documented local baseline; CI is clean); Pint 765; controller-gate / job-payload-lint / migration-lint; vue-tsc both tsconfigs; **Vitest 635 passed** (the 2 failures are the known `token-references` full-suite timeout flake, verified 2/2 isolated — which is also what confirms every new `var(--mds-…)` resolves); Vite build; `openapi.json` drift-free. **Mutation-verified — 6 mutations, and two of them changed the work.** Neutering `coerceHidden`'s source guard reddens 3 tests while removing the injection pass reddens a *different* 3, which is what proves the two mechanisms are **independently pinned rather than redundantly covering each other** — a single test would have passed under either mutation alone and told us nothing. And the checksum-stability test was caught **passing vacuously** under the injection mutation (with no injection, neither document carried the key, so they trivially matched); it now asserts presence as well as equality. **New standing lesson: when two mechanisms both make a property true, mutate them separately — a test that survives either one alone is pinning neither.** Second lesson: `form_field_validations` carries the draft-child RLS shape, so an insert without `form_version_id` violates the row-level policy rather than merely looking untidy. **CORPUS COUNT MOVED, deliberately** — the validation corpus is **106 → 114** and `grammar_version` **288 → 296** sites; the expressions and templates corpora are byte-untouched. H7 is the first increment to extend an existing corpus, so the invariant reads as written: byte-untouched unless an increment deliberately extends one and says so, and a changed count with no such note is drift. **Docs: no new number taken** (the H-map gives H7 no docs row — H6a's "no Doc #27" precedent), with reconciliations to `security-threat-model.md` §4 (+2 rows, Mitigated (H7)), `data-dictionary.md` §5, `form-versioning-schema-migration.md` §4, `testing-strategy.md` §3 (H7 added a second test to the DOM row rather than a new row — a new *entry point*, not a new surface: a prefilled value is untrusted because whoever handed out the link chose it, and it reaches the DOM on the first paint), `piping-output-encoding-design.md` (§3.1 + §8 both as-built), `ocr-pipeline-design.md` §2, `xlsform-interop-spec.md` (+⚠️ — the prefill source has no XLSForm expression at all, so an export silently loses it), `competitive-feature-parity-matrix.md` (+2 rows; it had none), `domain-glossary.md` (+**Prefill**, kept deliberately distinct from "default" — a default is a starting value a respondent may overwrite, a `fixed` prefill is one no client can influence), `technical-architecture.md` §4.3 + R3.

**2026-07-31 (same session, addendum) — H7 opened as PR #77 and went GREEN 6/6 after one red cycle, which turned out not to be H7's.** `builder-axe` failed on axe `scrollable-region-focusable` over `.palette` at the mobile viewport (`Builder — empty canvas (dark)`, failing both attempts). A re-run reproduced it, so it was not a flake — and checking `main` settled it: **the identical two tests failed at `e04e68b`** (the H6a merge) with the same rule, so the defect predates H7 entirely and had simply been passing intermittently. **The defect is real:** `FieldPalette` is `overflow-y: auto` over 31 add-buttons and satisfies the rule *through* those buttons, but `Builder.vue` binds `:disabled="saving"`, so during a debounced save every one of them is non-focusable and the scroll region has no keyboard access at all — the field types below the fold are unreachable without a pointer (WCAG 2.1.1). Fixed with `DataTable.vue`'s **measured** pattern rather than an unconditional `tabindex`: a palette that fits adds no tab stop (focusability is the remedy for unreachable content, not a decoration), `role="group"` deliberately not `region` (a landmark there would compete with the builder page's own), re-measured on resize and on a palette change, SSR-guarded. Pinned by three tests including the all-buttons-disabled case that actually fired, and mutation-verified (neutering `measure()` reddens exactly the two scroll cases). **New standing lesson: a "flaky" a11y gate can be catching a real race — before re-running a red one, check whether the same test has failed on `main` recently.** "Intermittent" and "wrong" are not mutually exclusive, and this one had been intermittently telling the truth for at least two merges.
